ChemistrySiumlator 核心项目资料（可上传的纯文本版）

用途：上传至不支持 ZIP 的平台，供阅读、审查代码、继续开发。
源码快照：7237221264aec7bf613aed17fe275a238718de5d
与 ChemistrySiumlator-core-2026-10-24.zip 中的内容逐字节一致。
包含：核心源码、测试、依赖锁文件、项目文档、题库元数据及资源生成工具。
不包含：原始真题 PDF、题目图片、大型研究压缩包、依赖缓存、构建产物。
因此，本文件不等于完整离线题库；资源恢复方法见 START-HERE-核心包说明.md。
覆盖范围及尚未实现的内容请以 docs/paper-coverage-2023-2026.md 为准，
不能据此声称已完成近四年全部 AS/IG 第二题覆盖。

阅读方法：先读 START-HERE-核心包说明.md、README.zh-CN.md 和 docs/usability-review-2026-10-24.md，
再按需要阅读源码。不同平台的上下文限制各异；能上传文件不代表会一次读完全部内容。
还原方法：每段 BEGIN FILE 后为 JSON 元数据，其中 bytes 为紧随换行后的 UTF-8 正文字节数；
按 bytes 读取正文，并保存至 path 对应的相对路径。SHA-256 可用于核对还原结果。
正文后额外添加的换行与 END FILE 标记不属于原文件。

# Project workflow

The user explicitly requested that completed changes be committed and pushed to GitHub by default. After finishing the requested work and appropriate checks, commit the task's changes and push to the task's remote branch without asking for routine confirmation. Report the resulting commit and branch, or the specific reason a push failed.

The selected remote branch for this project is `claude/trusting-franklin-x92q1i` on `origin`. A cloud checkout may use a local branch named `work`; when it has no upstream, push explicitly to the selected remote branch. Preserve remote updates and do not force-push.

See `README.md` for dependency and original-paper asset preparation. Generated files under `public/past-papers/`, `dist/`, and dependency directories are intentionally ignored; commit their reproducible source definitions and generation scripts.

# Qualitative Analysis Lab

**English** · [简体中文](README.zh-CN.md)

A browser-based simulator for the qualitative-analysis question of Cambridge practical exams — IGCSE Chemistry 0620 Paper 5 (Question 2) and AS & A Level Chemistry 9701 Paper 3. Students carry out the tests on a simulated bench, write down their own observations and conclusions on the question paper, then mark themselves against the published mark scheme.

Unlike an "Alternative to Practical" worksheet, nothing is scripted: every tube holds its actual contents, and what you see comes from a chemistry engine that reacts to exactly what you did — including mistakes.

## Features

- **Original papers, 2023–2026** — 67 available qualitative question sets (22 IGCSE, 45 AS), with original questions, mark schemes and materials. Nine cases have guided bench procedures, 18 more have configured free benches, and 40 offer source-based written practice. See the [coverage ledger](docs/paper-coverage-2023-2026.md) for exact limits. These are not all AS Question 2 papers.
- **A hands-on bench** — everything is real apparatus operated with your hands, not buttons: pick up a bottle and hold it over a tube to pour, park a dropper in a tube and squeeze it drop by drop, turn on the gas and strike a match to light the Bunsen burner, put a tube in the flame to heat it, dampen litmus paper at the wash bottle and hold it at the mouth of the tube, light a splint, collect gas with a teat pipette and bubble it through limewater, filter through a funnel. Samples stand in the rack with the other tubes. Drag, or tap to lift and tap the target; a ghost-hand demonstration shows the moves.
- **Real time** — reactions, settling, cooling and slow changes happen while you watch; the clock on the wall can run time ten times faster.
- **A chemistry engine, not a lookup table** — stoichiometry, solubility products, "soluble in excess", redox, gas evolution, heating and slow changes over time (e.g. Fe(OH)₂ turning brown at the surface).
- **Mistakes have consequences** — acidifying with HCl before AgNO₃, forgetting to acidify before Ba²⁺, re-using an un-rinsed tube, tap water, pouring in too much reagent at once, over-filling a tube…
- **Free-form answers and self-marking** — no auto-marking; the results page shows your answers next to the mark scheme and the "supervisor's results" produced by the same engine.
- **Works on phone, tablet and desktop** — responsive layouts for PC, iPad (portrait and landscape) and phones.
- **Answers stay on the device** — work in progress, notes and self-marks are kept in the browser's local storage.

## Quick start

Requirements: **Node.js 18+**, npm, **Python 3.11+**, and Git LFS. Validated with Node 24.19.0. The original-paper library is generated from the checksum-verified LFS archive.

```bash
git clone <this repository>
cd ChemistrySiumlator
npm ci
git lfs install --local
git lfs pull --include=dropbox_downloads/Chemistry_Qualitative_Practicals_2001-2026.zip
python3 -m venv ../.chemistry-paper-tools
. ../.chemistry-paper-tools/bin/activate
python -m pip install -r tools/requirements.txt
npm run prepare:papers
npm run dev
```

Open the address Vite prints (usually <http://localhost:5173>). To try it on a phone or iPad on the same Wi-Fi network:

```bash
npm run dev -- --host
```

and open the `Network:` address shown in the terminal on the device.

| Command | What it does |
|---|---|
| `npm run dev` | Development server with hot reload |
| `npm test` | Engine unit tests and past-paper regression tests (Vitest) |
| `npm run typecheck` | TypeScript type check |
| `npm run prepare:papers` | Generate original PDFs and readable page images from the verified archive |
| `npm run check:papers` | Verify all 201 original PDFs and generated page availability |
| `npm run test:browser` | Chromium desktop and touch workflow tests (requires Chromium; set `CHROMIUM_PATH` if needed) |
| `npm run build` | Production build into `dist/` (static files) |
| `npm run preview` | Serve the production build locally |

## Included past papers

| Paper | Series | Question | Marks | Unknowns |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0620/51 | May/June 2024 | Q2 | 14 | One solid, E, containing three ions |
| 0620/52 | Oct/Nov 2024 | Q2 | 15 | Two solids, M and N |
| 0620/51 | May/June 2023 | Q2 | 18 | One solid, E, containing three ions |
| 0620/52 | May/June 2024 | Q2 | 18 | Solids H and I |
| 9701/31 | May/June 2025 | Q3 | 12 | FA 7 (solution), FA 8 and FA 9 (sodium compounds) |
| 9701/34 | Oct/Nov 2024 | Q3 | 12 | FB 6 (heated, residue tested) and FB 7 (salt solution) |
| 9701/33 | May/June 2024 | Q3(a) | 10 | FA 5, a mixture of two cations and two anions (organic part (b) not simulated) |

The table above lists the original seven guided cases. Guided AS Q2 cases `9701_w23_31` and `9701_w23_35` are also available. Guided procedures are regression-tested in `src/engine/__tests__/papers.test.ts`; the other 18 configured benches have sample-validation and representative reaction checks, not a claim that every mark-scheme point is simulated. The [complete ledger](docs/paper-coverage-2023-2026.md) lists all 67 source sets.

## Project structure

```
src/
  engine/        Chemistry engine (no UI code; tested on its own)
  papers/        Past-paper cases: wording, samples, bench reagents, mark schemes, supervisor scripts
  ui/            React interface: bench, glassware, question paper, notebook, self-marking
tools/
  dropbox_download.py   Downloads the research material from Dropbox (with hash check)
dropbox_downloads/      Research material: past-paper archive (Git LFS) and research report
docs/                   User and developer guides (English and Simplified Chinese)
```

Tech stack: Vite 5, React 18, TypeScript 5 (strict), Vitest 2. No backend.

## Research material

`dropbox_downloads/` holds the source archive in Git LFS and the research report. The archive is required to generate `public/past-papers/` for a fresh checkout; generated assets are ignored by Git and checked before production builds. Once built, `dist/` includes the source library and needs no Python or archive at runtime.

## Limitations

- Observations are semi-quantitative and tuned to the level of detail Cambridge mark schemes expect; they are not a full physical-chemistry simulation.
- Only the qualitative-analysis question is simulated (no titration, rates or organic tests).
- Source coverage is the 67 retrieved public sets, not every practical exam or every AS Q2. October/November 2026 is absent from the archive. Original-paper written practice remains available for source-only cases.
- No auto-marking, accounts or teacher dashboard yet.

## Copyright

Question papers, mark schemes and confidential instructions © Cambridge University Press & Assessment, reproduced for private study only. Simulated observations may differ in detail from a real laboratory.

# Qualitative Analysis Lab（化学定性分析实验模拟）

[English](README.md) · **简体中文**

在浏览器中模拟 Cambridge 实验考试的定性分析题——IGCSE Chemistry 0620 Paper 5（第 2 题）及 AS & A Level Chemistry 9701 Paper 3。学生在虚拟实验台上亲手做实验，在试卷上自行写下观察和结论，最后对照官方评分标准自我评分。

它不是 "Alternative to Practical"（ATP）文字题：现象没有预先写死。每支试管保存真实内容，所看到的现象由化学反应引擎根据你的实际操作（包括错误操作）计算得出。

## 功能

- **2023–2026 原题资料**——收录资料包中可取得的 67 组定性题（IG 22 组、AS 45 组），含原题、评分标准和材料说明。9 组有逐步回归验证的实验流程，18 组配好自由实验台，40 组提供原题书面练习。详见[覆盖清单](docs/paper-coverage-2023-2026.md)；不等于所有 AS 第二题都已完成模拟。
- **动手操作的实验台**——一切都是用手操作的真实仪器，而不是按钮：拿起试剂瓶放到试管上方倾倒，把滴管放进试管逐滴挤压，开燃气、划火柴点燃本生灯，把试管放到火焰上加热，在洗瓶嘴润湿石蕊试纸再放到试管口，点燃木条，用胶头滴管收集气体通入石灰水，用漏斗过滤。样品和其他试管一起放在试管架上。可以拖动，也可以点一下拿起、再点一下目标；"虚拟手"示范会演示各种动作。
- **实时进行**——反应、沉降、冷却和缓慢变化都在你眼前发生；墙上的时钟可让时间以十倍速度流逝。
- **真正的反应引擎，而非查表**——化学计量、溶度积、"过量溶解"、氧化还原、气体逸出、加热，以及随时间发生的慢变化（例如 Fe(OH)₂ 表面逐渐变棕）。
- **错误操作有真实后果**——用 HCl 酸化后加 AgNO₃、加 Ba²⁺ 前忘记酸化、重用未清洗的试管、用自来水、一次倒入过量试剂、试管装得太满等。
- **自由作答、自我评分**——不自动改分；结果页把你的答案、评分标准和同一引擎生成的"监考员结果"（Supervisor's results）并排显示。
- **支持手机、平板和电脑**——针对电脑、iPad（横向及竖向）和手机设计的响应式布局。
- **答案保存在本机**——作答进度、笔记及自评分数保存在浏览器本地存储（localStorage）。

## 快速开始

需要 **Node.js 18+、npm、Python 3.11+ 和 Git LFS**。已验证 Node 24.19.0。原题页面从校验过的 LFS 资料包生成。

```bash
git clone <本仓库地址>
cd ChemistrySiumlator
npm ci
git lfs install --local
git lfs pull --include=dropbox_downloads/Chemistry_Qualitative_Practicals_2001-2026.zip
python3 -m venv ../.chemistry-paper-tools
. ../.chemistry-paper-tools/bin/activate
python -m pip install -r tools/requirements.txt
npm run prepare:papers
npm run dev
```

打开 Vite 显示的地址（通常是 <http://localhost:5173>）。如需在同一 Wi-Fi 下的手机或 iPad 上试用：

```bash
npm run dev -- --host
```

然后在设备上打开终端显示的 `Network:` 地址。

| 命令 | 作用 |
|---|---|
| `npm run dev` | 启动开发服务器（热更新） |
| `npm test` | 运行引擎单元测试及真题回归测试（Vitest） |
| `npm run typecheck` | TypeScript 类型检查 |
| `npm run prepare:papers` | 从校验过的资料包生成原题 PDF 和可缩放页面 |
| `npm run check:papers` | 校验 201 份原始 PDF 和页面是否齐全 |
| `npm run test:browser` | 桌面及触屏操作检查；需 Chromium，可用 `CHROMIUM_PATH` 指定位置 |
| `npm run build` | 生产构建，输出到 `dist/`（静态文件） |
| `npm run preview` | 在本机预览生产构建 |

## 已收录真题

| 试卷 | 考试季 | 题目 | 分数 | 未知物 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0620/51 | 2024 年 5/6 月 | Q2 | 14 | 一种固体 E，含三种离子 |
| 0620/52 | 2024 年 10/11 月 | Q2 | 15 | 两种固体 M 和 N |
| 0620/51 | 2023 年 5/6 月 | Q2 | 18 | 一种固体 E，含三种离子 |
| 0620/52 | 2024 年 5/6 月 | Q2 | 18 | 固体 H 和 I |
| 9701/31 | 2025 年 5/6 月 | Q3 | 12 | FA 7（溶液）、FA 8 和 FA 9（钠化合物） |
| 9701/34 | 2024 年 10/11 月 | Q3 | 12 | FB 6（加热后检验残留物）和 FB 7（盐溶液） |
| 9701/33 | 2024 年 5/6 月 | Q3(a) | 10 | FA 5，含两种阳离子和两种阴离子的混合物（有机部分 (b) 未模拟） |

上表为原先的 7 组引导实验；另外新增了 `9701_w23_31` 和 `9701_w23_35` 两组原卷 Q2 的引导流程和回归检查。另 18 组自由实验台仅做了样品和代表性反应验证，不声称已覆盖每个评分点。全部 67 组原题及状态见[覆盖清单](docs/paper-coverage-2023-2026.md)。

## 项目结构

```
src/
  engine/        化学反应引擎（不含界面代码，可独立测试）
  papers/        真题案例：题目文字、样品、实验台试剂、评分标准、监考员操作脚本
  ui/            React 界面：实验台、玻璃仪器、试卷、实验笔记、自我评分
tools/
  dropbox_download.py   从 Dropbox 下载研究资料（含哈希校验）
dropbox_downloads/      研究资料：真题压缩包（Git LFS）及研究报告
docs/                   用户指南及开发者指南（英文及简体中文）
```

技术栈：Vite 5、React 18、TypeScript 5（strict）、Vitest 2。无后端。

## 研究资料

`dropbox_downloads/` 存放 Git LFS 原题资料包和研究报告。新克隆后必须拉取资料包并生成 `public/past-papers/`；生成文件被 Git 忽略，生产构建前会检查齐全性。构建后的 `dist/` 已包含原题资料，运行时无需 Python 或压缩包。

## 局限

- 观察结果是半定量的，细节程度以符合 Cambridge 评分标准为准，并非完整的物理化学模拟。
- 只模拟定性分析题（不含滴定、反应速率或有机测试）。
- 67 组是资料包已取得的公开定性题，不是历年所有实验卷或所有 AS Q2。资料包不含 2026 年 10/11 月；未模拟的题目仍可读原卷、作答并自评。
- 暂无自动改分、账户或教师后台。

## 版权

试卷、评分标准及机密说明 © Cambridge University Press & Assessment，仅供私人学习使用。模拟的实验现象在细节上可能与真实实验室有所不同。

# Developer guide

**English** · [简体中文](developer-guide.zh-CN.md) · [Back to README](../README.md)

How Qualitative Analysis Lab is built, how the chemistry engine works, and how to add past papers, reagents, compounds and reactions.

## Contents

1. [Setup and commands](#1-setup-and-commands)
2. [Architecture](#2-architecture)

5. [Adding a past paper](#5-adding-a-past-paper)
6. [Adding reagents, compounds, species and reactions](#6-adding-reagents-compounds-species-and-reactions)

## 1. Setup and commands

Node.js 18+ (developed on Node 22) and npm.

For a fresh checkout, first follow the [README setup](../README.md#quick-start): the original-paper library needs Git LFS, Python 3.11+, the pinned `tools/requirements.txt`, and `npm run prepare:papers`. `npm run build` now checks the generated sources before compiling. `npm run test:browser` runs the Chromium/touch workflows. See the [coverage ledger](paper-coverage-2023-2026.md) for the distinction between source availability, configured benches and guided regression coverage.

```bash
npm install
npm run dev          # Vite dev server, http://localhost:5173 (add -- --host for LAN devices)
npm test             # Vitest, all engine and past-paper tests
npx vitest           # watch mode
npm run typecheck    # tsc, strict mode
npm run build        # tsc -b && vite build → dist/
npm run preview      # serve dist/
```

Stack: Vite 5, React 18, TypeScript 5 (`strict`), Vitest 2. There is no backend and no runtime dependency besides React.

## 2. Architecture

```
src/
  main.tsx                 React entry
  engine/                  chemistry engine — pure TypeScript, no DOM/React
    species.ts             species catalogue: ions, complexes, precipitates, gases; colours, flame colours
    compounds.ts           starting materials (formula, molar mass, how they dissolve, thermal steps)
    reagents.ts            bench reagents (concentrations as in the confidential instructions)
    rules.ts               reaction rules (complete / equilibrium / kinetic)
    vessel.ts              a tube's state; rule solver; time, temperature, gases
    colour.ts              Beer–Lambert colour mixing and exam colour words
    observe.ts             turns state changes into exam-style observation sentences
    lab.ts                 the Lab: vessels + actions (add, pour, heat, gas test, …) + journal
    appearance.ts          what the UI should draw for a vessel
    index.ts               public exports
    __tests__/             unit tests and past-paper regression tests
  papers/
    types.ts               PaperCase / QuestionPart / AnswerSpec
    igcse.ts               0620 Paper 5 cases
    alevel.ts              9701 Paper 3 cases (+ paper-specific reagents)
    index.ts               PAPERS list, paperById()
  ui/
    App.tsx                screens: Home → Exam → Results
    Exam.tsx               exam screen: bench, question paper, notebook, mobile tabs, real-time loop
    Tube.tsx               SVG glassware (liquid, precipitate, bubbles, stopper); also embeds in the bench scene
    bench/
      layout.ts            scene size and where every piece of apparatus sits; tube geometry
      art.tsx              SVG drawings of the apparatus (bottles, dropper, burner, splints, papers, …)
      model.ts             BenchModel: what is held, where it is, what a gesture means; drives the Lab
      Bench.tsx            renders the scene and forwards pointer input to the model
      Demo.tsx             first-run ghost-hand demonstration
      sound.ts             synthesised sound effects (WebAudio)
      __tests__/           gesture tests on the model
    PaperPane.tsx          question paper with answer spaces
    Results.tsx            self-marking page
    storage.ts             guarded localStorage helpers
    styles.css             all styles, responsive breakpoints
```

The dependency direction is strictly `ui → papers → engine`. The engine never imports UI code and can be tested and scripted on its own.

## 3. The chemistry engine

The engine is **semi-quantitative**: it is accurate enough that the observations match what Cambridge mark schemes credit, and that mistakes change the result, but it is not a research-grade physical-chemistry model.

### 3.1 State

Each `Vessel` (`vessel.ts`) stores:

- `n` — amounts of every species in **mmol** (ions, dissolved molecules, solids, undissolved compounds);
- `V` — liquid volume in cm³ (a tube with less than a small threshold is "dry");
- `T` — temperature in °C, heating mode, stopper;
- the head-space gases above the liquid, bubble rate, condensation, how settled a precipitate is;
- a log of events.

Nothing knows the "answer": a reaction happens only if the reactants are in the tube.

Species ids are plain strings such as `'Cu2+'`, `'SO4 2-'`, `'OH-'`, `'Cu(OH)2(s)'`, `'CO2(aq)'`, `'NH3'`, `'Cl2(g)'`. Undissolved starting materials are `cmp:<compound id>` species. Water is never written explicitly.

### 3.2 Rules

Rules live in `rules.ts`:

```ts
interface Rule {
  id: string
  type: 'complete' | 'equilibrium' | 'kinetic'
  reactants: Record<string, number>   // stoichiometry
  products: Record<string, number>
  priority?: number                   // lower runs first (fast rules)
  K?: number                          // equilibrium constant, mol dm⁻³ units, solids/water omitted
  reserve?: Record<string, number>    // minimum free concentration that must remain
  halfLife?: number | ((ctx) => number) // kinetic rules, seconds (Infinity = not running)
  when?: (ctx) => boolean             // extra condition (T, pH, concentrations…)
  dry?: boolean; anyPhase?: boolean; minTemp?: number
  events?: string[]                   // observable events, e.g. 'effervescence'
}
```

- **complete** — fast reactions that run to completion (neutralisation, most redox, dissolving). A `reserve` keeps a minimum free concentration of a reactant; this is how *soluble only in excess* is modelled — e.g. Al(OH)₃ only dissolves once enough excess OH⁻ is present.
- **equilibrium** — a single reaction solved exactly for the current contents by bisection on Q = K. Precipitation uses `K = 1/Ksp` (helper `ppt()`), as do silver halide–ammonia, Cu⁺–thiosulfate, starch–iodine and similar equilibria.
- **kinetic** — slow processes advanced with time using the half-life: air oxidation of Fe(OH)₂ / Mn(OH)₂, thiosulfate + acid → sulfur, the purple Fe³⁺–thiosulfate complex fading, catalytic decomposition of H₂O₂, carbonate + acid, reactions that only run when warm, thermal decomposition…

### 3.3 Solving

`Vessel.settle()` sweeps the fast rules (`FAST_RULES`, sorted by priority) repeatedly until nothing changes (max 300 sweeps). Within a sweep higher-priority rules act first, so added NaOH neutralises free acid (priority 1) before it can precipitate a metal hydroxide (priority 3). Changes below a significance threshold are ignored to avoid oscillation.

`Vessel.advance(dt)` steps time in 1–4 s increments: temperature relaxes towards room temperature (or the heating target: warm 65 °C, boil 100 °C, gentle 260 °C, strong 600 °C; a liquid heated to boiling reaches 100 °C in about 35 s and then loses water by evaporation until it boils dry), kinetic rules progress, the fast rules settle again, and volatile gases leave.

### 3.4 Gases

CO₂ beyond saturation forms bubbles (effervescence, with a rate). NH₃ and SO₂ partition into the head-space according to Henry's law constants with a van 't Hoff temperature dependence, so ammonia is detected when warm and is gradually lost. Head-space gases decay with a half-life of about 15 s, so a gas test only works while the gas is being produced. NO turns into brown NO₂ in air. Gas tests (`Lab.gasTest`) compare head-space amounts with thresholds.

### 3.5 Heating dry solids

Compounds can declare thermal steps (`ThermalStep`: `minTemp`, `halfLife`, products, events) — loss of water of crystallisation with condensation, decomposition of carbonates and hydroxides, melting, sublimation. `MIXED_THERMAL` covers reactions between solids heated together (e.g. iodide with a zinc compound giving purple iodine vapour). Some solids have a different colour while hot (`hotColour`).

### 3.6 Observations

`Lab` takes a `snapshot()` of a vessel before and after each action and `describe()` (`observe.ts`) compares them to produce sentences such as *"Pale blue precipitate forms."*, *"The precipitate dissolves, giving a deep blue solution."*, *"Effervescence."*, *"The solution turns from purple to colourless."*. It handles:

- visibility thresholds — a trace of solid is "faint", below a minimum nothing is reported;
- transient precipitates (a `Tracker` remembers the maximum reached during a multi-step addition);
- conversions (Fe(OH)₂ → Fe(OH)₃ "turns brown near the surface");
- reagent colours (purple KMnO₄ persisting vs. decolourised, orange dichromate turning green);
- solution colours from Beer–Lambert mixing of every coloured species (`colour.ts`), named with exam vocabulary.

`appearance()` turns the same state into drawing data (liquid level and RGB, turbidity, sediment, dry solid, pieces, bubbles, gas colour, condensation, flame).

## 4. Using the engine from code

```ts
import { Lab } from './engine'

const lab = new Lab({
  samples: [{ id: 'X', label: 'solution X', kind: 'solution', solution: { conc: { 'Cu2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 }, volume: 20 } }],
  reagents: ['NaOH', 'NH3'],
})

const t = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
lab.transfer(lab.sampleVessel('X').id, t.id, { depth: 1 })
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) console.log(lab.addReagent(t.id, 'NH3', { drops: 1 }).observations)
console.log(lab.addReagent(t.id, 'NH3', { cm3: 4 }).observations)   // deep blue solution
```

Main `Lab` methods (all return an `ActionResult` with `observations` — plus `frames` for additions, heating and standing — and are appended to `lab.journal`):

| Method | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `newVessel(kind, label?)` | `'test-tube' \| 'boiling-tube' \| 'hard-glass-tube'` |
| `sampleVessel(sampleId)` | the vessel holding a sample |
| `addReagent(vesselId, reagentId, amount)` | amounts: `{drops}`, `{cm3}`, `{depth}`, `{spatula:'small'\|'normal'}`, `{grams}`, `{pieces}` |
| `transfer(from, to, amount)` | also `{fraction}` and `{all:true}` |
| `divide(from, kinds[])` | equal portions into new vessels; returns `vesselIds` |
| `heat(id, 'warm'\|'boil'\|'gentle'\|'strong', seconds)` | |
| `wait(seconds, id?)`, `tick(dt)` | let time pass (one vessel or all) |
| `shake(id)`, `setStopper(id, on)` | |
| `gasTest(id, test)` | `damp-red-litmus`, `damp-blue-litmus`, `limewater`, `glowing-splint`, `lighted-splint`, `acidified-kmno4`, `acidified-dichromate-paper` |
| `flameTest(id)`, `pHTest(id)` | |
| `filter(id)` | returns `filtrateId` and `residueId` (filter paper) |
| `empty(id)`, `rinse(id)`, `discard(id)` | `empty` leaves a wet film; `rinse` cleans |

The interactive bench works in real time and uses a second, continuous API. An action is opened with `begin()`, material is added in small steps while bench time passes with `tick()`, and the action is written up (one journal entry) a few seconds after `release()`, so delayed changes such as fizzing are included:

| Method | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `begin(vesselId, label, {heating?})` → `OpenAction` | start an action on a vessel |
| `pourStep(a, source, cm3)` | pour from `{reagentId}` or `{vesselId}` (a dry tube tips its solid out) |
| `dropStep(a, reagentId, n?)` | drops from a dropper bottle |
| `scoop(source, grams)` / `deposit(a, n)` | take a spatula measure (or a piece of metal) / tip it into the vessel |
| `filterStep(a, source, paperId, cm3)` | pour through a filter paper (vessel of kind `filter-paper`) into the action's vessel |
| `setHeating(id, mode \| null)` | heat while time passes |
| `release(a, label?, linger?)`, `cancel(a)`, `flush()` | finish (written up after `linger` s), drop an empty action, write up everything |
| `agitate(id)` | shake |
| `mouthTest(id, test, {damp})` | test paper / splint at the mouth of the tube, no time passes (a dry paper shows no change) |
| `indicatorDip(id, paper)` | dip red litmus, blue litmus or universal indicator paper into the contents |
| `collectGas(id)` / `bubbleGas(sample, id)` | teat pipette: draw in the gas at the mouth / bubble it through a liquid (limewater turns milky) |
| `flameReading(id)`, `gasReading(id, test)`, `note(…)` | readings without side effects; record a note in the journal |

## 5. Adding a past paper

1. **Read the source material** — question paper, mark scheme and confidential instructions (in the LFS archive in `dropbox_downloads/`). Note the exact composition and mass of each unknown and the concentrations of the bench reagents.
2. **Check the chemistry is covered** — every compound in the unknowns must exist in `compounds.ts`, every reagent in `reagents.ts`. Add what is missing (section 6). Paper-specific reagents (e.g. *FB 8* = 0.100 mol dm⁻³ thiosulfate) can be registered in the paper file with `registerReagent()`, as `alevel.ts` does.
3. **Write the `PaperCase`** in `src/papers/igcse.ts` or `alevel.ts`:

```ts
export const P0620_s25_51: PaperCase = {
  id: '0620_s25_51',
  syllabus: 'Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620',
  level: 'IGCSE',
  series: 'May/June 2025',
  component: 'Paper 51 (Practical Test)',
  question: 2,
  totalMarks: 15,                         // must equal the sum of the parts' marks (tested)
  summary: 'One solid, X, containing two ions.',   // neutral: must not give the answer away
  samples: [{ id: 'X', label: 'solid X', kind: 'solid', solid: [{ compound: 'FeSO4.7H2O', grams: 2 }] }],
  reagents: ['water', 'NaOH', 'NH3', 'HNO3', 'AgNO3', 'Ba(NO3)2'],
  intro: 'You are provided with solid X. …',
  parts: [
    { id: 'a', label: '(a)', text: 'Dissolve … Record your observations.', marks: 2,
      answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 3 },          // or { kind: 'table', header, rows } / { kind: 'fields', fields }
      markScheme: ['green precipitate [1]', 'insoluble in excess [1]'] },
    // …
  ],
  identities: ['Solid X: iron(II) sulfate-7-water (2 g).'],
  omitted: undefined,                     // describe any part of the original question not simulated
  supervisor(lab) {
    // carry out the instructions exactly; return the observation text for each part id
    const X = lab.sampleVessel('X')
    // …
    return { a: t(/* ActionResults */) }
  },
}
```

   - `preamble` on a part holds procedure text that belongs to no single part (e.g. "Divide solution E into four portions…").
   - The `t(...results)` helper in `igcse.ts` joins observation texts.
   - The supervisor script is used both for the "Supervisor's results" on the self-marking page and for the regression test, so make it follow the wording of the question literally (amounts, order, "leave to stand").
4. **Register it** — add it to `IGCSE_PAPERS` / `AS_PAPERS`; `papers/index.ts` exposes all papers to the home page.
5. **Add mark-scheme expectations** in `src/engine/__tests__/papers.test.ts`:

```ts
'0620_s25_51': {
  a: [/[Gg]reen precipitate forms/, /does not dissolve|insoluble/],
},
```

   Each regex must match the supervisor's observation text for that part. Run `npm test`; if a pattern fails, fix the chemistry (rules, constants, thresholds), not the expectation — unless the expectation itself is wrong.
6. **Check the UI** with `npm run dev`: do the question on the bench as a student would, including a few typical mistakes.

## 6. Adding reagents, compounds, species and reactions

### Reagents (`reagents.ts`)

```ts
add({ id: 'KI', label: 'aqueous potassium iodide', short: 'KI(aq)', kind: 'solution', conc: { 'K+': 0.1, 'I-': 0.1 } })
add({ id: 'Mg-ribbon', label: 'magnesium ribbon (2 cm)', short: 'Mg ribbon', kind: 'piece', compound: 'Mg', pieceGrams: 0.022 })
```

`kind` is `'solution'` (concentrations in mol dm⁻³, must be charge-balanced), `'solid'` (spatula measures) or `'piece'`. `bottleTint` colours the bottle in the UI. `solutionFromGrams()` converts "g dm⁻³ of compound" from the confidential instructions into concentrations.

### Compounds (`compounds.ts`)

```ts
c({ id: 'NaBr', formula: 'NaBr', name: 'sodium bromide', molarMass: 102.89, colour: '#fafafa', colourName: 'white',
  dissolves: { 'Na+': 1, 'Br-': 1 } })
```

Soluble compounds give `dissolves`; insoluble ones give `asSpecies` (the solid species they become). Add `thermal` steps if the solid is heated in a question. Hydrated salts include their water of crystallisation in the molar mass and release `H2O(g)` on heating (condensation).

### Species (`species.ts`)

Every id used in a rule must be a registered species (tested). Aqueous coloured species need `tint` + `refConc` (the apparent colour at that concentration) and `colourName`; solids need `colour`, `colourName` and optionally `visibility` (how strongly a small amount shows) and `hotColour`; gases may have `gasColour`; flame-test elements set `flame`.

### Rules (`rules.ts`)

```ts
// precipitation: K = 1/Ksp
ppt('Ni(OH)2', { 'Ni2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Ni(OH)2(s)', 5.5e-16)

// soluble in excess, only once some free OH- is left over
r({ id: 'Zn(OH)2+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Zn(OH)2(s)': 1, 'OH-': 2 },
  products: { 'Zn(OH)4 2-': 1 }, reserve: { 'OH-': 0.02 } })

// slow process with a condition
r({ id: 'ClO+H+Cl', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'ClO-': 1, 'Cl-': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Cl2(g)': 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('H+') > 1 && x.c('Cl-') > 0.5 ? 10 : Infinity) })
```

(The exact parameters of existing rules are in `rules.ts`; the snippets illustrate the shape.)

Guidelines:

- Choose `priority` by what must happen first: neutralisation (1) → carbonate / sulfite protonation (2–3) → precipitation (3) → complexation / dissolving in excess (4–5) → redox.
- Rules must conserve charge (tested) and atoms.
- Prefer real constants (Ksp, K) and adjust only where needed to reproduce what the syllabus notes and mark schemes describe. Add a comment when a value is tuned.
- Use `events` for things that are seen but are not a change in amount (e.g. `'effervescence'`, `'crackling'`).
- After a change, run all tests — a new rule can change the results of other papers.

## 7. Testing

| File | Covers |
|---|---|
| `cations.test.ts` | NaOH and NH₃ tests for Al³⁺, Ca²⁺, Ba²⁺, Cr³⁺, Cu²⁺, Fe²⁺, Fe³⁺, Mg²⁺, Mn²⁺, Zn²⁺, NH₄⁺; flame tests |
| `anions.test.ts` | carbonate, nitrate, nitrite, sulfate, sulfite, thiosulfate, halides, iodine/starch; gas tests (H₂, O₂) |
| `procedure.test.ts` | order of addition and existing contents: wrong acid, no acidification, un-rinsed tubes, tap water, leftover H₂O₂, transient precipitates, re-acidifying complexes, ammonia lost on cooling, slow air oxidation |
| `papers.test.ts` | every `PaperCase` supervisor run against its mark scheme; marks add up |
| `rules.test.ts` | every species referenced by a rule exists; every rule conserves charge |
| `thermal.test.ts` | heated salts keep their non-volatile ions; boiling evaporates water, warming does not |
| `realtime.test.ts` | continuous API: pours, drops, heating in the flame, damp vs dry litmus, dipping litmus, teat pipette and limewater, filtering |
| `helpers.ts` | `solutionLab`, `solidLab`, `portion`, `dropwiseThenExcess`, `text` |
| `ui/bench/__tests__/model.test.ts` | gestures on the bench model (drag, tap-then-tap, press-and-hold): pouring, dropper, burner, splint, spatula, teat pipette, wire, litmus, funnel, waste |

Write a procedure test whenever you fix a behaviour caused by an operation order or leftover contents, so it stays fixed.

## 8. The user interface

- `App.tsx` holds the screen state (`home` / `exam` / `results`). Each attempt mounts a fresh `Exam` with a new `Lab` and `BenchModel`.
- **Everything on the bench is apparatus.** There are no action buttons: an item is picked up and brought to something else. `BenchModel` (`bench/model.ts`) decides what that means from *what is held* and *which zone it is over*:
  - zones are worked out geometrically from the layout (`zoneFor`): a tube's mouth, inside or liquid; a rack slot; the burner's upper or lower heating position; the flame; the wash-bottle nozzle; waste, sink, tile, funnel, boxes, jars;
  - **discrete** actions happen on release (tube into a slot or the flame, stopper on, spatula, dropper parked in a tube, funnel onto a tube, tube into the waste or sink);
  - **continuous / dwell** actions happen while the item stays there (a bottle pours while held over a tube; a strip tests the gas after ~0.7 s at the mouth; a splint lights after ~0.4 s in the flame);
  - two input styles share the same logic: **drag**, and **tap to lift, then tap the target** (press and hold on the target for continuous actions). With an item lifted, a press elsewhere is resolved on release or after 160 ms: moving drags the other object (the lifted item is put back), holding still starts the hold action, a quick tap uses the item there.
- Apparatus state lives in the model: burner (gas, lit, air hole), splints (unlit / burning / glowing / dead, with burn-out times), matches, strips (damp, colour), teat pipette (gas sample), wire (loaded reading), funnel (tube, filter paper vessel), parked dropper, used strips on the tile, fast clock.
- `Exam.tsx` calls `model.step(dt)` every 100 ms; the clock on the bench runs bench time ×1 or ×10. The engine's open actions are written up when they settle, and the latest entry can be shown as a caption when *Observation text* is on.
- `Bench.tsx` renders the scene (`viewBox` 1360 × 900) scaled to fit; on narrow phones it fills the height and scrolls sideways. Invisible hit rectangles (`model.hitRects()`, first = on top) receive the pointer; they have `touch-action: none` so drags do not scroll, while the background still pans.
- Glassware is SVG: liquids use multiply blending, precipitates and turbidity use noise filters (`SvgDefs` in `Tube.tsx`), bubbles, flames, streams and drops are CSS animations. Tubes are memoised on their (cached) `Appearance`.
- Layouts (`styles.css`): ≥ 1100 px (desktop, iPad landscape) = bench left, paper/notebook right (the paper can be hidden with ⇥); ≤ 1099 px = one pane at a time with bottom tabs (Lab / Paper / Notebook); tall tablets in portrait (700–1099 px wide, ≥ 960 px high) = bench above, paper below.
- `storage.ts` stores answers, notes, marks and preferences (observation text, sound, demo seen) under the `qa-lab:` prefix; every access is wrapped in `try/catch`.
- The UI language is English (matching the exam). Text on the bench is limited to what is printed on real apparatus (bottle labels, tube labels).

## 9. Building

`npm run build` writes a static site to `dist/`. `vite.config.ts` sets `base: './'`, so the files work from any sub-path or even from a local folder served by a static server.

## 10. Conventions

- TypeScript strict mode; no `any` in engine code.
- Engine code stays free of DOM and React.
- Amounts in mmol, volumes in cm³, concentrations in mol dm⁻³, temperatures in °C, times in seconds.
- Observation sentences follow mark-scheme vocabulary (British spelling: *colour*, *sulfate*, *aluminium*).
- Question wording in `papers/` follows the published paper; summaries must not reveal the identities.
- Run `npm test` and `npm run build` before committing.

# 开发者指南

[English](developer-guide.md) · **简体中文** · [返回 README](../README.zh-CN.md)

介绍 Qualitative Analysis Lab 的构建方式、化学反应引擎的工作原理，以及如何新增真题、试剂、化合物和反应。

## 目录

1. [环境与命令](#1-环境与命令)
2. [架构](#2-架构)

## 1. 环境与命令

需要 Node.js 18 或以上（开发时使用 Node 22）及 npm。

```bash
npm install
npm run dev          # Vite 开发服务器，http://localhost:5173（加 -- --host 可供局域网设备访问）
npm test             # Vitest，运行全部引擎及真题测试
npx vitest           # 监听模式
npm run typecheck    # tsc，strict 模式
npm run build        # tsc -b && vite build → dist/
npm run preview      # 预览 dist/
```

技术栈：Vite 5、React 18、TypeScript 5（`strict`）、Vitest 2。无后端，除 React 外没有运行时依赖。

## 2. 架构

```
src/
  main.tsx                 React 入口
  engine/                  化学反应引擎——纯 TypeScript，不依赖 DOM/React
    species.ts             物种目录：离子、配合物、沉淀、气体；颜色、焰色
    compounds.ts           起始物质（化学式、摩尔质量、溶解方式、受热分解步骤）
    reagents.ts            实验台试剂（浓度按机密说明）
    rules.ts               反应规则（complete / equilibrium / kinetic）
    vessel.ts              试管状态；规则求解；时间、温度、气体
    colour.ts              Beer–Lambert 混色及考试用颜色词
    observe.ts             把状态变化转为考试用语的观察句子
    lab.ts                 Lab：容器 + 操作（加试剂、倒液、加热、气体检验……）+ 操作记录
    appearance.ts          供界面绘制容器的数据
    index.ts               公开导出
    __tests__/             单元测试及真题回归测试
  papers/
    types.ts               PaperCase / QuestionPart / AnswerSpec
    igcse.ts               0620 Paper 5 案例
    alevel.ts              9701 Paper 3 案例（及试卷专用试剂）
    index.ts               PAPERS 列表、paperById()
  ui/
    App.tsx                画面：首页 → 考试 → 结果
    Exam.tsx               考试画面：实验台、试卷、笔记、手机标签、实时循环
    Tube.tsx               SVG 玻璃仪器（液体、沉淀、气泡、塞子）；也可嵌入实验台场景
    bench/
      layout.ts            场景尺寸及每件仪器的位置；试管几何
      art.tsx              仪器的 SVG 绘图（试剂瓶、滴管、本生灯、木条、试纸……）
      model.ts             BenchModel：手上拿着什么、在哪里、手势代表什么；驱动 Lab
      Bench.tsx            绘制场景并把指针输入交给模型
      Demo.tsx             首次使用时的"虚拟手"示范
      sound.ts             合成音效（WebAudio）
      __tests__/           针对模型的手势测试
    PaperPane.tsx          带答题位置的试卷
    Results.tsx            自我评分页面
    storage.ts             带保护的 localStorage 工具函数
    styles.css             全部样式及响应式断点
```

依赖方向严格为 `ui → papers → engine`。引擎从不引用界面代码，可以独立测试和编写脚本。

## 3. 化学反应引擎

引擎是**半定量**的：准确程度足以让观察结果符合 Cambridge 评分标准的给分点，并让错误操作改变结果，但并非研究级的物理化学模型。

### 3.1 状态

每个 `Vessel`（`vessel.ts`）保存：

- `n`——每种物种的物质的量，单位 **mmol**（离子、溶解分子、固体、未溶解的化合物）；
- `V`——液体体积，单位 cm³（低于一个很小的阈值即视为"干"）；
- `T`——温度（°C）、加热方式、塞子；
- 液面上方的气体（head-space）、气泡速率、冷凝水、沉淀的沉降程度；
- 事件记录。

引擎中没有任何地方知道"正确答案"：只有试管里存在反应物，反应才会发生。

物种 id 是普通字符串，例如 `'Cu2+'`、`'SO4 2-'`、`'OH-'`、`'Cu(OH)2(s)'`、`'CO2(aq)'`、`'NH3'`、`'Cl2(g)'`。未溶解的起始物质为 `cmp:<化合物 id>`。水从不显式写出。

### 3.2 规则

规则位于 `rules.ts`：

```ts
interface Rule {
  id: string
  type: 'complete' | 'equilibrium' | 'kinetic'
  reactants: Record<string, number>   // 化学计量
  products: Record<string, number>
  priority?: number                   // 数值越小越先执行（快反应）
  K?: number                          // 平衡常数，mol dm⁻³ 单位，不含固体和水
  reserve?: Record<string, number>    // 必须保留的最低游离浓度
  halfLife?: number | ((ctx) => number) // 动力学规则的半衰期（秒），Infinity 表示不进行
  when?: (ctx) => boolean             // 额外条件（温度、pH、浓度……）
  dry?: boolean; anyPhase?: boolean; minTemp?: number
  events?: string[]                   // 可观察事件，如 'effervescence'
}
```

- **complete**——进行到底的快反应（中和、大多数氧化还原、溶解）。`reserve` 让某反应物保持最低游离浓度，用来模拟"过量才溶解"——例如 Al(OH)₃ 只有在有足够过量 OH⁻ 时才溶解。
- **equilibrium**——针对当前内容，用二分法精确求解 Q = K 的单一平衡。沉淀使用 `K = 1/Ksp`（辅助函数 `ppt()`），卤化银–氨、Cu⁺–硫代硫酸根、淀粉–碘等平衡也是如此。
- **kinetic**——按半衰期随时间推进的慢过程：Fe(OH)₂／Mn(OH)₂ 的空气氧化、硫代硫酸根遇酸析出硫、Fe³⁺–硫代硫酸根紫色配合物褪色、H₂O₂ 催化分解、碳酸盐与酸、只在加热时进行的反应、热分解等。

### 3.3 求解

`Vessel.settle()` 反复扫描快反应（`FAST_RULES`，按优先级排序），直到不再变化（最多 300 轮）。每轮中优先级高的规则先执行，因此加入的 NaOH 会先中和游离酸（优先级 1），之后才会沉淀金属氢氧化物（优先级 3）。低于显著性阈值的变化会被忽略，以避免振荡。

`Vessel.advance(dt)` 以 1–4 秒为步长推进时间：温度向室温（或加热目标：warm 65 °C、boil 100 °C、gentle 260 °C、strong 600 °C；加热至沸腾的液体约 35 秒达到 100 °C，之后水分蒸发，直至蒸干）靠近，动力学规则推进，快反应重新求解，挥发性气体逸出。

### 3.4 气体

超过饱和的 CO₂ 形成气泡（effervescence，带速率）。NH₃ 和 SO₂ 按亨利常数（含 van 't Hoff 温度修正）分配到液面上方，所以氨气在加热时才能检出，并会逐渐散失。液面上方的气体以约 15 秒的半衰期衰减，因此只有在气体生成期间检验才有效。NO 在空气中变成棕色 NO₂。气体检验（`Lab.gasTest`）把液面上方气体的量与阈值比较。

### 3.5 加热干燥固体

化合物可以声明受热步骤（`ThermalStep`：`minTemp`、`halfLife`、产物、事件）——失去结晶水并产生冷凝水、碳酸盐和氢氧化物分解、熔化、升华。`MIXED_THERMAL` 处理一起加热的固体之间的反应（例如碘化物与锌化合物产生紫色碘蒸气）。有些固体在热时颜色不同（`hotColour`）。

### 3.6 观察

`Lab` 在每次操作前后对容器做 `snapshot()`，再由 `describe()`（`observe.ts`）比较两者，生成如 *"Pale blue precipitate forms."*、*"The precipitate dissolves, giving a deep blue solution."*、*"Effervescence."*、*"The solution turns from purple to colourless."* 的句子。它处理：

- 可见度阈值——微量固体为 "faint"，低于下限则不报告；
- 短暂出现的沉淀（`Tracker` 记录多步加入过程中达到的最大量）；
- 转化（Fe(OH)₂ → Fe(OH)₃，"turns brown near the surface"）；
- 试剂颜色（紫色 KMnO₄ 保持或褪色、橙色重铬酸盐变绿）；
- 以 Beer–Lambert 定律混合所有有色物种得到的溶液颜色（`colour.ts`），并用考试用词命名。

`appearance()` 把同一状态转为绘图数据（液面高度及 RGB、浑浊度、沉淀、干燥固体、片状物、气泡、气体颜色、冷凝水、火焰）。

## 4. 在代码中使用引擎

```ts
import { Lab } from './engine'

const lab = new Lab({
  samples: [{ id: 'X', label: 'solution X', kind: 'solution', solution: { conc: { 'Cu2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 }, volume: 20 } }],
  reagents: ['NaOH', 'NH3'],
})

const t = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
lab.transfer(lab.sampleVessel('X').id, t.id, { depth: 1 })
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) console.log(lab.addReagent(t.id, 'NH3', { drops: 1 }).observations)
console.log(lab.addReagent(t.id, 'NH3', { cm3: 4 }).observations)   // 深蓝色溶液
```

`Lab` 的主要方法（均返回带 `observations` 的 `ActionResult`——加试剂、加热和静置还带 `frames`——并追加到 `lab.journal`）：

| 方法 | 用途 |
|---|---|
| `newVessel(kind, label?)` | `'test-tube' \| 'boiling-tube' \| 'hard-glass-tube'` |
| `sampleVessel(sampleId)` | 装有样品的容器 |
| `addReagent(vesselId, reagentId, amount)` | 数量：`{drops}`、`{cm3}`、`{depth}`、`{spatula:'small'\|'normal'}`、`{grams}`、`{pieces}` |
| `transfer(from, to, amount)` | 另可用 `{fraction}` 和 `{all:true}` |
| `divide(from, kinds[])` | 平均分到新容器，返回 `vesselIds` |
| `heat(id, 'warm'\|'boil'\|'gentle'\|'strong', seconds)` | |
| `wait(seconds, id?)`、`tick(dt)` | 让时间流逝（单个容器或全部） |
| `shake(id)`、`setStopper(id, on)` | |
| `gasTest(id, test)` | `damp-red-litmus`、`damp-blue-litmus`、`limewater`、`glowing-splint`、`lighted-splint`、`acidified-kmno4`、`acidified-dichromate-paper` |
| `flameTest(id)`、`pHTest(id)` | |
| `filter(id)` | 返回 `filtrateId` 和 `residueId`（滤纸） |
| `empty(id)`、`rinse(id)`、`discard(id)` | `empty` 会留下液膜；`rinse` 会清洗干净 |

交互实验台以实时方式运行，使用另一套连续 API：用 `begin()` 开始一个操作，在 `tick()` 推进实验台时间的同时分小步加入物质，`release()` 后再过几秒才写入记录（一条记录），因此延迟出现的变化（例如冒泡）也会包括在内：

| 方法 | 用途 |
|---|---|
| `begin(vesselId, label, {heating?})` → `OpenAction` | 对一个容器开始操作 |
| `pourStep(a, source, cm3)` | 从 `{reagentId}` 或 `{vesselId}` 倾倒（干燥的试管会把固体倒出） |
| `dropStep(a, reagentId, n?)` | 从滴瓶滴加 |
| `scoop(source, grams)` / `deposit(a, n)` | 取一药匙（或一片金属）／倒进容器 |
| `filterStep(a, source, paperId, cm3)` | 经滤纸（`filter-paper` 类容器）倒入操作对应的容器 |
| `setHeating(id, mode \| null)` | 在时间流逝时加热 |
| `release(a, label?, linger?)`、`cancel(a)`、`flush()` | 结束（`linger` 秒后写入记录）、丢弃空操作、立即写入全部记录 |
| `agitate(id)` | 振荡 |
| `mouthTest(id, test, {damp})` | 在试管口用试纸／木条检验，不推进时间（干燥试纸没有变化） |
| `indicatorDip(id, paper)` | 把红色石蕊、蓝色石蕊或通用指示剂试纸浸入内容物 |
| `collectGas(id)` / `bubbleGas(sample, id)` | 胶头滴管：在试管口吸入气体／把气体通入液体（石灰水变浑浊） |
| `flameReading(id)`、`gasReading(id, test)`、`note(…)` | 无副作用的读数；在记录中写入备注 |

## 5. 新增真题

1. **阅读原始资料**——试卷、评分标准及机密说明（在 `dropbox_downloads/` 的 LFS 压缩包中）。记下每种未知物的确切成分和质量，以及实验台试剂的浓度。
2. **确认化学内容已覆盖**——未知物中的每种化合物必须在 `compounds.ts` 中存在，每种试剂必须在 `reagents.ts` 中存在。缺少的请补充（见第 6 节）。试卷专用试剂（例如 *FB 8* = 0.100 mol dm⁻³ 硫代硫酸钠）可以像 `alevel.ts` 那样在试卷文件中用 `registerReagent()` 注册。
3. **编写 `PaperCase`**，放在 `src/papers/igcse.ts` 或 `alevel.ts`：

```ts
export const P0620_s25_51: PaperCase = {
  id: '0620_s25_51',
  syllabus: 'Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620',
  level: 'IGCSE',
  series: 'May/June 2025',
  component: 'Paper 51 (Practical Test)',
  question: 2,
  totalMarks: 15,                         // 必须等于各小题分数之和（有测试检查）
  summary: 'One solid, X, containing two ions.',   // 中性描述：不得透露答案
  samples: [{ id: 'X', label: 'solid X', kind: 'solid', solid: [{ compound: 'FeSO4.7H2O', grams: 2 }] }],
  reagents: ['water', 'NaOH', 'NH3', 'HNO3', 'AgNO3', 'Ba(NO3)2'],
  intro: 'You are provided with solid X. …',
  parts: [
    { id: 'a', label: '(a)', text: 'Dissolve … Record your observations.', marks: 2,
      answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 3 },          // 或 { kind: 'table', header, rows } / { kind: 'fields', fields }
      markScheme: ['green precipitate [1]', 'insoluble in excess [1]'] },
    // …
  ],
  identities: ['Solid X: iron(II) sulfate-7-water (2 g).'],
  omitted: undefined,                     // 说明原题中未模拟的部分
  supervisor(lab) {
    // 严格按题目步骤操作；返回每个小题 id 对应的观察文字
    const X = lab.sampleVessel('X')
    // …
    return { a: t(/* ActionResults */) }
  },
}
```

   - 小题的 `preamble` 用于放不属于单一小题的步骤文字（例如 "Divide solution E into four portions…"）。
   - `igcse.ts` 中的辅助函数 `t(...results)` 用于合并观察文字。
   - 监考员脚本同时用于自我评分页面的"Supervisor's results"和回归测试，所以要逐字按题目操作（用量、顺序、"leave to stand"）。
4. **注册**——加入 `IGCSE_PAPERS`／`AS_PAPERS`；`papers/index.ts` 会把所有真题提供给首页。
5. **在 `src/engine/__tests__/papers.test.ts` 中加入评分标准检查**：

```ts
'0620_s25_51': {
  a: [/[Gg]reen precipitate forms/, /does not dissolve|insoluble/],
},
```

   每个正则表达式都必须匹配该小题的监考员观察文字。运行 `npm test`；若某个检查失败，应修正化学模型（规则、常数、阈值），而不是修改检查——除非检查本身写错了。
6. **在界面中检查**：运行 `npm run dev`，像学生一样在实验台上完成该题，并尝试几种典型的错误操作。

## 6. 新增试剂、化合物、物种和反应

### 试剂（`reagents.ts`）

```ts
add({ id: 'KI', label: 'aqueous potassium iodide', short: 'KI(aq)', kind: 'solution', conc: { 'K+': 0.1, 'I-': 0.1 } })
add({ id: 'Mg-ribbon', label: 'magnesium ribbon (2 cm)', short: 'Mg ribbon', kind: 'piece', compound: 'Mg', pieceGrams: 0.022 })
```

`kind` 可为 `'solution'`（浓度单位 mol dm⁻³，电荷必须平衡）、`'solid'`（药匙量）或 `'piece'`。`bottleTint` 决定界面中试剂瓶的颜色。`solutionFromGrams()` 可把机密说明中的"每 dm³ 含若干克化合物"换算为浓度。

### 化合物（`compounds.ts`）

```ts
c({ id: 'NaBr', formula: 'NaBr', name: 'sodium bromide', molarMass: 102.89, colour: '#fafafa', colourName: 'white',
  dissolves: { 'Na+': 1, 'Br-': 1 } })
```

可溶化合物填写 `dissolves`；不溶化合物填写 `asSpecies`（对应的固体物种）。若题目会加热该固体，加入 `thermal` 步骤。含结晶水的盐，摩尔质量要包括结晶水，并在加热时释放 `H2O(g)`（产生冷凝水）。

### 物种（`species.ts`）

规则中用到的每个 id 都必须是已注册的物种（有测试检查）。有色的水溶物种需要 `tint` + `refConc`（该浓度下的表观颜色）及 `colourName`；固体需要 `colour`、`colourName`，可选 `visibility`（少量时的显眼程度）和 `hotColour`；气体可设 `gasColour`；焰色元素设 `flame`。

### 规则（`rules.ts`）

```ts
// 沉淀：K = 1/Ksp
ppt('Ni(OH)2', { 'Ni2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Ni(OH)2(s)', 5.5e-16)

// 过量才溶解：只有在剩余一定游离 OH- 时才进行
r({ id: 'Zn(OH)2+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Zn(OH)2(s)': 1, 'OH-': 2 },
  products: { 'Zn(OH)4 2-': 1 }, reserve: { 'OH-': 0.02 } })

// 带条件的慢过程
r({ id: 'ClO+H+Cl', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'ClO-': 1, 'Cl-': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Cl2(g)': 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('H+') > 1 && x.c('Cl-') > 0.5 ? 10 : Infinity) })
```

（现有规则的确切参数见 `rules.ts`；以上代码只用于说明写法。）

指引：

- 按"什么必须先发生"选择 `priority`：中和（1）→ 碳酸根／亚硫酸根质子化（2–3）→ 沉淀（3）→ 配位／过量溶解（4–5）→ 氧化还原。
- 规则必须守恒电荷（有测试检查）和原子。
- 尽量使用真实常数（Ksp、K），只在需要重现课程大纲和评分标准所描述的现象时才调整。调整过的数值请加注释。
- 对看得见但不是物质的量变化的现象使用 `events`（例如 `'effervescence'`、`'crackling'`）。
- 修改后运行全部测试——新规则可能会改变其他真题的结果。

## 7. 测试

| 文件 | 覆盖范围 |
|---|---|
| `cations.test.ts` | Al³⁺、Ca²⁺、Ba²⁺、Cr³⁺、Cu²⁺、Fe²⁺、Fe³⁺、Mg²⁺、Mn²⁺、Zn²⁺、NH₄⁺ 的 NaOH 及 NH₃ 测试；焰色测试 |
| `anions.test.ts` | 碳酸根、硝酸根、亚硝酸根、硫酸根、亚硫酸根、硫代硫酸根、卤离子、碘／淀粉；气体检验（H₂、O₂） |
| `procedure.test.ts` | 加入顺序及原有内容：用错酸、未酸化、未清洗试管、自来水、残留 H₂O₂、短暂沉淀、配合物再酸化、冷却后氨气散失、缓慢空气氧化 |
| `papers.test.ts` | 每个 `PaperCase` 的监考员操作对照评分标准；分数总和正确 |
| `rules.test.ts` | 规则引用的每个物种都存在；每条规则电荷守恒 |
| `thermal.test.ts` | 受热的盐保留非挥发性离子；沸腾会蒸发水分，温和加热不会 |
| `realtime.test.ts` | 连续 API：倾倒、滴加、在火焰中加热、湿润与干燥石蕊试纸、浸入试纸、胶头滴管与石灰水、过滤 |
| `helpers.ts` | `solutionLab`、`solidLab`、`portion`、`dropwiseThenExcess`、`text` |
| `ui/bench/__tests__/model.test.ts` | 实验台模型上的手势（拖动、点一下再点一下、按住）：倾倒、滴管、本生灯、木条、药匙、胶头滴管、金属丝、石蕊试纸、漏斗、废液缸 |

每当修复一个由操作顺序或残留内容引起的问题时，请写一个 procedure 测试，确保问题不会复发。

## 8. 用户界面

- `App.tsx` 保存画面状态（`home`／`exam`／`results`）。每次作答都会挂载新的 `Exam`、`Lab` 和 `BenchModel`。
- **实验台上的一切都是仪器。** 没有操作按钮：拿起一样东西，带到另一样东西那里。`BenchModel`（`bench/model.ts`）根据*手上拿着什么*和*它在哪个区域上方*决定这代表什么：
  - 区域按布局以几何方式计算（`zoneFor`）：试管口、管内或液面以下；试管架空位；本生灯的上／下加热位置；火焰；洗瓶嘴；废液缸、水槽、白瓷板、漏斗、试管盒、广口瓶；
  - **一次性**动作在松手时发生（试管放进空位或火焰、塞上塞子、药匙、滴管停放在试管上、漏斗放到试管上、试管拖到废液缸或水槽）；
  - **持续／停留**动作在物件停留期间发生（试剂瓶放在试管上方就一直倾倒；试纸在试管口约 0.7 秒后检验气体；木条在火焰中约 0.4 秒后点燃）；
  - 两种输入方式共用同一套逻辑：**拖动**，以及**点一下拿起、再点一下目标**（持续动作则在目标上按住）。拿起物件后，在别处按下会在松手或 160 毫秒后才判断：移动表示拖动另一件物件（先把拿着的放回），按住不动表示开始持续动作，快速点击表示在该处使用物件。
- 仪器状态保存在模型中：本生灯（燃气、是否点燃、空气孔）、木条（未点燃／燃烧／带火星／熄灭，含燃尽时间）、火柴、试纸（是否湿润、颜色）、胶头滴管（气体样本）、金属丝（已蘸取的读数）、漏斗（所在试管、滤纸容器）、停放的滴管、白瓷板上用过的试纸、时钟快进。
- `Exam.tsx` 每 100 毫秒调用一次 `model.step(dt)`；实验台的时钟让实验台时间以 ×1 或 ×10 速度运行。引擎中未结束的操作在稳定后写入记录；打开 *Observation text* 时，最新一条记录会以字幕显示。
- `Bench.tsx` 把场景（`viewBox` 1360 × 900）缩放到合适大小；在较窄的手机上会占满高度并左右滚动。不可见的点击矩形（`model.hitRects()`，列表第一项在最上层）接收指针事件；它们设置了 `touch-action: none`，拖动时不会滚动，而背景仍可平移。
- 玻璃仪器使用 SVG：液体使用 multiply 混合，沉淀和浑浊使用噪声滤镜（`Tube.tsx` 中的 `SvgDefs`），气泡、火焰、液流和液滴使用 CSS 动画。试管以（已缓存的）`Appearance` 做记忆化渲染。
- 布局（`styles.css`）：宽度 ≥ 1100 px（电脑、iPad 横屏）＝左边实验台、右边试卷／笔记（可用 ⇥ 隐藏试卷）；≤ 1099 px＝每次显示一个面板，底部有标签（Lab / Paper / Notebook）；竖屏的高平板（宽 700–1099 px、高 ≥ 960 px）＝上方实验台、下方试卷。
- `storage.ts` 以 `qa-lab:` 为前缀保存答案、笔记、分数和偏好设置（观察文字、声音、是否已看过示范）；每次访问都包在 `try/catch` 中。
- 界面语言为英文（与考试一致）。实验台上的文字只限于真实仪器上本来就有的文字（试剂瓶标签、试管标签）。

## 9. 构建

`npm run build` 把静态网站输出到 `dist/`。`vite.config.ts` 设置了 `base: './'`，所以文件可放在任何子路径下，或由静态服务器从本地文件夹提供。

## 10. 约定

- TypeScript strict 模式；引擎代码中不使用 `any`。
- 引擎代码不依赖 DOM 和 React。
- 物质的量用 mmol，体积用 cm³，浓度用 mol dm⁻³，温度用 °C，时间用秒。
- 观察句子沿用评分标准用语（英式拼写：*colour*、*sulfate*、*aluminium*）。
- `papers/` 中的题目文字沿用原卷；`summary` 不得透露未知物身份。
- 提交前运行 `npm test` 和 `npm run build`。

# 2023–2026 真题覆盖清单（任务二）

当前结果不是“所有 AS 和 IG 原卷第二题的完整模拟”。本轮按 2023–2026 四年处理，保留原卷题号，未将 AS 的 Q1/Q3 改称 Q2。

- 已接入资料包内全部 **67 组定性题**：IGCSE 22，AS 45。每组含原题、原评分标准和原材料说明；共 201 份 PDF、185 页题目、659 页资料图像，可本地阅读、缩放、作答、自评。
- **9 组引导实验**：原先 7 组，加上 2023 年 AS 31/35 两组 Q2；操作流程与评分现象有回归检查。原有 `9701_s24_33` 引导实验仍只模拟 (a)，有机 (b) 见原题，未伪称已模拟。
- **18 组新配好的 IG 自由实验台**：样品身份、质量/浓度、试剂取自对应材料说明；有代表性反应检查，未逐个验证全部评分点。加上原有 4 组，22 组 IG 均能进入实验台。
- **40 组 AS 目前仅原题书面练习**，不显示虚假的交互实验台或监考员结果。
- 提供年份、原题号、练习模式和卷号筛选。评分标准和材料说明在提交后出现，学习者答案独立保存。

## 尚未完成的要求与外部阻碍

这份资料包收集的是定性分析题；其中 AS 原卷 Q2 只有 `9701_w23_31`、`9701_w23_35`。不能用其余 Q3 的资料冒充“全部 AS 第二题”。要补齐其他 AS Q2，需取得完整原卷、对应评分标准和材料说明，再按其具体实验（可能涉及定量等）开发和验证。

当前从 `pastpapers.papacambridge.com` 补取完整原卷返回 HTTP 403。所需域名 `pastpapers.papacambridge.com` 和 `www.cambridgeinternational.org` 已加入环境配置草稿；保存草稿不会立即改变运行中的网络访问。重试仍为 403。未请求用户提供密钥，也未关闭证书或校验。

2026 年 October/November 不在已取得资料包中，未核实发布情况，不能列为已覆盖。本轮没有启用后台定时任务：平台没有可用的调度接口，GitHub API 请求也被拒绝；按用户后续指令，在任务一完成后立即执行了本轮任务二工作。

## 可重现来源

原资料包：`dropbox_downloads/Chemistry_Qualitative_Practicals_2001-2026.zip`（Git LFS，130824313 字节）。

SHA-256：`4b2e3266cc666b68ee1aa092fb07ea7edd68b72c5d3b0a45f92e35251dab678c`。

`tools/prepare_past_papers.py` 验证资料包，复制原始 PDF，生成保留原版表格及化学式的图像和 `src/papers/archive-data.ts`。清单保留原始来源 URL、页码和每份 PDF 的 SHA-256。不会用关键词出现次数推断样品成分，也不会合成缺失题目或评分标准。

```sh
git lfs install --local
git lfs pull --include=dropbox_downloads/Chemistry_Qualitative_Practicals_2001-2026.zip
python3 -m venv ../.chemistry-paper-tools
. ../.chemistry-paper-tools/bin/activate
python -m pip install -r tools/requirements.txt
npm ci
npm run prepare:papers
npm run check:papers
npm test
npm run test:browser
npm run build
```

生成的 `public/past-papers/` 约 104 MiB，受 Git 忽略，必须由脚本生成；构建前会检查资料。重复生成已验证。构建输出自带资料，读取已导入原题不依赖外网。Chromium 测试可用 `CHROMIUM_PATH` 指定系统浏览器；否则安装 Playwright 浏览器（`npx playwright install chromium`）。

# 使用问题复现与修复记录（任务一）

基线提交：`22f9712`。以下是 23 个分别可复现的使用问题；部分问题共享状态管理或温度传递修复，并非声称有 23 个独立根因。没有把原本通过的防回归检查计作已发现问题。

## 第一阶段：问题清单及验证

| 编号 | 原版复现方式与影响 | 修复后的行为 |
|---|---|---|
| R01 | 点击拿起焰色反应丝，再长按溶液管；只定位到管内，无法蘸取溶液 | 区分固体和液体，长按也能蘸液 |
| R02 | 点击拿起试剂瓶、长按试管倒液，再滑走；瓶不跟手 | 滑动转为拖动，立即停止原目标倒液 |
| R03 | 将点击拿起的吸管放到管口，触发取消/窗口失焦；仍自行吸气 | 取消时停止工具操作并放回 |
| R04 | 火柴在停留延时过程中到期；仍可在删除前点燃灯 | 先处理过期，再处理点火 |
| R05 | 给放有滴管的管塞上塞子；滴管仍留在管上 | 塞管前取走滴管 |
| R06 | 在漏斗中停放滴管，再取走漏斗；滴管仍关联旧接收管 | 移走漏斗时解除滴管及其记录 |
| R07 | 在漏斗中停放滴管，再拿起其接收管；滴管悬空仍关联旧管 | 拿起接收管时一并解除滴管 |
| R08 | 加热时对同管执行另一项操作；加热继续但后续没有有效记录 | 加热和添加操作可并行记录，并检查记录有效性 |
| R09 | 将干固体倒入漏斗后放回；固体转移但操作记录被当作零体积取消 | 按质量记录固体过滤 |
| R10 | 持续倒液时另一操作结束了原记录；后续化学物质继续加入却无有效记录 | 重新开启倒液记录 |
| R11 | 过滤后返回值显示滤液和残渣，笔记本却显示“无明显变化” | 返回结果和笔记本内容一致 |
| R12 | 正在冒泡的管倒空；空管仍显示旧气泡 | 倒空时清除旧气泡状态 |
| R13 | 反应记录等待沉淀时立即冲洗；记录丢失或描述已冲洗的内容 | 清空内容前完成原操作记录 |
| R14 | 100°C 的 2 cm³ 溶液加入 20°C 的 2 cm³ 水；仍为 100°C | 使用体积加权温度（近似 60°C） |
| R15 | 将热溶液倒入干净试管；溶液立刻变成 20°C | 转移携带源液温度 |
| R16 | 从同管反复吸取氨气；可凭空收集超过原有氨量 | 溶解气体采样扣除相应物质且受剩余量限制 |
| R17 | 操作已取消/被其他操作结束后仍向旧句柄倒液；物质进入且无日志 | 失效操作不能再添加物质 |
| R18 | 已开始滴加后目标被封口；旧操作仍可向封口管滴入 | 引擎也验证接收管是否封口 |
| R19 | 本地答案为合法 JSON `null` 或错误类型；打开试题白屏 | 校验存储类型和答案字段，安全恢复默认值 |
| R20 | 已给上一轮打分，再从首页进入并重新提交；继承旧分数 | 每次提交重新开始评分 |
| R21 | 答案输入多行；评分页折叠为一行 | 保留换行和长文本换行 |
| R22 | 只用键盘访问实验台；所有器材均不能聚焦/操作 | 器材提供名称、焦点、Enter/空格操作及 Escape 取消 |
| R23 | 持有器材时打开并关闭演示；关闭事件穿透使器材被放回 | 演示关闭不穿透到实验台 |

验证命令：

```sh
npm test
npm run test:browser
npm run typecheck
npm run build
```

原版的 18 项引擎/模型复现测试在 `/tmp` 中通过原始提交的源文件运行，18 项全部失败；5 项浏览器问题也已在修改对应 UI 前运行失败。修复后第一阶段为 182 项单元测试、6 项浏览器检查通过（R24 小屏布局检查原本通过，因此没有算进问题数）。类型检查和生产构建通过后才开始第二阶段手机优化。

温度混合是半定量模型，不声称包含混合热、玻璃热容或精确量热。气体采样也是有限物质量近似。

## 第二阶段：手机端操作

- 手势画面通过 `requestAnimationFrame` 合并刷新，不再等化学时钟每 100ms 更新才移动。
- 实验台统一处理触摸：器材拖动不会被浏览器误判为滚动；空白处拖动可平移视图。
- 同时只接受一根主手指，正确处理触摸取消、失去捕获和窗口失焦。
- 靠近边缘并停住时仍持续滚动，并在滚动后重新计算器材位置；横向和纵向均可操作。
- 增加试剂、试管、加热、工具、清洗区域导航，以及缩放、全景和放回。
- 触屏默认放大器材并扩大命中区域；工具栏、评分和主要触摸按钮至少 44px。
- 输入字号至少 16px，避免 iPhone 输入时自动缩放；试题表格与字段适应窄屏。
- 切到试卷/笔记时释放手中器材；页面进入后台时暂停化学时钟，防止隐藏页面继续误操作。
- 试卷、笔记和静态场景减少重复渲染；支持减少动画偏好。

浏览器测试使用真实 Chromium 触摸事件，覆盖建管、点击倒液、放回、边缘拖动、取消、多指干扰、分页输入保持、320–430px 手机宽度、横屏和 iPad 尺寸。桌面检查保留。没有声称已在实体 iPhone 或 Safari 上测试。

## 任务二补充发现

新增 AS Q2 的原评分标准回归发现：锰酸根在浓水提取液中被模型设为半秒歧化，导致应该观察到绿色时立即变紫。已区分浓、稀、中性、酸性及强碱条件；同时保留原 `9701_w24_34` 稀水提取液呈紫色的检查。时间和浓度阈值用于半定量模拟并注明来源观察窗口，不声称精确动力学常数。新增的强酸反应测试验证锰酸根确实转化为高锰酸根和二氧化锰。

# 下一轮使用优化分析

审查日期：2026-10-24（北京时间）。应用基线：`d3839537f613009aa5b58a0fa120cc5b1ba00440`。

结论：下一轮优先保护学习进度，并让手机用户清楚知道“手里拿着什么、为什么没有操作成功、如何放回”。随后改善选卷和原卷阅读。当前没有性能测量证明需要重写渲染或引入新的动画框架。

本轮产出是分析与复现记录，以下建议尚未实施。上一轮的 236 项单元测试和 18 项浏览器检查属于既有验证；本轮没有将诊断探查算作新增通过的回归测试。

## 实际复现与优先级

桌面使用 Chromium、1280×900；手机使用 Chromium 触摸模拟、390×844。浏览器探查记录见 [usability-evidence-2026-10-24.json](usability-evidence-2026-10-24.json)。没有在实体 iPhone、Safari 或 Android 设备上验证。

| 编号 | 优先级 | 学生会遇到什么 | 本轮证据 | 建议改动与验收 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| U01 | P0 | 刷新或浏览器返回后，不能继续原实验；评分页也不能直接恢复 | 实际创建第二支试管并倒入 0.2 cm³ 后刷新，回到首页；重进后只剩初始样品管、日志为 0，书面答案还在。评分从 `1 / 14` 刷新后回首页，再次提交变成 `0 / 14`。实验页和首页 URL 相同，浏览器返回越过应用内部页面 | 保存完整练习尝试，并提供“继续练习”“查看上次评分”和明确的“重新开始”。刷新后保留试管内容、位置、日志、答案、笔记、评分和当前页面；新一次提交仍必须与恢复旧评分区分 |
| U02 | P0 | 保存失败时继续作答，误以为答案已经留在设备上 | 模拟 `QuotaExceededError` 后输入答案：没有错误提示；刷新重进后答案为空。`storage.save()` 吞掉错误且没有返回成功状态 | 显示“已保存 / 未能保存”；失败时保留内存中的答案，提供下载备份，不能显示成功。恢复写入能力后可重新保存；不要无限重试或逐帧写存储 |
| U03 | P1 | 拿起器材后，不容易找到“放回” | 390px 手机中，“放回”按钮左边在 x=525、宽 87px，初始视口完全看不到；它排在导航和缩放按钮之后 | 把当前器材名称和“放回”放在固定可见的位置，将区域导航保留在可滚动行。320–430px 宽度都无需先横滑工具栏才能放回 |
| U04 | P1 | 向塞住的样品管加液失败，却不知道是哪里不对，还可能换拿了样品管 | 点击拿起 NaOH 瓶，再点击有塞子的 solid E：手持物从 `bottle` 变为 `tube`，塞子仍在，没有 notice。`tapZone()` 返回 null，随后进入通用换拿分支 | 对明确被塞子阻挡的加液给出“先取下塞子”的操作提示，保留手持瓶。保留用户主动换拿其他器材的路径。提示只说明操作条件，不提前透露未知物身份或化学答案 |
| U05 | P1 | 用卷面上的普通卷号找不到题 | `0620/52` 和 `9701/31` 都返回 0；内部格式 `0620_s26_52` 返回 1 | 支持课程/卷号、年份和考季组合；规范化斜杠、空格及常见考季写法。不能把同一卷别的不同年份合并成一份题 |
| U06 | P1 | 看完一份题回选卷页，必须重新筛选和搜索 | 选 2026 年并搜索卷号，进入后返回，年份变为 all，搜索为空，重新显示 67 张卡片 | 保留筛选、搜索和列表滚动位置；区分“返回列表”和“清除筛选”。零结果时提供直接清除条件的入口 |
| U07 | P1 | 手机阅读原卷时放大幅度太大，无法逐级缩小，也缺少页码跳转 | 原卷容器 360px；点一次 + 后横向内容变为 960px；没有针对原卷的缩小按钮，只有 Fit。当前需要在内外两个滚动区域间移动 | 采用基于当前显示宽度的分级缩放，增加 −、明确倍率和页码跳转；切到答案后返回时保留阅读位置。先验证长表格和公式，不用未经核对的 OCR 重写原题 |
| U08 | P1 | 自评分的 `0 / 14` 无法直接说明“尚未评分”还是“确实得零分” | 代码检查：总分把未评分项按 0 计算，页面没有已评项数；新收录的非引导题只有整题作答和整题评分 | 保留零分，但另显示“已评 x/y 项”和未评项定位。原题小问评分需逐份核对题号与分值后再拆分，不应从 PDF 的任意数字猜出评分项 |
| U09 | P2 | 键盘能拿器材、建管，但还无法完成全部实验 | DOM 有 33 个器材按钮；键盘处理只覆盖 `hitRects()`，火焰、废液区、清洗区和空架位置没有对应操作目标，且没有方向键移动路径 | 补齐目标选择、移动或等价的可访问操作路径。必须实际只用键盘完成“建管→加液→加热→检验→倒空→冲洗”，不能用单个建管测试代表全流程可用 |
| U10 | P2 | 纯书面练习也持续运行空实验模型 | 没有实验台的 source-only 页面，在 1.1 秒内模型 version 增加 11；代码每 100ms 调用空 Lab 并刷新状态 | source-only 不创建/推进化学模型；用低频时钟更新作答用时。这个多余更新已确认，但尚未测量其电耗或帧率影响，不应称它是当前主要卡顿原因 |

P0 表示实际数据丢失或无法恢复学习进度；P1 表示高频操作和任务理解；P2 表示完整操作能力及后续效率。该排序不是用问题数量代替影响判断。

## 建议的实施顺序

### 第一批：保存反馈和手机操作闭环

范围：U02、U03、U04，并补 U05 的常用卷号检索。

- 答案与笔记有可信的保存状态；写入失败仍能导出当前内容。
- “持有的器材 + 放回”始终可见，不依赖横向工具栏自动滚动。
- 塞住目标等明确阻挡条件有即时提示；取消、换拿、拖动和连续倒液仍各自工作。
- `0620/52` 能找到对应各年份卷别；检索与课程、年份筛选可组合。

这些改动相对独立，预期改动成本小到中等。必须保持考试模式的观察文字开关，不把操作帮助变成答案提示。

### 第二批：可恢复的完整练习尝试

范围：U01、U06，以及 U08 的评分进度。

这批改动成本较高，不能简单对 `BenchModel` 做 `JSON.stringify()`。需要考虑：

- 按尝试保存，而非所有进度都仅按卷号存放；保留旧尝试的评分，新尝试独立计分。
- 试管成分、体积、温度、气体、沉降、塞子、位置、实验时间、日志、样品对应关系均可恢复。
- 恢复持久对象，不恢复正在按住的手指、pointer capture 或倒液手势；恢复前明确进入暂停/继续状态，避免补算离线反应。
- 正在等待观察的操作要有明确的保存规则，不能丢失已加的物质，或在恢复后重复加入。
- 为快照设版本和题目配置标识；旧配置不兼容时仍可查看或导出书面记录，不能静默用新样品替换旧状态。
- 合并频繁保存，优先在有效操作结束、输入改变及切到后台时处理；不能每个拖动动画帧都写存储。
- 把应用页面与浏览器返回/前进对应起来；恢复选卷筛选及滚动位置。

核心验收场景：完成一段实验并写答案，刷新、关页重开、前进后退、进入评分页后再恢复，都不会丢失或重复化学物质，也不会清掉已有评分。另测存储不可用、快照损坏和版本不兼容的降级行为。

### 第三批：读题、作答、评分之间少来回翻

范围：U07、U08 的逐小问结构、U09、U10。

- 手机按页定位原题，倍率连续可控，题目与答案各自保持阅读位置。
- 平板和桌面优先并排对照，手机采用清楚的视图切换；评分材料仍在提交后出现。
- 先核对原题小问与分值，再增加分项作答和分项评分，不扩大未经验证的真题覆盖声明。
- 完成键盘全流程验证，最后再用实机数据决定进一步性能优化。

## 本轮验证边界

本轮已用浏览器复现刷新后的实验/评分状态、存储写入失败、常用卷号检索、筛选丢失、手机按钮位置、原卷缩放、封口目标换拿及内部历史缺失；键盘路径和评分进度结合代码与 DOM 检查。证据 JSON 中的输入是专门生成的探查文本，不是用户真实答案。

尚未开展学生试用、实体手机测试、屏幕阅读器全流程或 FPS/电耗基准。源码和既有测试未在本轮修改；后续实施时应先为对应行为补回归验证，再提交并推送。

# User guide

**English** · [简体中文](user-guide.zh-CN.md) · [Back to README](../README.md)

This guide is for students and teachers using Qualitative Analysis Lab to practise the qualitative-analysis question of Cambridge IGCSE 0620 Paper 5 and AS & A Level 9701 Paper 3.

## Contents

1. [Opening the app](#1-opening-the-app)
2. [Choosing a question](#2-choosing-a-question)
3. [The exam screen](#3-the-exam-screen)
4. [Working on the bench](#4-working-on-the-bench)
5. [Writing your answers](#5-writing-your-answers)
6. [Finishing and self-marking](#6-finishing-and-self-marking)
7. [Good practice — and what happens if you don't follow it](#7-good-practice--and-what-happens-if-you-dont-follow-it)
8. [Using a phone or iPad](#8-using-a-phone-or-ipad)
9. [Where your work is saved](#9-where-your-work-is-saved)
10. [FAQ](#10-faq)

## 1. Opening the app

Someone who has the repository runs:

```bash
npm install
npm run dev -- --host
```

- On the same computer, open <http://localhost:5173>.
- On a phone or iPad on the same Wi-Fi, open the `Network:` address the terminal prints (for example `http://192.168.1.20:5173`).

Any modern browser works (Chrome, Edge, Safari, Firefox).

## 2. Choosing a question

The home page lists the questions in two groups:

- **Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620 · Paper 5 Practical Test**
- **Cambridge International AS & A Level Chemistry 9701 · Paper 3**

Each card shows the exam series, the total marks and a neutral description of the unknowns (it never gives the answer away). Tap a card to start.

## 3. The exam screen

| Area | What it is |
|---|---|
| **Top bar** | ← back to the question list · paper and question number · ⏱ time spent · **?** replay the demonstration · 🔊 sound on/off · *Observation text* switch · ⇥ hide/show the question paper (computer) · **Finish** |
| **Bench** | A laboratory bench drawn as it would be in the exam room (see below) |
| **Question paper / Lab notebook** | The question with answer spaces, and a log of everything you have done plus a box for rough notes |

The first time you open a question, a ghost hand shows the main movements: taking a tube, using a dropper, turning on the gas, lighting the burner and heating a tube. Tap anywhere to skip it; **?** shows it again.

### What is on the bench

| Where | Apparatus |
|---|---|
| Shelf | The reagent bottles for this question (as in the confidential instructions), each with its own dropper; limewater; solid reagents in jars; magnesium ribbon or aluminium foil in a dish |
| Test-tube rack | The **samples** (e.g. *solid E*, *FA 7*, usually stoppered) stand in the rack with your other tubes — they can be used like any other tube |
| Middle of the bench | Bunsen burner with gas tap and air-hole collar, box of matches, filter funnel on its stand, wash bottle of distilled water, waste beaker |
| Front tray | Boxes of clean test-tubes, boiling tubes and hard-glass test-tubes; stoppers; spatula; red litmus, blue litmus and universal indicator papers (with colour chart); splints; teat pipettes; flame-test wire; white tile |
| Wall | Clock showing how much time has passed on the bench |
| Front right | Sink (for rinsing tubes) |

**Time runs on the bench.** Precipitates settle, bubbling dies down, hot tubes cool, iron(II) hydroxide slowly turns brown at the surface — look again at a tube after a while, just as in a real lab. Tap the **clock** to make time run ten times faster (the rim turns orange); tap it again to go back to normal speed. Use it when the question says "leave to stand" or while you wait for something to heat up.

### The *Observation text* switch

Off by default. In the real exam nobody tells you what you see, so normally you only **see** the apparatus: colours, precipitates, bubbles, gases, condensation, the colour of a test paper, a coloured flame. Turn the switch on to also show a short written description of each result — useful while you learn the vocabulary, or when a colour is hard to judge on your screen.

## 4. Working on the bench

Everything is done the way you would do it with your hands: **pick something up and bring it to something else.** There are no menus or buttons.

There are two ways to move things, and you can mix them:

- **Drag** — press on an item, move it, let go. Best with a mouse and on an iPad.
- **Tap, then tap** — tap an item to lift it (it floats and bobs slightly), then tap where it should go. To keep doing something (pouring, holding a strip at a tube), **press and hold** on the target. Tap an empty part of the bench to put the item back. Handy on a phone.

While you hold something, the place where it would act is outlined with a moving dashed line.

### Tubes

- **A clean tube**: tap a box on the tray (it goes into the next free place in the rack), or drag a tube from the box to the rack.
- **Move a tube**: drag it to another place in the rack.
- **Stoppers**: drag a stopper from the box onto a tube; drag the stopper off to remove it. Nothing can be added to, poured from or tested in a stoppered tube.
- **Shake**: while holding a tube, move it quickly from side to side.
- **Empty**: drag the tube onto the **waste beaker** (a wet film stays behind). **Rinse**: drag it onto the **sink**. **Throw away**: drag it back into its box (samples cannot be thrown away).

### Adding liquids

- **Pour**: hold a reagent bottle (or a tube, or the wash bottle) over the mouth of a tube. It tips and pours **for as long as you keep it there**; move away or let go to stop. Yellow marks beside the tube show the depth in centimetres, so you can judge "1 cm depth" or "2 cm depth" as in the exam.
- **Drops**: take the **dropper** out of a bottle (press on its red teat) and put it into a tube — it rests there. **Each tap on the dropper gives one drop**; press and hold for a steady stream of drops. Take the dropper away (drag it anywhere) when you have finished — "dropwise, then in excess" means drops first, then pour from the bottle.
- **Distilled water**: pour from the wash bottle the same way.
- **Limewater**: pour it into a clean tube first, then bubble gas into it (see below).

### Adding solids

- **Spatula**: bring the spatula to a tube containing a dry solid (or to a jar) to take a measure; bring it again to take more. Then bring it to another tube to tip the solid in.
- **Magnesium ribbon / aluminium foil**: drag a piece from the dish into the tube.
- **Tipping out a solid**: hold a tube containing a solid over another tube, as for pouring.

### Heating

1. Tap the **gas tap** to turn the gas on (you hear a hiss).
2. Drag a **match** from the box to the top of the burner — it lights. Matches burn out after a few seconds.
3. Tap the **air-hole collar** to switch between a roaring blue flame (air hole open, hot) and a yellow flame (closed, cooler).
4. Drag a tube onto the burner: it is held in a test-tube holder above the flame.
   - **Higher position** (bottom of the tube at the tip of the flame) — warming gently / heating a solid gently.
   - **Lower position** (in the blue flame) — heating to boiling / heating a solid strongly.
5. Drag the tube away to stop heating. Tap the gas tap again to turn the burner off.

Watch for melting, colour changes (some solids look different while hot), condensation near the mouth of the tube, bubbling, coloured gases and the liquid boiling away. Strong heating of a solid is best done in a **hard-glass test-tube**. A stoppered tube must not be heated — its stopper blows out.

### Testing gases

Gases are only there while they are being given off (or for a short time after warming), so test **straight away**.

- **Litmus / universal indicator paper**: take a strip from its booklet. To make it **damp**, hold it at the nozzle of the wash bottle for a moment. Then hold it at the **mouth of the tube** until it changes. A dry strip does not respond to gases. (Dipping the strip into the liquid tests the **solution** instead — not the gas.) Used strips are kept on the white tile so you can look at them again.
- **Splints**: take a splint from the box and hold it in the flame to light it. Hold the **lighted splint** at the mouth of the tube. To get a **glowing splint**, wave the burning splint quickly from side to side (or tap it while it is lifted).
- **Limewater test**: take a **teat pipette**, hold it at the mouth of the reacting tube to draw in the gas, then put its tip into a tube of limewater to bubble the gas through.

### Other tests

- **Flame test**: take the flame-test wire, dip its loop into the solid or solution, then hold it in the **blue** flame. The flame takes the colour for a few seconds; leaving the wire in the flame cleans it.
- **pH**: dip a strip of universal indicator paper into the solution and compare it with the colour chart on the booklet.
- **Filtering**: drag the funnel from its stand onto a tube in the rack, then pour the mixture into the funnel. The filtrate runs into the tube; the residue stays on the filter paper. You can pour other reagents through the residue in the same way. Drag the funnel back to its stand to take it off, or into the waste beaker to throw the paper away.

## 5. Writing your answers

Open **Question paper** (desktop: tab on the right; phone: **📄 Paper** at the bottom). The paper follows the original layout: introduction, then each part with its marks and an answer space — lines, a table of tests and observations, or labelled fields.

- Write in the style of the mark scheme: name the colour **and** the state (e.g. "white precipitate, soluble in excess giving a colourless solution"), "effervescence", "damp red litmus turns blue", etc.
- Record what you **saw**, not what you expected. If you made a mistake on the bench, redo the test in a clean tube.
- **Lab notebook** lists every action with its time and tube. Use the *Rough notes* box for anything else.

Answers are saved automatically as you type.

## 6. Finishing and self-marking

Tap **Finish** (top bar or bottom of the paper) and confirm. The self-marking page shows, for every part:

- the question (tap *Question* to expand),
- **Your answer**,
- the published **Mark scheme**,
- **Supervisor's results (simulation)** — what this simulation produces when the instructions are followed exactly, like the supervisor's report sent with real scripts,
- a row of mark buttons (0 … maximum) to award yourself marks.

Your total is shown at the top right. At the bottom you can see **what the unknowns were** (from the confidential instructions) and **everything you did in the lab**, with the observations for each step — compare it with what you wrote.

Then choose **Try this question again** (clears your answers, notes and marks for this question and gives you a fresh bench) or **Choose another question**.

Mark honestly: give a mark only if your answer contains the point in the mark scheme. If the supervisor's results differ from your observations, look in your lab log for the step where your procedure differed.

## 7. Good practice — and what happens if you don't follow it

The engine only knows what is in each tube, so the classic mistakes give the results they would give in a real lab:

| If you… | You will see… |
|---|---|
| acidify with dilute **HCl** before adding AgNO₃ | a white precipitate (AgCl) even when the sample has no chloride — use dilute **nitric acid** |
| forget to acidify before adding Ba²⁺ or Ag⁺ to a carbonate | a white precipitate with barium nitrate (a false "sulfate"), a precipitate with silver nitrate |
| re-use a tube without rinsing | traces of the previous test, e.g. chloride clouding AgNO₃ |
| use tap water instead of distilled water | slight cloudiness with silver nitrate |
| add NaOH to an acidified solution | the first drops only neutralise the acid; the precipitate appears later |
| pour in a large excess of NaOH at once (e.g. to Al³⁺ or Zn²⁺) | the precipitate is seen only for a moment, or not at all — add **dropwise** |
| test for ammonia after the tube has gone cold | no change — ammonia was driven off while warm; test while warming |
| leave H₂O₂ in the tube and then add acidified KMnO₄ | the KMnO₄ is decolourised, with effervescence (oxygen) |
| test a gas long after the reaction has stopped | no result — the gas has escaped |
| hold a **dry** litmus paper at the mouth of the tube | no change — dampen it first |
| dip red litmus into the alkaline mixture instead of holding it at the mouth | it turns blue whether or not ammonia is given off — the test is for the **gas** |
| try to add something to a stoppered sample | nothing goes in — take the stopper out |
| heat a stoppered tube | the stopper blows out |
| put a tube on the burner without lighting it, or use the yellow flame | little or no heating |
| overfill a tube | it overflows |

Other tips: use about 1–2 cm depth of solution for a test; take a **new tube for each test** unless the question says to add to the same tube; follow the order of reagents in the question; leave mixtures to stand when asked.

## 8. Using a phone or iPad

- **Phone** (and other narrow windows): use the tabs at the bottom — **🧪 Lab**, **📄 Paper**, **📓 Notebook**. The bench fills the height of the screen and **scrolls sideways**: swipe on an empty part of the bench to see the rest. Dragging an item to the edge scrolls the bench along. *Tap, then tap* is often easier than dragging on a small screen.
- **iPad portrait**: bench at the top, question paper below, so you can work and write without switching.
- **iPad landscape / computer**: bench on the left, question paper and lab notebook on the right. Use **⇥** in the top bar to hide the paper and make the bench larger.

For long answers on an iPad, a hardware keyboard helps but is not required.

## 9. Where your work is saved

Answers, rough notes, self-marks and settings (observation text, sound, whether you have seen the demonstration) are stored **in this browser on this device** (local storage). They are not sent anywhere.

- They survive closing the tab or reloading — but the **bench** does not: leaving the question and coming back gives a fresh set of samples.
- A private / incognito window, clearing site data, or a different browser or device starts empty.
- *Try this question again* deliberately clears the answers, notes and marks for that question.

## 10. FAQ

**The colour on my screen is hard to judge.** Screens differ. Turn on *Observation text* to read the description, then try to recognise it next time.

**Nothing happened when I tested the gas.** Gases are only there while they are being produced (or for a short while after warming). Repeat the step and test straight away; for ammonia, warm the mixture first. Litmus and indicator papers must be damp, and the tube must not be stoppered.

**Nothing happens when I bring something to a tube.** Check the dashed outline: it shows where the item would act. Stoppered tubes accept nothing but having their stopper removed. If you are holding something else, put it down first (tap an empty part of the bench).

**I want to undo something.** As in a real lab, you cannot. Empty the tube into the waste beaker, rinse it at the sink, and do the test again in a clean tube.

**Waiting takes too long.** Tap the clock on the wall to make time run ten times faster.

**Why does my result differ from the supervisor's results?** Usually because of the amounts used, the order of addition, a tube that was not rinsed, or time — the supervisor follows the question exactly. Compare your lab log with the question.

**Is my answer marked automatically?** No. You mark yourself against the mark scheme.

**Can I get back the same bench after leaving?** No. Your written answers are kept, but the bench always starts fresh.

===== END FILE =====

# 用户指南

[English](user-guide.md) · **简体中文** · [返回 README](../README.zh-CN.md)

本指南面向使用 Qualitative Analysis Lab 练习 Cambridge IGCSE 0620 Paper 5 及 AS & A Level 9701 Paper 3 定性分析题的学生和老师。程序界面为英文，下文在中文说明后用括号标注界面上的英文名称。

## 目录

1. [打开程序](#1-打开程序)
2. [选择题目](#2-选择题目)
3. [考试界面](#3-考试界面)
4. [在实验台上操作](#4-在实验台上操作)
5. [填写答案](#5-填写答案)
6. [交卷与自我评分](#6-交卷与自我评分)
7. [规范操作——以及不规范会怎样](#7-规范操作以及不规范会怎样)
8. [在手机或 iPad 上使用](#8-在手机或-ipad-上使用)
9. [作答保存在哪里](#9-作答保存在哪里)
10. [常见问题](#10-常见问题)

## 1. 打开程序

由持有仓库的人执行：

```bash
npm install
npm run dev -- --host
```

- 在同一台电脑上打开 <http://localhost:5173>。
- 在同一 Wi-Fi 下的手机或 iPad 上，打开终端显示的 `Network:` 地址（例如 `http://192.168.1.20:5173`）。

任何现代浏览器均可（Chrome、Edge、Safari、Firefox）。

## 2. 选择题目

首页把题目分为两组：

- **Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620 · Paper 5 Practical Test**
- **Cambridge International AS & A Level Chemistry 9701 · Paper 3**

每张卡片显示考试季、总分和对未知物的中性描述（不会透露答案）。点击卡片即可开始。

## 3. 考试界面

| 区域 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| **顶栏** | ← 返回题目列表 · 试卷及题号 · ⏱ 已用时间 · **?** 重播示范 · 🔊 声音开关 · 观察文字开关（*Observation text*）· ⇥ 隐藏／显示试卷（电脑）· 交卷（**Finish**） |
| **实验台** | 按考场实验台样子绘制的工作台（见下文） |
| **试卷／实验笔记**（Question paper / Lab notebook） | 带答题位置的题目；以及所有操作的记录和草稿栏 |

第一次打开题目时，会有一只"虚拟的手"示范主要动作：取试管、用滴管、开燃气、点燃本生灯、加热试管。点击任意位置可跳过；按 **?** 可再看一次。

### 实验台上有什么

| 位置 | 仪器 |
|---|---|
| 试剂架 | 本题的试剂瓶（与机密说明一致），每瓶都配有自己的滴管；石灰水；装在广口瓶里的固体试剂；放在培养皿里的镁带或铝箔 |
| 试管架 | **样品**（如 *solid E*、*FA 7*，通常塞着塞子）和其他试管一起放在试管架上——可以像普通试管一样使用 |
| 台面中间 | 本生灯（含燃气阀和空气孔调节环）、火柴盒、放在漏斗架上的漏斗、蒸馏水洗瓶、废液缸 |
| 前方托盘 | 干净的试管、沸腾管、硬质玻璃试管各一盒；塞子；药匙；红色石蕊试纸、蓝色石蕊试纸和通用指示剂试纸（附比色卡）；木条；滴管（胶头滴管）；焰色测试金属丝；白瓷板 |
| 墙上 | 显示实验台已过时间的时钟 |
| 右前方 | 水槽（用来冲洗试管） |

**实验台上的时间会持续流逝。** 沉淀会沉降、气泡逐渐停止、热试管会冷却、氢氧化亚铁表面会慢慢变棕——隔一会儿再看看试管，就像在真实实验室一样。点击**时钟**可让时间以十倍速度流逝（表圈变成橙色），再点一次恢复正常。题目要求"静置"或等待加热时可以使用。

### 观察文字开关（*Observation text*）

默认关闭。真实考试中没有人告诉你看到了什么，所以平时你只能**看到**仪器本身：颜色、沉淀、气泡、气体、冷凝水、试纸的颜色、火焰的颜色。打开开关后，每个结果会附带简短的文字描述——适合学习术语，或屏幕上颜色难以判断时使用。

## 4. 在实验台上操作

所有操作都和亲手做实验一样：**拿起一样东西，把它带到另一样东西那里。** 没有菜单，也没有按钮。

移动物件有两种方式，可以混用：

- **拖动**——按住物件，移动，松手。适合鼠标和 iPad。
- **点一下，再点一下**——点一下物件把它拿起（它会浮起并轻微上下浮动），再点一下要放到的位置。需要持续进行的动作（倾倒、把试纸放在试管口）则在目标上**按住不放**。点击实验台空白处即可放回物件。在手机上很方便。

拿着物件时，它会起作用的位置会以流动的虚线框标示。

### 试管

- **取干净试管**：点一下托盘上的试管盒（试管会放到试管架下一个空位），或把试管从盒子拖到试管架。
- **移动试管**：把它拖到试管架的另一个位置。
- **塞子**：把塞子从塞子盒拖到试管上；把塞子拖离即可取下。塞着塞子的试管不能加入、倒出或检验任何东西。
- **振荡**：拿着试管时左右快速晃动。
- **倒掉**：把试管拖到**废液缸**（管壁会留下液膜）。**冲洗**：拖到**水槽**。**丢弃**：拖回对应的试管盒（样品不能丢弃）。

### 加入液体

- **倾倒**：把试剂瓶（或试管、洗瓶）拿到试管口上方。它会倾斜，**一直放在那里就一直倒**；移开或松手即停止。试管旁的黄色刻度以厘米显示液面深度，方便像考试一样判断"1 cm 深"或"2 cm 深"。
- **逐滴**：从试剂瓶取出**滴管**（按住红色胶头），放进试管——滴管会停在那里。**每点一下滴管滴一滴**；按住不放则连续滴下。用完后把滴管拿走（拖到任意位置）。"逐滴加入，然后过量"即先逐滴，再从试剂瓶倾倒。
- **蒸馏水**：用洗瓶以同样方式倾倒。
- **石灰水**：先倒进一支干净试管，再把气体通入（见下文）。

### 加入固体

- **药匙**：把药匙带到装有干燥固体的试管（或广口瓶）取一匙；再带过去一次即多取一些。然后把药匙带到另一支试管，把固体倒进去。
- **镁带／铝箔**：从培养皿拖一片到试管。
- **倒出固体**：把装有固体的试管拿到另一支试管上方，与倾倒液体相同。

### 加热

1. 点一下**燃气阀**开气（会听到嘶嘶声）。
2. 从火柴盒拖一根**火柴**到本生灯顶部——灯就会点燃。火柴几秒后会燃尽。
3. 点一下**空气孔调节环**，在蓝色呼呼作响的火焰（空气孔打开，温度高）和黄色火焰（关闭，温度较低）之间切换。
4. 把试管拖到本生灯上：它会被试管夹夹住，悬在火焰上方。
   - **较高位置**（试管底部在火焰尖端）——温和加热液体／缓慢加热固体。
   - **较低位置**（在蓝色火焰中）——加热至沸腾／强热固体。
5. 把试管拖离即停止加热。再点一下燃气阀可关闭本生灯。

注意观察熔化、颜色变化（有些固体热时颜色不同）、试管口附近的冷凝水、气泡、有色气体，以及液体被蒸干。强热固体最好使用**硬质玻璃试管**。塞着塞子的试管不能加热——塞子会被冲出。

### 检验气体

气体只在生成期间（或加热后的短时间内）存在，所以要**立即**检验。

- **石蕊／通用指示剂试纸**：从试纸本取一条。要**润湿**试纸，把它放在洗瓶嘴处片刻。然后把它放在**试管口**，直到颜色变化。干燥的试纸对气体没有反应。（把试纸浸入液体检验的是**溶液**，而不是气体。）用过的试纸会放在白瓷板上，方便再看。
- **木条**：从木条盒取一根，放进火焰点燃。把**燃着的木条**放在试管口。要得到**带火星的木条**，把燃着的木条左右快速挥动（或在拿起状态下点一下它）。
- **石灰水检验**：取一支**胶头滴管**，放在反应试管口吸入气体，再把滴管尖端伸进装有石灰水的试管，把气体通入。

### 其他测试

- **焰色测试**：取焰色测试金属丝，把金属圈蘸上固体或溶液，再放进**蓝色**火焰。火焰会呈现颜色几秒；把金属丝留在火焰中即可烧干净。
- **pH**：把一条通用指示剂试纸浸入溶液，与试纸本上的比色卡对比。
- **过滤**：把漏斗从漏斗架拖到试管架上的一支试管上，再把混合物倒进漏斗。滤液流进试管，滤渣留在滤纸上。也可以用同样方法把其他试剂倒过滤渣。把漏斗拖回漏斗架即可取下，拖进废液缸则丢弃滤纸。

## 5. 填写答案

打开**试卷**（电脑：右侧的 *Question paper* 标签；手机：底部的 **📄 Paper**）。试卷沿用原题版式：引言，然后是各小题、分值及答题位置——答题线、测试与观察表格，或带标签的填空栏。

- 按评分标准的写法作答：同时写出颜色**和**状态（例如 "white precipitate, soluble in excess giving a colourless solution"），以及 "effervescence"、"damp red litmus turns blue" 等。
- 记录你**看到**的现象，而不是你预期的现象。如果在实验台上操作出错，用一支干净的试管重做。
- **实验笔记**（Lab notebook）按时间和试管列出每一步操作；其他内容可写在 *Rough notes*（草稿）栏。

答案会在输入时自动保存。

## 6. 交卷与自我评分

点击 **Finish**（顶栏或试卷底部）并确认。自我评分页面会为每一小题显示：

- 题目（点击 *Question* 展开）；
- **你的答案**（Your answer）；
- 官方**评分标准**（Mark scheme）；
- **监考员结果（模拟）**（Supervisor's results (simulation)）——严格按题目步骤操作时本模拟产生的结果，相当于真实考试随答卷寄出的监考员报告；
- 一排分数按钮（0 至满分），用来给自己打分。

总分显示在右上角。页面底部可查看**未知物是什么**（来自机密说明），以及**你在实验室中的全部操作**及每一步的观察——可与自己写的答案对照。

之后可选择 **Try this question again**（清除本题的答案、笔记和分数，并重新开始一个新的实验台）或 **Choose another question**（选择其他题目）。

请诚实评分：只有当你的答案包含评分标准中的要点时才给分。如果监考员结果与你的观察不同，请在操作记录中找出你的步骤与题目不同之处。

## 7. 规范操作——以及不规范会怎样

引擎只知道每支试管里有什么，所以典型的错误操作会得到与真实实验室相同的结果：

| 如果你…… | 会看到…… |
|---|---|
| 加 AgNO₃ 前用稀 **HCl** 酸化 | 即使样品不含氯离子也出现白色沉淀（AgCl）——应使用稀**硝酸** |
| 对碳酸盐加 Ba²⁺ 或 Ag⁺ 前忘记酸化 | 加硝酸钡出现白色沉淀（误判为"硫酸根"），加硝酸银出现沉淀 |
| 重用未清洗的试管 | 上一次测试的残留，例如氯离子令 AgNO₃ 变浑浊 |
| 用自来水代替蒸馏水 | 加硝酸银时略显浑浊 |
| 向已酸化的溶液加 NaOH | 最初几滴只是中和酸，沉淀稍后才出现 |
| 一次倒入大量过量 NaOH（例如对 Al³⁺ 或 Zn²⁺） | 沉淀只出现一瞬间，甚至看不到——应**逐滴**加入 |
| 试管冷却后才检验氨气 | 没有变化——氨气在温热时已被赶走；应在加热时检验 |
| 试管中残留 H₂O₂ 后再加酸化 KMnO₄ | KMnO₄ 褪色，并伴有气泡（氧气） |
| 反应停止很久后才检验气体 | 没有结果——气体已经逸散 |
| 把**干燥**的石蕊试纸放在试管口 | 没有变化——要先润湿 |
| 把红色石蕊试纸浸入碱性混合物，而不是放在试管口 | 无论有没有放出氨气都会变蓝——这个测试检验的是**气体** |
| 想向塞着塞子的样品加东西 | 加不进去——先取下塞子 |
| 加热塞着塞子的试管 | 塞子被冲出 |
| 把试管放到未点燃的本生灯上，或使用黄色火焰 | 几乎不加热 |
| 试管装得太满 | 溢出 |

其他建议：每个测试用约 1–2 cm 深的溶液；除非题目要求加到同一支试管，否则**每个测试使用一支新试管**；按题目的试剂顺序操作；题目要求静置时要静置。

## 8. 在手机或 iPad 上使用

- **手机**（及其他较窄的窗口）：使用底部标签——**🧪 Lab**（实验台）、**📄 Paper**（试卷）、**📓 Notebook**（笔记）。实验台会占满屏幕高度并可**左右滚动**：在实验台空白处滑动即可查看其他部分；把物件拖到边缘时实验台会自动滚动。小屏幕上"点一下，再点一下"往往比拖动更方便。
- **iPad 竖屏**：上方是实验台，下方是试卷，无需切换即可边做实验边作答。
- **iPad 横屏／电脑**：左侧是实验台，右侧是试卷和实验笔记。按顶栏的 **⇥** 可隐藏试卷，让实验台更大。

在 iPad 上写较长答案时，外接键盘会更方便，但并非必需。

## 9. 作答保存在哪里

答案、草稿、自评分数及各项设置（观察文字、声音、是否已看过示范）保存在**本设备的这个浏览器**中（localStorage），不会发送到任何地方。

- 关闭标签页或刷新后仍会保留——但**实验台**不会保留：离开题目再回来会得到一套全新的样品。
- 使用无痕／隐私窗口、清除网站数据，或换用其他浏览器或设备时，内容为空。
- *Try this question again* 会刻意清除该题的答案、笔记和分数。

## 10. 常见问题

**屏幕上的颜色难以判断。** 不同屏幕显示有差异。打开 *Observation text* 阅读文字描述，下次再尝试辨认。

**检验气体时没有反应。** 气体只在生成期间（或加热后的短时间内）存在。重复该步骤并立即检验；检验氨气时要先加热混合物。石蕊和指示剂试纸必须是湿润的，试管也不能塞着塞子。

**把物件带到试管那里却没有反应。** 留意虚线框：它标示物件会起作用的位置。塞着塞子的试管只能取下塞子。如果手上拿着其他物件，请先放下（点击实验台空白处）。

**我想撤销操作。** 和真实实验室一样，不能撤销。把试管倒进废液缸，在水槽冲洗，再用干净的试管重做。

**等待时间太长。** 点击墙上的时钟，让时间以十倍速度流逝。

**为什么我的结果与监考员结果不同？** 通常是因为用量、加入顺序、未清洗试管或时间不同——监考员严格按题目操作。请对照题目检查你的操作记录。

**答案会自动改分吗？** 不会。你需要对照评分标准自行评分。

**离开后能回到原来的实验台吗？** 不能。写下的答案会保留，但实验台每次都会重新开始。

===== END FILE =====

  await expect(page.locator('.paper-card')).toHaveCount(2)
  await expect(page.locator('.paper-card').first()).toContainText('Question 2')
})

test('source-only paper supports original pages, answers and self marking without pretending to have a bench', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByLabel('Find paper', { exact: true }).fill('9701_s26_31')
  await page.locator('.paper-card').click()
  await expect(page.getByRole('application')).toHaveCount(0)
  await expect(page.locator('.paper-note')).toContainText('not yet available')
  await expect(page.locator('.paper-head')).toContainText('Question 1')
  const image = page.locator('.source-pages img').first()

  await page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Your answers to Question 1' }).fill('(a)(ii) Mn²⁺ and Cl⁻\n(b)(ii) Al₂(SO₄)₃')
  page.on('dialog', d => d.accept())
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Finish', exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.locator('.original-mark-scheme')).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.locator('.your-answer')).toContainText('Mn²⁺ and Cl⁻')
  await expect(page.locator('.score-pill')).toHaveText('0 / 15')
})
