test('all 201 original source PDFs are available locally with valid PDF headers', async ({ request }) => {
  // Use the generated metadata directly, including CI assets, rather than asserting a hardcoded URL.
  const { ARCHIVE_DATA } = await import('../src/papers/archive-data')
  for (const paper of ARCHIVE_DATA) {
    for (const asset of Object.values(paper.assets)) {
      const response = await request.get(`/${asset.pdf}`)
      expect(response.ok(), asset.pdf).toBe(true)
      expect((await response.body()).subarray(0, 5).toString()).toBe('%PDF-')
    }
  }
})

import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test'

test.use({ viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 }, isMobile: true, hasTouch: true })

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/')
  await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.setItem('qa-lab:demo-seen', 'true'))
  await page.locator('.paper-card').first().tap()
  await expect(page.getByRole('application')).toBeVisible()
})

test('touch workflow: create a tube, tap a bottle, pour and put it back', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'New test-tube', exact: true }).tap()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'bottle aqueous sodium hydroxide', exact: true }).tap()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'test-tube 1', exact: true }).tap()
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => {
    const m = (window as any).__bench
    return [...m.lab.vessels.values()].find((v: any) => v.label === 'test-tube 1').V
  })).toBeGreaterThan(0.15)
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Put back', exact: true }).tap()
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as any).__bench.held)).toBeNull()
})

test('phone navigation reaches both ends of the bench without page overflow', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Wash', exact: true }).tap()
  await expect.poll(() => page.locator('.bench-wrap').evaluate(el => el.scrollLeft)).toBeGreaterThan(400)
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reagents', exact: true }).tap()
  await expect.poll(() => page.locator('.bench-wrap').evaluate(el => el.scrollLeft)).toBeLessThan(200)
  for (const width of [320, 390, 430]) {
    await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 844 })
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth)).toBe(width)
  }
})

test('stationary edge drag keeps scrolling, and cancellation releases the item', async ({ page }) => {
  const rect = await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'bottle aqueous sodium hydroxide', exact: true }).boundingBox()
  const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page)
  const point = { x: rect!.x + rect!.width / 2, y: rect!.y + rect!.height / 2 }
  await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', { type: 'touchStart', touchPoints: [point] })
  await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', { type: 'touchMove', touchPoints: [{ x: 384, y: point.y + 100 }] })
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => (window as any).__bench.held?.dragging)).toBe(true)
  const initial = await page.locator('.bench-wrap').evaluate(el => el.scrollLeft)
  await expect.poll(() => page.locator('.bench-wrap').evaluate(el => el.scrollLeft)).toBeGreaterThan(initial + 60)
  await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', { type: 'touchCancel', touchPoints: [] })
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => (window as any).__bench.held)).toBeNull()
})

test('a second finger cannot replace the apparatus gesture', async ({ page }) => {
  const cdp = await page.context().newCDPSession(page)
  const rect = await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'bottle aqueous sodium hydroxide', exact: true }).boundingBox()
  const first = { id: 1, x: rect!.x + rect!.width / 2, y: rect!.y + rect!.height / 2 }
  await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', { type: 'touchStart', touchPoints: [first] })
  const moved = { ...first, x: first.x + 40, y: first.y + 50 }
  await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', { type: 'touchMove', touchPoints: [moved] })
  await expect.poll(() => page.evaluate(() => (window as any).__bench.held?.item.k)).toBe('bottle')
  await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', { type: 'touchStart', touchPoints: [moved, { id: 2, x: 250, y: 500 }] })
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as any).__bench.held?.item.k)).toBe('bottle')
  await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', { type: 'touchCancel', touchPoints: [] })
})

test('switching to the paper releases a lifted tool and retains typed answers', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tools', exact: true }).tap()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'wire', exact: true }).tap()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '📄 Paper', exact: true }).tap()
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as any).__bench.held)).toBeNull()
  const answer = page.locator('textarea.answer').first()
  expect(await answer.evaluate(el => parseFloat(getComputedStyle(el).fontSize))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(16)
  await answer.fill('Pale blue precipitate')
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '🧪 Lab', exact: true }).tap()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '📄 Paper', exact: true }).tap()
  await expect(answer).toHaveValue('Pale blue precipitate')
})

test('portrait, landscape and tablet layouts keep the paper and notebook reachable', async ({ page }) => {
  for (const size of [{ width: 844, height: 390 }, { width: 768, height: 1024 }, { width: 1024, height: 768 }]) {
    await page.setViewportSize(size)
    if (await page.locator('.bottom-tabs').isVisible()) {
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: '📄 Paper', exact: true }).tap()
    } else {
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Question paper', exact: true }).tap()
    }
    await expect(page.locator('textarea.answer').first()).toBeVisible()
    if (await page.locator('.bottom-tabs').isVisible()) {
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: '📓 Notebook', exact: true }).tap()
    } else {
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lab notebook', exact: true }).tap()
    }
    await expect(page.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Rough notes' })).toBeVisible()
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth)).toBe(size.width)
  }
})

import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test'

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/')
  await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.setItem('qa-lab:demo-seen', 'true'))

async function start(page: import('@playwright/test').Page) {
  await page.locator('.paper-card').first().click()
  await expect(page.getByRole('application')).toBeVisible()
}

test('R19 corrupted saved answers cannot crash the question paper', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.setItem('qa-lab:answers:0620_s24_51', 'null'))
  await start(page)
  await expect(page.locator('textarea.answer').first()).toBeVisible()
  await page.locator('textarea.answer').first().fill('White precipitate')
  await expect(page.locator('textarea.answer').first()).toHaveValue('White precipitate')
})

test('R20 a new submission does not inherit a previous submission score', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.setItem('qa-lab:marks:0620_s24_51', '{"b-i":2,"a":1,"b-ii":2}'))
  await start(page)
  page.on('dialog', d => d.accept())
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Finish', exact: true }).click()
  await expect(page.locator('.score-pill')).toHaveText('0 / 14')
})

test('R21 written line breaks remain visible during self marking', async ({ page }) => {
  await start(page)
  const input = page.locator('textarea.answer').first()
  await input.fill('White precipitate\nDissolves in excess')
  page.on('dialog', d => d.accept())
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Finish', exact: true }).click()
  const answer = page.locator('.ans-line span').filter({ hasText: 'White precipitate' }).last()
  expect(await answer.evaluate(el => getComputedStyle(el).whiteSpace)).toMatch(/pre/)
})

test('R22 apparatus can be operated using the keyboard', async ({ page }) => {
  await start(page)
  const box = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'New test-tube', exact: true })
  await box.focus()
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter')
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as any).__bench.standing().length)).toBe(2)
})

test('R23 dismissing the tutorial does not accidentally put back a held tool', async ({ page }) => {
  await start(page)
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    (window as any).__bench.held = { item: { k: 'wire', reading: null, colour: null }, x: 500, y: 500, dragging: false, ox: 0, oy: 0 }
  })
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Show how the bench works' }).click()
  await page.locator('.demo > rect').click({ position: { x: 300, y: 300 } })
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as any).__bench.held?.item.k)).toBe('wire')
})

test('R24 answer tables fit a 320px phone without horizontal clipping', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 320, height: 740 })
  await start(page)
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '📄 Paper', exact: true }).click()
  const dimensions = await page.locator('.side-body').evaluate(el => ({ client: el.clientWidth, scroll: el.scrollWidth }))
  expect(dimensions.scroll).toBeLessThanOrEqual(dimensions.client + 1)
})

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/__tests__/anions.test.ts", "bytes": 5359, "sha256": "6d34becabb749a4ff727ee7bf6cfb60a4789448dd5159e86efc481012b3e50d1"} =====
import { describe, expect, test } from 'vitest'
import { portion, solutionLab, text } from './helpers'

describe('anion tests (IGCSE/AS notes)', () => {
  test('carbonate: effervescence with dilute acid, gas turns limewater milky', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 0.5 })
    const t = portion(lab, 2)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { depth: 2 }))).toMatch(/Effervescence/)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'limewater'))).toMatch(/milky/)
  })
  test('a few drops of acid on carbonate do not fizz at first (hydrogencarbonate forms first)', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 0.5 })
    const t = portion(lab, 2)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { drops: 1 }))).not.toMatch(/Effervescence/)
  })
  test.each([
    ['Cl-', /White precipitate forms/, /dissolves, giving a colourless solution/],
    ['Br-', /Cream precipitate forms/, /partially dissolves/],
    ['I-', /Yellow precipitate forms/, /^No visible change/],
  ])('halide %s with acidified silver nitrate, then aqueous ammonia', (ion, ppt, nh3) => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.1, [ion]: 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'AgNO3', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(ppt)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'NH3-2M', { depth: 3 }))).toMatch(nh3)
  })
  test('nitrate: NaOH + aluminium foil, warm: ammonia', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'NO3-': 0.2 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { depth: 1 })
    lab.addReagent(t, 'Al-foil', { pieces: 1 })
    lab.heat(t, 'warm', 60)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'damp-red-litmus'))).toMatch(/turns blue/)
  })
  test('no nitrate: aluminium + NaOH gives hydrogen bubbles but no ammonia', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'Cl-': 0.2 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { depth: 1 })
    lab.addReagent(t, 'Al-foil', { pieces: 1 })
    const warm = text(lab.heat(t, 'warm', 60))
    expect(warm).toMatch(/Effervescence/)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'damp-red-litmus'))).toMatch(/stays red/)
  })
  test('nitrite decolourises acidified KMnO4; nitrate does not', () => {
    const a = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'NO2-': 0.2 })
    const ta = portion(a)
    expect(text(a.addReagent(ta, 'KMnO4-H+', { drops: 3 }))).not.toMatch(/purple|pink/)
    const b = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'NO3-': 0.2 })
    const tb = portion(b)
    expect(text(b.addReagent(tb, 'KMnO4-H+', { drops: 3 }))).toMatch(/pink|purple/)
  })
  test('nitrite with dilute acid gives brown gas', () => {
    const a = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'NO2-': 0.2 })
    const ta = portion(a)
    const r = text(a.addReagent(ta, 'H2SO4', { depth: 1 })) + text(a.wait(20, ta))
    expect(r).toMatch(/[Bb]rown gas/)
  })
  test('sulfate: acidified barium nitrate gives white ppt', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'Ba(NO3)2', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
  })
  test('sulfite: white ppt with Ba2+ that dissolves in excess dilute strong acid; decolourises KMnO4', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'SO3 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'BaCl2', { depth: 1 }))).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HCl-2M', { depth: 2 }))).toMatch(/precipitate dissolves/)
    const t2 = portion(lab)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t2, 'KMnO4-H+', { drops: 3 }))).not.toMatch(/purple|pink/)
  })
  test('sulfite: sulfur dioxide on warming with acid turns acidified dichromate green', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.4, 'SO3 2-': 0.2 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    lab.addReagent(t, 'HCl', { depth: 1 })
    lab.heat(t, 'warm', 30)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'acidified-dichromate-paper'))).toMatch(/green/)
  })
  test('thiosulfate: pale yellow / off-white ppt slowly with acid', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'S2O3 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    const first = text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HCl', { depth: 1 }))
    expect(first).not.toMatch(/precipitate/)
    expect(text(lab.wait(60, t))).toMatch(/[Pp]ale yellow precipitate/)
  })
  test('iodine with starch gives blue-black', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'K+': 0.1, 'I-': 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    lab.addReagent(t, 'H2SO4', { drops: 5 })
    lab.addReagent(t, 'H2O2', { depth: 1 })
    lab.wait(20, t)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'starch', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/blue-black/)
  })
})

describe('gases', () => {
  test('hydrogen pops; magnesium in acid', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'H+': 1, 'Cl-': 1 })
    const t = portion(lab, 2)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'Mg-ribbon', { pieces: 1 }))).toMatch(/Effervescence/)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'lighted-splint'))).toMatch(/squeaky pop/)
  })
  test('oxygen relights a glowing splint: MnO2 + H2O2', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ H2O2: 0.8 })
    const t = portion(lab, 2)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'MnO2', { spatula: 'small' }))).toMatch(/Effervescence/)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'glowing-splint'))).toMatch(/relights/)
  })
})

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/__tests__/cations.test.ts", "bytes": 5814, "sha256": "452111c6b90ed0897aedb4f509ca68af199d2d9bbd66f17f7e96e9feba3a824d"} =====
import { describe, expect, test } from 'vitest'
import { dropwiseThenExcess, portion, solutionLab, text } from './helpers'

const SALTS: Record<string, Record<string, number>> = {
  Al: { 'Al3+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.15 },
  NH4: { 'NH4+': 0.2, 'Cl-': 0.2 },
  Ca: { 'Ca2+': 0.1, 'NO3-': 0.2 },
  Ba: { 'Ba2+': 0.1, 'Cl-': 0.2 },
  Cr: { 'Cr3+': 0.1, 'Cl-': 0.3 },
  Cu: { 'Cu2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 },
  Fe2: { 'Fe2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 },
  Fe3: { 'Fe3+': 0.1, 'Cl-': 0.3 },
  Mg: { 'Mg2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 },
  Mn: { 'Mn2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 },
  Zn: { 'Zn2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 },
}

function naoh(salt: string, reagent = 'NaOH') {
  const lab = solutionLab(SALTS[salt])
  return dropwiseThenExcess(lab, portion(lab), reagent)
}
function nh3(salt: string, reagent = 'NH3') {
  const lab = solutionLab(SALTS[salt])
  return dropwiseThenExcess(lab, portion(lab), reagent)
}

describe('cations with aqueous sodium hydroxide (IGCSE/AS notes)', () => {
  test('Al3+: white ppt, soluble in excess giving colourless solution', () => {
    const r = naoh('Al')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).toMatch(/white precipitate dissolves, giving a colourless solution/)
  })
  test('Ca2+: white ppt, insoluble in excess', () => {
    const r = naoh('Ca')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/[Ww]hite precipitate/)
    expect(r.excess).not.toMatch(/dissolves/)
  })
  test('Ba2+: only a faint white ppt', () => {
    const r = naoh('Ba', 'NaOH-2M')
    expect(r.drops + r.excess).toMatch(/faint white precipitate/)
  })
  test('Cr3+: grey-green ppt, soluble in excess giving dark green solution', () => {
    const r = naoh('Cr')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/[Gg]rey-green precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).toMatch(/dissolves, giving a (dark )?green solution/)
  })
  test('Cu2+: pale blue ppt, insoluble in excess', () => {
    const r = naoh('Cu')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/[Pp]ale blue precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).not.toMatch(/dissolves/)
  })
  test('Fe2+: green ppt, insoluble, turning brown near surface on standing', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab(SALTS.Fe2)
    const t = portion(lab)
    const r = dropwiseThenExcess(lab, t, 'NaOH')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/[Gg]reen precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).not.toMatch(/dissolves/)
    const stand = text(lab.wait(180, t))
    expect(stand).toMatch(/green precipitate turns red-brown near the surface/)
  })
  test('Fe3+: red-brown ppt, insoluble in excess', () => {
    const r = naoh('Fe3')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/[Rr]ed-brown precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).not.toMatch(/dissolves/)
  })
  test('Mg2+: white ppt, insoluble in excess', () => {
    const r = naoh('Mg')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).not.toMatch(/dissolves/)
  })
  test('Mn2+: off-white ppt rapidly turning brown', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab(SALTS.Mn)
    const t = portion(lab)
    const r = dropwiseThenExcess(lab, t, 'NaOH')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/[Oo]ff-white precipitate forms/)
    expect(text(lab.wait(60, t))).toMatch(/off-white precipitate turns brown/)
  })
  test('Zn2+: white ppt, soluble in excess', () => {
    const r = naoh('Zn')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).toMatch(/dissolves, giving a colourless solution/)
  })
  test('NH4+: no ppt; ammonia produced on warming', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab(SALTS.NH4)
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    const r = text(lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { depth: 1 }))
    expect(r).toMatch(/No visible change/)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'damp-red-litmus'))).toMatch(/stays red/)
    lab.heat(t, 'warm', 40)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'damp-red-litmus'))).toMatch(/turns blue/)
  })
})

describe('cations with aqueous ammonia', () => {
  test('Al3+: white ppt insoluble in excess', () => {
    const r = nh3('Al')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).not.toMatch(/dissolves/)
  })
  test('Ca2+: no ppt', () => {
    const r = nh3('Ca')
    expect(r.drops + r.excess).not.toMatch(/precipitate/)
  })
  test('Ba2+: no ppt', () => {
    const r = nh3('Ba', 'NH3-2M')
    expect(r.drops + r.excess).not.toMatch(/precipitate/)
  })
  test('Cr3+: grey-green ppt insoluble in excess', () => {
    const r = nh3('Cr')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/[Gg]rey-green precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).not.toMatch(/dissolves/)
  })
  test('Cu2+: pale blue ppt soluble in excess giving deep blue solution', () => {
    const r = nh3('Cu')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/[Pp]ale blue precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).toMatch(/dissolves, giving a deep blue solution/)
  })
  test('Fe2+ / Fe3+ / Mg2+ / Mn2+: ppt insoluble in excess', () => {
    for (const [s, c] of [['Fe2', /[Gg]reen/], ['Fe3', /[Rr]ed-brown/], ['Mg', /White/], ['Mn', /[Oo]ff-white/]] as const) {
      const r = nh3(s)
      expect(r.drops).toMatch(c)
      expect(r.excess).not.toMatch(/dissolves/)
    }
  })
  test('Zn2+: white ppt soluble in excess', () => {
    const r = nh3('Zn')
    expect(r.drops).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
    expect(r.excess).toMatch(/dissolves, giving a colourless solution/)
  })
})

describe('flame tests', () => {
  const cases: [Record<string, number>, RegExp][] = [
    [{ 'Li+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 0.5 }, /Red flame/],
    [{ 'Na+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 0.5 }, /Yellow flame/],
    [{ 'K+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 0.5 }, /Lilac flame/],
    [{ 'Ca2+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 1 }, /Orange-red flame/],
    [{ 'Ba2+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 1 }, /Light green flame/],
    [{ 'Cu2+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 1 }, /Blue-green flame/],
    [{ 'K+': 0.5, 'Na+': 0.3, 'Cl-': 0.8 }, /Yellow flame/],
  ]
  test.each(cases)('%o', (conc, expected) => {
    const lab = solutionLab(conc)
    expect(text(lab.flameTest('X'))).toMatch(expected)
  })
})

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/__tests__/helpers.ts", "bytes": 1166, "sha256": "46d5312646d8747b87e8850462681b924c623bac3b1f192907be1e3c04e21a98"} =====
import { Lab, type Amount, type SampleDef } from '../lab'

export function solutionLab(conc: Record<string, number>, volume = 20, reagents?: string[]) {
  return new Lab({ samples: [{ id: 'X', label: 'X', kind: 'solution', solution: { conc, volume } }], reagents })
}

export function solidLab(solid: SampleDef['solid']) {
  return new Lab({ samples: [{ id: 'S', label: 'S', kind: 'solid', solid }] })
}

export const text = (r: { observations: { text: string }[] }) => r.observations.map((o) => o.text).join(' ')

/** Add a reagent drop by drop and then in excess; return the joined observations of each stage. */
export function dropwiseThenExcess(lab: Lab, vesselId: string, reagent: string, drops = 6, excess: Amount = { cm3: 4 }) {
  const dropObs: string[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < drops; i++) dropObs.push(text(lab.addReagent(vesselId, reagent, { drops: 1 })))
  const ex = text(lab.addReagent(vesselId, reagent, excess))
  return { drops: dropObs.join(' '), excess: ex }
}

export function portion(lab: Lab, depth = 1, kind: 'test-tube' | 'boiling-tube' = 'test-tube') {
  const t = lab.newVessel(kind)
  lab.transfer('X', t.id, { depth })
  return t.id
}

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/__tests__/procedure.test.ts", "bytes": 6150, "sha256": "806975cc5f5744a27f8afd4df5b13dcf7eeac15f9a7f65ebc6045c6cab5982ba"} =====
// Realistic consequences of procedural choices and mistakes: the engine must not depend on
// "the right answer", only on what is actually in the tube.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'vitest'
import { portion, solutionLab, text } from './helpers'

describe('order of addition and existing contents', () => {
  test('NaOH added to an acidified iron(III) solution: first drops only neutralise the acid', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Fe3+': 0.1, 'Cl-': 0.3 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 })
    const first = text(lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { drops: 3 }))
    expect(first).not.toMatch(/precipitate/)
    const more = text(lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { cm3: 3 }))
    expect(more).toMatch(/[Rr]ed-brown precipitate forms/)
  })

  test('acidifying with HCl before AgNO3 gives a white ppt even with no chloride in the sample', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.2, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const ok = portion(lab)
    lab.addReagent(ok, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(ok, 'AgNO3', { drops: 5 }))).not.toMatch(/White precipitate/)
    const wrong = portion(lab)
    lab.addReagent(wrong, 'HCl', { depth: 1 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(wrong, 'AgNO3', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
  })

  test('forgetting to acidify: carbonate gives a white ppt with barium nitrate (false sulfate)', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 0.5 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'Ba(NO3)2', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { depth: 2 }))).toMatch(/precipitate dissolves|Effervescence/)
  })

  test('forgetting to acidify: carbonate gives a pale yellow ppt with silver nitrate', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 0.5 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'AgNO3', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/[Pp]ale yellow precipitate/)
  })

  test('left-over hydrogen peroxide in the tube still decolourises KMnO4, now with effervescence (O2)', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Fe2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const fresh = portion(lab)
    const a = text(lab.addReagent(fresh, 'KMnO4-H+', { drops: 3 }))
    expect(a).not.toMatch(/pink|purple|Effervescence/)
    const used = portion(lab)
    lab.addReagent(used, 'H2O2', { depth: 1 })
    lab.wait(5, used)
    const b = text(lab.addReagent(used, 'KMnO4-H+', { drops: 3 }))
    expect(b).not.toMatch(/pink|purple/)
    expect(b).toMatch(/Effervescence/)
  })

  test('iron(III) alone does not decolourise acidified KMnO4', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Fe3+': 0.05, 'SO4 2-': 0.075 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'KMnO4-H+', { drops: 3 }))).toMatch(/pink|purple|orange/)
  })

  test('re-using an un-rinsed tube: traces of chloride from a previous test cloud AgNO3', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 0.5 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    lab.empty(t)
    // now test a nitrate solution in the same tube without rinsing
    lab.addReagent(t, 'water', { depth: 1 })
    lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'AgNO3', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/precipitate/)
  })

  test('a rinsed tube does not carry over the previous contents', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 0.5 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    lab.rinse(t)
    lab.addReagent(t, 'water', { depth: 1 })
    lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'AgNO3', { drops: 5 }))).not.toMatch(/precipitate/)
  })

  test('tap water instead of distilled water gives a faint cloudiness with silver nitrate', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({})
    const t = lab.newVessel('test-tube').id
    lab.addReagent(t, 'tap-water', { depth: 2 })
    lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { drops: 5 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'AgNO3', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/faint white precipitate/)
  })

  test('aluminium ions with a large excess of NaOH poured in at once: precipitate seen only momentarily', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Al3+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.15 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { cm3: 4 }))).toMatch(/forms but dissolves again/)
  })

  test('acid added to the zincate solution re-precipitates zinc hydroxide, then dissolves it', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Zn2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { cm3: 4 })
    const back = text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { cm3: 3.6 }))
    expect(back).toMatch(/White precipitate/)
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { cm3: 4 }))).toMatch(/dissolves/)
  })

  test('acidifying the deep blue copper-ammonia complex brings back the pale blue colour', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Cu2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    lab.addReagent(t, 'NH3', { cm3: 6 })
    const r = text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { cm3: 12 }))
    expect(r).toMatch(/deep blue/)
    expect(r).toMatch(/pale blue|colourless/)
  })

  test('diammine silver(I) acidified: white AgCl returns', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.1, 'Cl-': 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    lab.addReagent(t, 'AgNO3', { drops: 10 })
    lab.addReagent(t, 'NH3', { cm3: 4 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(t, 'HNO3', { cm3: 8 }))).toMatch(/white precipitate/i)
  })

  test('warming the ammonium + NaOH mixture drives off ammonia; once cold and depleted the test fails', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'NH4+': 0.05, 'Cl-': 0.05 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1, 'boiling-tube')
    lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { depth: 1 })
    lab.heat(t, 'boil', 180)
    lab.wait(240, t)
    expect(text(lab.gasTest(t, 'damp-red-litmus'))).toMatch(/stays red/)
  })

  test('iron(II) hydroxide left standing a long time turns largely brown', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Fe2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = portion(lab)
    lab.addReagent(t, 'NaOH', { cm3: 1 })
    const r = text(lab.wait(1800, t))
    expect(r).toMatch(/turns red-brown/)
  })
})

// The continuous actions used by the interactive bench: pouring while a bottle is held over a
// tube, drops from a parked dropper, heating while a tube sits in the flame, test papers and splints.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'vitest'
import { Lab } from '../lab'
import { solidLab, solutionLab, text } from './helpers'

const last = (lab: Lab) => text(lab.journal[lab.journal.length - 1])

/** Hold a bottle over a tube for `seconds` at `rate` cm³/s, ticking the bench in 0.1 s steps. */
function pourFor(lab: Lab, vesselId: string, reagentId: string, seconds: number, rate = 1.2) {
  const a = lab.begin(vesselId, `Pour ${reagentId}`)
  for (let t = 0; t < seconds; t += 0.1) {
    lab.pourStep(a, { reagentId }, rate * 0.1)
    lab.tick(0.1)
  }
  lab.release(a)
  lab.tick(5)
}

describe('continuous actions', () => {
  test('a pour is written up once, after it has settled', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Cu2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const n0 = lab.journal.length
    const a = lab.begin(t.id, 'Pour X')
    lab.pourStep(a, { vesselId: 'X' }, 1.3)
    lab.release(a)
    expect(lab.journal.length).toBe(n0)
    pourFor(lab, t.id, 'NH3', 3)
    expect(last(lab)).toMatch(/[Pp]ale blue precipitate forms but dissolves again|deep blue/)
  })

  test('drops from a parked dropper, then a pour in excess (Zn2+ with NaOH)', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Zn2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const p = lab.begin(t.id, 'Pour X')
    lab.pourStep(p, { vesselId: 'X' }, 1.3)
    lab.release(p, undefined, 0)
    const d = lab.begin(t.id, 'Add drops')
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      lab.dropStep(d, 'NaOH')
      lab.tick(0.4)
    }
    lab.release(d, undefined, 2)
    lab.tick(3)
    expect(last(lab)).toMatch(/White precipitate forms/)
    pourFor(lab, t.id, 'NaOH', 3)
    expect(last(lab)).toMatch(/dissolves, giving a colourless solution/)
  })

  test('heating while the tube sits in the flame: water of crystallisation condenses', () => {
    const lab = solidLab([{ compound: 'CuSO4.5H2O', grams: 1 }])
    const h = lab.newVessel('hard-glass-tube')
    lab.transfer('S', h.id, { all: true })
    const a = lab.begin(h.id, 'Heat strongly', { heating: true })
    lab.setHeating(h.id, 'strong')
    for (let t = 0; t < 40; t += 0.5) lab.tick(0.5)
    lab.setHeating(h.id, null)
    lab.release(a, 'Heat strongly for 40 s', 1)
    lab.tick(2)
    expect(last(lab)).toMatch(/[Cc]ondensation/)
    expect(last(lab)).toMatch(/white/)
  })

  test('damp red litmus at the mouth detects ammonia; dry litmus does not', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'NH4+': 0.2, 'Cl-': 0.2 })
    const t = lab.newVessel('boiling-tube')
    const a = lab.begin(t.id, 'Pour X')
    lab.pourStep(a, { vesselId: 'X' }, 3)
    lab.pourStep(a, { reagentId: 'NaOH' }, 3)
    lab.release(a, undefined, 0)
    lab.setHeating(t.id, 'warm')
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) lab.tick(1)
    expect(text(lab.mouthTest(t.id, 'damp-red-litmus', { damp: false }))).toMatch(/dry red litmus paper does not change/)
    expect(text(lab.mouthTest(t.id, 'damp-red-litmus', { damp: true }))).toMatch(/turns blue/)
  })

  test('dipping red litmus into the alkaline mixture turns it blue even without ammonium (a classic mistake)', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.1, 'Cl-': 0.1 })
    const t = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const a = lab.begin(t.id, 'Pour X')
    lab.pourStep(a, { vesselId: 'X' }, 1.3)
    lab.pourStep(a, { reagentId: 'NaOH' }, 1.3)
    lab.release(a, undefined, 0)
    expect(text(lab.indicatorDip(t.id, 'red-litmus'))).toMatch(/turns blue/)
    expect(text(lab.mouthTest(t.id, 'damp-red-litmus', { damp: true }))).toMatch(/stays red/)
  })

  test('teat pipette: carbon dioxide from a carbonate turns limewater milky', () => {
    const lab = solidLab([{ compound: 'Na2CO3', grams: 0.5 }])
    const acid = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const lw = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const l = lab.begin(lw.id, 'Pour limewater')
    lab.pourStep(l, { reagentId: 'limewater' }, 2.6)
    lab.release(l, undefined, 0)
    const p = lab.begin(acid.id, 'Pour acid')
    lab.pourStep(p, { reagentId: 'HNO3' }, 2.6)
    lab.release(p, undefined, 0)
    const s = lab.begin(acid.id, 'Add solid')
    lab.deposit(s, lab.scoop({ vesselId: 'S' }, 0.1))
    lab.tick(1)
    const gas = lab.collectGas(acid.id)
    expect(text(lab.bubbleGas(gas, lw.id)!)).toMatch(/limewater turns milky/)
  })

  test('fresh limewater is a clear solution', () => {
    const lab = new Lab()
    const lw = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const l = lab.begin(lw.id, 'Pour limewater')
    lab.pourStep(l, { reagentId: 'limewater' }, 2.6)
    expect(text(lab.release(l, undefined, 0)!)).toBe('No visible change.')
    expect([...lw.n.keys()].some((k) => k.endsWith('(s)'))).toBe(false)
  })

  test('teat pipette: air from an empty tube leaves limewater clear', () => {
    const lab = new Lab()
    const empty = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const lw = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const l = lab.begin(lw.id, 'Pour limewater')
    lab.pourStep(l, { reagentId: 'limewater' }, 2.6)
    lab.release(l, undefined, 0)
    expect(text(lab.bubbleGas(lab.collectGas(empty.id), lw.id)!)).toMatch(/stays clear/)
  })

  test('filtering: precipitate stays on the paper, the filtrate runs through', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Cu2+': 0.1, 'SO4 2-': 0.1 })
    const t = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const a = lab.begin(t.id, 'Pour X')
    lab.pourStep(a, { vesselId: 'X' }, 2)
    lab.pourStep(a, { reagentId: 'NaOH' }, 0.6)
    lab.release(a, undefined, 0)
    const paper = lab.newVessel('filter-paper', 'filter paper')
    const below = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const f = lab.begin(below.id, 'Filter')
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) lab.filterStep(f, { vesselId: t.id }, paper.id, 0.4)
    const r = lab.release(f, undefined, 0)!
    expect(text(r)).toMatch(/[Pp]ale blue residue on the filter paper/)
    expect(below.V).toBeGreaterThan(1.5)
  })

  test('flame reading from a loaded wire', () => {
    const lab = solidLab([{ compound: 'KI', grams: 0.5 }])
    expect(lab.flameReading('S')).toMatch(/Lilac/)
  })
})

import { describe, expect, test } from 'vitest'

test('the public archive contains exactly the available 2023–2026 source sets, with honest original question numbers', () => {
  expect(PAPER_SOURCES).toHaveLength(67)
  expect(new Set(PAPERS.map(p => p.id)).size).toBe(67)
  expect(PAPER_SOURCES.filter(p => p.code === '0620')).toHaveLength(22)

describe.each(Object.entries(RECENT_BENCHES))('%s sample preparation', (id, definition) => {
  test('loads all declared samples with finite amounts and registered reagents', () => {
    const lab = new Lab(definition)
    expect(lab.samples.size).toBe(definition.samples.length)
    expect(PAPERS.find(p => p.id === id)?.coverage).toBe(id.startsWith('9701') ? 'guided' : 'configured')
    for (const s of definition.samples) {
      const v = lab.sampleVessel(s.id)
      expect(v.isEmpty()).toBe(false)
      for (const [species, amount] of v.n) {
        expect(SPECIES[species], species).toBeDefined()
        expect(Number.isFinite(amount) && amount > 0).toBe(true)
      }
      for (const solid of s.solid ?? []) expect(COMPOUNDS[solid.compound]).toBeDefined()
    }
    for (const reagent of definition.reagents) expect(REAGENTS[reagent], reagent).toBeDefined()
  })
})

function portion(id: string, sample: string, acid = false) {
  const lab = new Lab(RECENT_BENCHES[id])
  const v = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  lab.transfer(sample, v.id, { fraction: 0.08 })
  if (!v.wet) lab.addReagent(v.id, acid ? 'HCl' : 'water', { cm3: 2 })
  return { lab, v }
}

import { expect, test } from 'vitest'
import { Lab } from '../lab'
import { text } from './helpers'

test('R11 filter journal matches the returned filtrate and residue observations', () => {
  const lab = new Lab()
  const source = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  source.receive({ 'Cu(OH)2(s)': 0.2 }, 3)
  const { result } = lab.filter(source.id)
  expect(text(lab.journal[lab.journal.length - 1])).toBe(text(result))
})

test('R12 emptying a reacting tube stops phantom bubbling', () => {
  const lab = new Lab()
  const v = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  v.V = 3
  v.bubbleRate = 1
  lab.empty(v.id)
  expect(v.bubbleRate).toBe(0)
})

test('R13 rinsing writes up a pending reaction before destroying its contents', () => {
  const lab = new Lab()
  const v = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  const a = lab.begin(v.id, 'Add copper solution')
  lab.pourStep(a, { reagentId: 'CuSO4' }, 1)
  lab.rinse(v.id)
  expect(lab.journal[0].action).toBe('Add copper solution')
  expect(lab.journal[1].action).toBe('Rinse with distilled water')
})

test('R14 cold water cools a hot solution when poured in', () => {
  const lab = new Lab()
  const v = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  v.V = 2
  v.T = 100
  const a = lab.begin(v.id, 'Dilute')
  lab.pourStep(a, { reagentId: 'water' }, 2)
  expect(v.T).toBeCloseTo(60)
})

test('R15 hot liquid retains its temperature when transferred to a clean tube', () => {
  const lab = new Lab()
  const from = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  from.V = 3
  from.T = 80
  const to = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  lab.pourStep(lab.begin(to.id, 'Transfer'), { vesselId: from.id }, 2)
  expect(to.T).toBeCloseTo(80)
})

test('R16 repeated gas sampling cannot create more ammonia than the tube contains', () => {
  const lab = new Lab()
  const v = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  v.receive({ NH3: 0.1 }, 1)
  let collected = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < 1000; i++) collected += lab.collectGas(v.id).get('NH3(g)') ?? 0
  expect(collected).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.100001)
  expect(v.amount('NH3')).toBeLessThan(0.1)
})

test('R17 a cancelled action cannot silently accept unrecorded chemicals', () => {
  const lab = new Lab()
  const v = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  const a = lab.begin(v.id, 'Pour')
  lab.cancel(a)
  expect(lab.pourStep(a, { reagentId: 'NaOH' }, 1)).toBe(0)
  expect(v.V).toBe(0)
})

test('R18 a stoppered receiver rejects drops even if its action started while open', () => {
  const lab = new Lab()
  const v = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  const a = lab.begin(v.id, 'Drops')
  v.stoppered = true
  expect(lab.dropStep(a, 'NaOH')).toBe(0)

import { expect, test } from 'vitest'
import { RULES } from '../rules'
import { SPECIES } from '../species'

test('every species used by a rule exists', () => {
  for (const r of RULES) for (const s of [...Object.keys(r.reactants), ...Object.keys(r.products)]) expect(SPECIES[s], `${r.id}: ${s}`).toBeDefined()
})

test('every rule conserves charge', () => {
  const charge = (st: Record<string, number>) => Object.entries(st).reduce((sum, [s, nu]) => sum + nu * (SPECIES[s]?.charge ?? 0), 0)
  for (const r of RULES) expect(charge(r.reactants), r.id).toBeCloseTo(charge(r.products), 9)
})

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/__tests__/thermal.test.ts", "bytes": 2607, "sha256": "877718505121ae6d634e77067c05ad19e04e92ce10da61f94fccf5bed646088a"} =====
// Heating: boiling liquids lose water; heated salts keep their non-volatile ions in the residue.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'vitest'
import { allCompounds } from '../compounds'
import { SPECIES } from '../species'
import { solidLab, solutionLab, portion, text } from './helpers'

// ions that may legitimately leave entirely as gases (NH3, HCl, CO2, NO2, …)
const VOLATILE_IONS = new Set(['NH4+', 'H+', 'CO3 2-', 'HCO3-', 'NO3-', 'Cl-'])

describe('heating dry solids', () => {
  test.each(allCompounds().filter((d) => d.thermal && d.dissolves).map((d) => [d.id, d] as const))(
    '%s: every heating step leaves a solid holding its non-volatile ions',
    (_id, d) => {
      const keeps = Object.keys(d.dissolves!).some((ion) => !VOLATILE_IONS.has(ion))
      if (!keeps) return
      for (const step of d.thermal!) {
        const solids = Object.keys(step.products).filter((p) => SPECIES[p]?.phase === 's')
        expect(solids.length, `${d.id} at ${step.minTemp} °C`).toBeGreaterThan(0)
      }
    },
  )

  test('hydrated magnesium sulfate: the anhydrous residue still gives Mg2+ and SO4 2- tests', () => {
    const lab = solidLab([{ compound: 'MgSO4.7H2O', grams: 1 }])
    const h = lab.newVessel('hard-glass-tube')
    lab.transfer('S', h.id, { all: true })
    expect(text(lab.heat(h.id, 'gentle', 90))).toMatch(/[Cc]ondensation/)
    lab.wait(120, h.id)
    lab.addReagent(h.id, 'water', { depth: 3 })
    lab.shake(h.id)
    const a = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    const b = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
    lab.transfer(h.id, a.id, { depth: 1 })
    lab.transfer(h.id, b.id, { depth: 1 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(a.id, 'NaOH', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/White precipitate/)
    lab.addReagent(b.id, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 })
    expect(text(lab.addReagent(b.id, 'Ba(NO3)2', { drops: 5 }))).toMatch(/White precipitate/)
  })
})

describe('boiling liquids', () => {
  test('heating to boiling drives off water and eventually boils to dryness', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 0.5 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1.5)
    const V0 = lab.vessel(t).V
    expect(text(lab.heat(t, 'boil', 60))).toMatch(/boils/)
    expect(lab.vessel(t).V).toBeLessThan(V0 - 0.3)
    expect(text(lab.heat(t, 'boil', 90))).toMatch(/boils away, leaving a residue/)
    expect(lab.vessel(t).wet).toBe(false)
  })

  test('warming gently does not boil the liquid away', () => {
    const lab = solutionLab({ 'Na+': 0.5, 'Cl-': 0.5 })
    const t = portion(lab, 1.5)
    const V0 = lab.vessel(t).V
    lab.heat(t, 'warm', 90)
    expect(lab.vessel(t).V).toBeCloseTo(V0, 5)
  })
})

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/appearance.ts", "bytes": 3518, "sha256": "4b4fdc6ca4d8a7695a210ff34b9e08e560d10ff60f42e89d68692fadd15bb47f"} =====
// Visual description of a vessel for rendering (no chemistry decisions here).

import { solutionColour, solidAppearance, mixColours, type RGB } from './colour'
import { drySolidDescription, solidMetric } from './observe'
import { SPECIES } from './species'
import { molarMassOf } from './lab'
import type { Vessel } from './vessel'

export interface Appearance {
  kind: Vessel['kind']
  label: string
  /** 0..1 of the usable height */
  liquidLevel: number
  liquidRGB: RGB
  /** 0..1 how strongly coloured the liquid is */
  liquidAlpha: number
  /** suspended precipitate */
  turbidity: number
  turbidityRGB: RGB
  /** settled precipitate layer, 0..1 of height */
  sediment: number
  sedimentRGB: RGB
  /** dry solid at the bottom (powder / crystals), 0..1 of height */
  solid: number
  solidRGB: RGB
  /** small pieces of metal */
  pieces: { kind: 'ribbon' | 'foil' | 'powder'; amount: number }[]
  /** 0..1 bubbling intensity */
  bubbles: number
  /** coloured gas in the head-space */
  gasRGB: RGB | null
  gasAlpha: number
  condensation: number
  hot: boolean
  heating: boolean
  stoppered: boolean
  T: number
}

const PIECE_KIND: Record<string, 'ribbon' | 'foil' | 'powder'> = { 'Mg(s)': 'ribbon', 'Al(s)': 'foil', 'Zn(s)': 'powder' }

export function appearance(v: Vessel): Appearance {
  const cap = v.spec.capacity
  const sol = solutionColour(v)
  const pieces: Appearance['pieces'] = []
  let susp = 0
  let sedimentVol = 0
  const suspParts: { rgb: RGB; w: number }[] = []
  const sedParts: { rgb: RGB; w: number }[] = []
  let dryMass = 0
  for (const [id, amt] of v.n) {
    const sp = SPECIES[id]
    if (sp?.phase !== 's' || amt < 1e-6) continue
    if (PIECE_KIND[id]) {
      pieces.push({ kind: PIECE_KIND[id], amount: amt })
      continue
    }
    if (v.wet) {
      const m = solidMetric(v, id)
      const rgb = solidAppearance(id, v.T).rgb
      susp += m * (1 - v.settled)
      suspParts.push({ rgb, w: m * (1 - v.settled) })
      // hydrous precipitates are bulky: ~0.06 cm³ per mmol once settled
      const vol = amt * 0.06 * v.settled
      sedimentVol += vol
      sedParts.push({ rgb, w: vol })
    } else {
      dryMass += (amt / 1000) * molarMassOf(id)
    }
  }
  const usable = cap / v.spec.area
  const dry = drySolidDescription(v)
  const gasParts: { rgb: RGB; w: number }[] = []
  let gasAmt = 0
  for (const [g, a] of v.headspace) {
    const c = SPECIES[g]?.gasColour
    if (!c || a < 1e-4) continue
    const rgb = [parseInt(c.slice(1, 3), 16), parseInt(c.slice(3, 5), 16), parseInt(c.slice(5, 7), 16)] as RGB
    gasParts.push({ rgb, w: a })
    gasAmt += a
  }
  const intensity = sol.intensity
  return {
    kind: v.kind,
    label: v.label,
    liquidLevel: Math.min(1, v.V / cap),
    liquidRGB: sol.rgb,
    liquidAlpha: v.wet ? Math.min(0.92, 0.08 + (1 - Math.exp(-intensity * 0.9)) * 0.85) : 0,
    turbidity: Math.min(0.95, 1 - Math.exp(-susp * 160)),
    turbidityRGB: mixColours(suspParts),
    sediment: Math.min(0.5, sedimentVol / v.spec.area / usable),
    sedimentRGB: mixColours(sedParts),
    solid: dryMass > 0 ? Math.min(0.45, ((dryMass * 0.7) / v.spec.area / usable) * 1.2 + 0.02) : 0,
    solidRGB: dry?.rgb ?? [240, 240, 240],
    pieces,
    bubbles: Math.min(1, v.bubbleRate * 25),
    gasRGB: gasParts.length ? mixColours(gasParts) : null,
    gasAlpha: Math.min(0.8, gasAmt * 40),
    condensation: Math.min(1, v.condensation * 8),
    hot: v.T > 45,
    heating: v.heating !== null,
    stoppered: v.stoppered,
    T: v.T,
  }
}

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/colour.ts", "bytes": 4585, "sha256": "ba7f4f79f796b51084c8b62cd67820c6dfa443dd1aff8f755392e62dc9c1d1d4"} =====
// Colour model: Beer–Lambert mixing for solutions, weighted blending for solids, and
// exam-style colour words.

import { SPECIES, colourNameOf } from './species'
import type { Vessel } from './vessel'

export type RGB = [number, number, number]

const REF_PATH = 1.3

export function hexToRgb(hex: string): RGB {
  const h = hex.replace('#', '')
  return [parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16), parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16), parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16)]
}

export function rgbToHex([r, g, b]: RGB): string {
  const c = (x: number) => Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(255, x))).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')
  return `#${c(r)}${c(g)}${c(b)}`
}

const epsCache = new Map<string, RGB>()
/** absorbance per (mol dm⁻³) per channel for a reference 1.3 cm path */
function epsilon(id: string): RGB | null {
  const sp = SPECIES[id]
  if (!sp?.tint || !sp.refConc) return null
  let e = epsCache.get(id)
  if (!e) {
    const rgb = hexToRgb(sp.tint)
    e = rgb.map((ch) => -Math.log10(Math.max(ch / 255, 0.004)) / sp.refConc!) as RGB
    epsCache.set(id, e)
  }
  return e
}

export function pathLength(v: Vessel) {
  return v.kind === 'boiling-tube' ? 2.1 : 1.3
}

export interface SolutionColour {
  rgb: RGB
  name: string
  /** total absorbance, a measure of intensity */
  intensity: number
}

export function solutionColour(v: Vessel): SolutionColour {
  if (!v.wet) return { rgb: [255, 255, 255], name: 'none', intensity: 0 }
  const path = pathLength(v) / REF_PATH
  const A: RGB = [0, 0, 0]
  const contrib: { id: string; a: number; c: number }[] = []
  for (const [id] of v.n) {
    const e = epsilon(id)
    if (!e) continue
    const c = v.conc(id)
    if (c <= 0) continue
    let a = 0
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const x = e[i] * c * path
      A[i] += x
      a += x
    }
    contrib.push({ id, a, c })
  }
  const rgb = A.map((a) => 255 * Math.pow(10, -a)) as RGB
  const total = contrib.reduce((s, x) => s + x.a, 0)
  if (total < 0.03) return { rgb, name: 'colourless', intensity: total }
  contrib.sort((a, b) => b.a - a.a)
  // an intensely coloured species (e.g. starch–iodine) names the mixture even when not dominant
  const strong = contrib.find((x) => SPECIES[x.id].nameDominance !== undefined && x.a >= SPECIES[x.id].nameDominance! * total)
  let name: string
  if (strong) name = colourNameOf(strong.id, strong.c) ?? nameFromRgb(rgb)
  else if (contrib[0].a >= 0.72 * total) name = colourNameOf(contrib[0].id, contrib[0].c) ?? nameFromRgb(rgb)
  else name = nameFromRgb(rgb)
  return { rgb, name, intensity: total }
}

/** Approximate exam-style colour word for an RGB colour. */
export function nameFromRgb([r, g, b]: RGB): string {
  const R = r / 255
  const G = g / 255
  const B = b / 255
  const max = Math.max(R, G, B)
  const min = Math.min(R, G, B)
  const l = (max + min) / 2
  const d = max - min
  const s = d === 0 ? 0 : d / (1 - Math.abs(2 * l - 1))
  let h = 0
  if (d !== 0) {
    if (max === R) h = 60 * (((G - B) / d) % 6)
    else if (max === G) h = 60 * ((B - R) / d + 2)
    else h = 60 * ((R - G) / d + 4)
  }
  if (h < 0) h += 360
  if (l < 0.12) return 'black'
  if (s < 0.12) return l > 0.85 ? 'colourless' : l > 0.55 ? 'grey' : l > 0.25 ? 'dark grey' : 'black'
  let base: string
  if (h < 12 || h >= 345) base = l > 0.55 ? 'pink' : 'red'
  else if (h < 45) base = l < 0.5 ? 'brown' : 'orange'
  else if (h < 68) base = l < 0.4 ? 'brown' : 'yellow'
  else if (h < 160) base = 'green'
  else if (h < 195) base = 'blue-green'
  else if (h < 255) base = 'blue'
  else if (h < 290) base = 'purple'
  else base = l > 0.6 ? 'pink' : 'purple'
  if (base === 'brown') return l < 0.25 ? 'dark brown' : base
  if (l > 0.8) return `pale ${base}`
  if (l < 0.28) return `dark ${base}`
  return base
}

export function mixColours(parts: { rgb: RGB; w: number }[]): RGB {
  const tot = parts.reduce((s, p) => s + p.w, 0)
  if (tot <= 0) return [255, 255, 255]
  const out: RGB = [0, 0, 0]
  for (const p of parts) for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) out[i] += (p.rgb[i] * p.w) / tot
  return out
}

/** Colour and word for a solid species, honouring thermochromism. */
export function solidAppearance(id: string, T: number): { rgb: RGB; name: string } {
  const sp = SPECIES[id]
  if (!sp) return { rgb: [200, 200, 200], name: 'grey' }
  if (T > 250 && sp.hotColour) return { rgb: hexToRgb(sp.hotColour), name: sp.hotColourName ?? nameFromRgb(hexToRgb(sp.hotColour)) }
  const colour = sp.colour ?? '#dddddd'
  const name = typeof sp.colourName === 'string' ? sp.colourName : nameFromRgb(hexToRgb(colour))
  return { rgb: hexToRgb(colour), name }
}

// Starting materials (bench chemicals and unknowns).
//
// A soluble compound sits in a vessel as its own solid species `cmp:<id>` until water is
// present, then dissociates into its ions. Insoluble compounds (e.g. BaCO3) map straight to
// the corresponding precipitate species so that the ordinary acid / thermal rules apply.

import { registerSpecies, SPECIES } from './species'

export interface ThermalStep {
  /** temperature (°C) at which the step proceeds */
  minTemp: number
  /** seconds for half of the remaining solid to react once minTemp is reached */
  halfLife: number
  /** products per mole of the solid species (solids stay, `(g)` species go to the head-space,
   *  'H2O(g)' produces condensation on the cooler part of the tube) */
  products: Record<string, number>
  /** extra reactants consumed per mole (other solids in the tube) */
  coReactants?: Record<string, number>
  /** observable events emitted while the step proceeds */
  events?: string[]
}

export interface CompoundDef {
  id: string
  formula: string
  name: string
  molarMass: number
  colour: string
  colourName: string
  /** ions released per mole when dissolved in water (omit for insoluble compounds) */
  dissolves?: Record<string, number>
  /** insoluble compounds: the solid species this compound is in the engine */
  asSpecies?: string
  /** thermal behaviour when heated dry */
  thermal?: ThermalStep[]
}

const c = (d: CompoundDef) => list.push(d)

export const COMPOUNDS: Record<string, CompoundDef> = {}
for (const d of list) {
  COMPOUNDS[d.id] = d
  if (!d.asSpecies) {
    registerSpecies({ id: `cmp:${d.id}`, formula: d.formula, name: d.name, phase: 's', colour: d.colour, colourName: d.colourName, visibility: 1 })
  }
}

/** The engine species a compound becomes when placed in a vessel. */
export function compoundSpecies(id: string): string {
  const d = COMPOUNDS[id]
  if (!d) throw new Error(`Unknown compound: ${id}`)
  return d.asSpecies ?? `cmp:${id}`
}

  return list

// Ensure every species referenced in thermal products exists (fail fast during development).
for (const d of list) {
  for (const step of d.thermal ?? []) {
    for (const p of Object.keys(step.products)) {
      if (!SPECIES[p]) throw new Error(`Thermal product ${p} of ${d.id} is not a known species`)
    }
  }
}
