===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/rules.ts", "bytes": 30669, "sha256": "dc423e5ee8068b244a9ce3be9072df8cf480a11f2f7b437a1a0a47f842026afb"} =====
// Reaction rules.
//
// The engine is a semi-quantitative stoichiometric model, tuned for Cambridge Q2-style
// qualitative analysis rather than research chemistry:
//
//  * complete     – fast reactions that run to completion (neutralisation, most redox).
//                   An optional `reserve` keeps a minimum free concentration of a reagent,
//                   which is how "soluble only in excess" behaviour is modelled.
//  * equilibrium  – single-reaction equilibria solved exactly for the current contents
//                   (precipitation via Ksp, silver halide / ammonia, starch–iodine …).
//  * kinetic      – slow processes that progress with time (air oxidation, thiosulfate + acid,
//                   catalytic decomposition of H2O2, heating …).
//
// Fast rules are applied in ascending `priority`; whenever one fires the scan restarts from
// the top, so e.g. added NaOH always neutralises free acid before it can precipitate a metal
// hydroxide. Water is never written explicitly.

import { SOLID_THERMAL, MIXED_THERMAL, COMPOUNDS, compoundSpecies } from './compounds'

export type Stoich = Record<string, number>

export interface RuleCtx {
  /** temperature, °C */
  T: number
  /** liquid volume, cm³ */
  V: number
  /** true when there is enough liquid for solution chemistry */
  wet: boolean
  /** amount, mmol */
  n(id: string): number
  /** concentration, mol dm⁻³ (0 when dry) */
  c(id: string): number
  /** rough pH estimate of the solution (7 when dry) */
  pH(): number
}

export type RuleType = 'complete' | 'equilibrium' | 'kinetic'

export interface Rule {
  id: string
  type: RuleType
  reactants: Stoich
  products: Stoich
  priority?: number
  /** equilibrium constant in mol dm⁻³ units; solids and water omitted from Q */
  K?: number
  /** minimum free concentration (mol dm⁻³) of reactants that must remain */
  reserve?: Record<string, number>
  /** kinetic half-life in seconds (Infinity = not running) */
  halfLife?: number | ((ctx: RuleCtx) => number)
  /** extra condition */
  when?: (ctx: RuleCtx) => boolean
  /** rule acts on dry solids (heating) rather than in solution */
  dry?: boolean
  /** applies whether wet or dry */
  anyPhase?: boolean
  minTemp?: number
  /** observable events emitted when the rule makes noticeable progress */
  events?: string[]
}

export const RULES: Rule[] = []
const r = (rule: Rule) => {
  RULES.push(rule)
  return rule
}

// ======================================================================= dissolving solids
// Soluble compounds dissolve as soon as there is water (shaking is assumed).
for (const d of Object.values(COMPOUNDS)) {
  if (!d.dissolves) continue
  r({ id: `dissolve:${d.id}`, type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { [compoundSpecies(d.id)]: 1 }, products: { ...d.dissolves } })
}
// Dehydrated residues that are soluble
r({ id: 'dissolve:Na2CO3(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'Na2CO3(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Na+': 2, 'CO3 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:Na2SO3(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'Na2SO3(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Na+': 2, 'SO3 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:Na2SO4(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'Na2SO4(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Na+': 2, 'SO4 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:K2SO4(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'K2SO4(s)': 1 }, products: { 'K+': 2, 'SO4 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:NaNO2(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'NaNO2(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Na+': 1, 'NO2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:KNO2(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'KNO2(s)': 1 }, products: { 'K+': 1, 'NO2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:CuSO4(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'CuSO4(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Cu2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:FeSO4(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'FeSO4(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Fe2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:CoCl2(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'CoCl2(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Co2+': 1, 'Cl-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:BaCl2(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'BaCl2(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Ba2+': 1, 'Cl-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:CrAlum-green(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'CrAlum-green(s)': 1 }, products: { 'K+': 1, 'Cr3+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:FeAlum-melt(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'FeAlum-melt(s)': 1 }, products: { 'NH4+': 1, 'Fe3+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:NH4Cl-sublimate(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'NH4Cl-sublimate(s)': 1 }, products: { 'NH4+': 1, 'Cl-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'dissolve:K2MnO4(s)', type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { 'K2MnO4(s)': 1 }, products: { 'K+': 2, 'MnO4 2-': 1 } })
const RESIDUE_IONS: Record<string, Stoich> = {
  'Na2S2O3(s)': { 'Na+': 2, 'S2O3 2-': 1 },
  'NH4Br-sublimate(s)': { 'NH4+': 1, 'Br-': 1 },
  'NH4HSO4(s)': { 'NH4+': 1, 'H+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 },
  'MgSO4(s)': { 'Mg2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 },
  'MgCl2(s)': { 'Mg2+': 1, 'Cl-': 2 },
  'Ca(NO3)2(s)': { 'Ca2+': 1, 'NO3-': 2 },
  'Al2(SO4)3(s)': { 'Al3+': 2, 'SO4 2-': 3 },
  'KAl(SO4)2(s)': { 'K+': 1, 'Al3+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 2 },
  'CrCl3(s)': { 'Cr3+': 1, 'Cl-': 3 },
  'MnSO4(s)': { 'Mn2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 },
  'NiSO4(s)': { 'Ni2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 },
  'CuCl2(s)': { 'Cu2+': 1, 'Cl-': 2 },
  'ZnSO4(s)': { 'Zn2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 },
}
for (const [sp, ions] of Object.entries(RESIDUE_IONS)) r({ id: `dissolve:${sp}`, type: 'complete', priority: 0, reactants: { [sp]: 1 }, products: ions })

// ======================================================================= acid–base (complete)
r({ id: 'neutralise', type: 'complete', priority: 1, reactants: { 'H+': 1, 'OH-': 1 }, products: {} })
r({ id: 'NH3+H+', type: 'complete', priority: 1, reactants: { NH3: 1, 'H+': 1 }, products: { 'NH4+': 1 } })
r({ id: 'CO3+H+', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'CO3 2-': 1, 'H+': 1 }, products: { 'HCO3-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'HCO3+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'HCO3-': 1, 'OH-': 1 }, products: { 'CO3 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'CO2+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'CO2(aq)': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, products: { 'CO3 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'SO2+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'SO2(aq)': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, products: { 'SO3 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'HCO3+H+', type: 'complete', priority: 3, reactants: { 'HCO3-': 1, 'H+': 1 }, products: { 'CO2(aq)': 1 } })
r({ id: 'SO3+H+', type: 'complete', priority: 3, reactants: { 'SO3 2-': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'SO2(aq)': 1 } })
// chlorate(I) oxidises iodide before it can release chlorine with acid
r({ id: 'ClO+I-', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'ClO-': 1, 'I-': 2 }, products: { I2: 1, 'Cl-': 1, 'OH-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'ClO+H+Cl', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'ClO-': 1, 'Cl-': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Cl2(g)': 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('H+') > 1 && x.c('Cl-') > 0.5 ? 10 : Infinity) })
// chromate / dichromate equilibrium: 2CrO4 2- + 2H+ ⇌ Cr2O7 2- + H2O
r({ id: 'CrO4/Cr2O7', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 3, reactants: { 'CrO4 2-': 2, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Cr2O7 2-': 1 }, K: 4e14 })
r({ id: 'Cr2O7+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Cr2O7 2-': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, products: { 'CrO4 2-': 2 } })
// ammonium + hydroxide (strong base liberates ammonia)
r({ id: 'NH4+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 4, reactants: { 'NH4+': 1, 'OH-': 1 }, products: { NH3: 1 } })

// ======================================================================= precipitation (Ksp equilibria)
const ppt = (id: string, ions: Stoich, solid: string, Ksp: number, priority = 3) =>
  r({ id: `ppt:${id}`, type: 'equilibrium', priority, reactants: ions, products: { [solid]: 1 }, K: 1 / Ksp })

// hydroxides with strong base
ppt('Al(OH)3', { 'Al3+': 1, 'OH-': 3 }, 'Al(OH)3(s)', 1.3e-33)
ppt('Cr(OH)3', { 'Cr3+': 1, 'OH-': 3 }, 'Cr(OH)3(s)', 6e-31)
ppt('Fe(OH)3', { 'Fe3+': 1, 'OH-': 3 }, 'Fe(OH)3(s)', 2.8e-39)
ppt('Fe(OH)2', { 'Fe2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Fe(OH)2(s)', 8e-16)
ppt('Cu(OH)2', { 'Cu2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Cu(OH)2(s)', 2.2e-20)
ppt('Zn(OH)2', { 'Zn2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Zn(OH)2(s)', 3e-17)
ppt('Ni(OH)2', { 'Ni2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Ni(OH)2(s)', 5.5e-16)
ppt('Co(OH)2', { 'Co2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Co(OH)2(s)', 1e-15)
ppt('Mn(OH)2', { 'Mn2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Mn(OH)2(s)', 2e-13)
ppt('Pb(OH)2', { 'Pb2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Pb(OH)2(s)', 1.2e-15)
ppt('Mg(OH)2', { 'Mg2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Mg(OH)2(s)', 5.6e-12)
// concentration-based value (no activity coefficients): saturated limewater is ~0.02 mol dm⁻³
ppt('Ca(OH)2', { 'Ca2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Ca(OH)2(s)', 4e-5)
// Ba(OH)2 is fairly soluble: only a faint precipitate unless [Ba2+] is very low (9701 notes)
ppt('Ba(OH)2', { 'Ba2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Ba(OH)2(s)', 4.7e-2)
// 2Ag+ + 2OH- ⇌ Ag2O + H2O ; Ksp(AgOH) ≈ 2e-8
ppt('Ag2O', { 'Ag+': 2, 'OH-': 2 }, 'Ag2O(s)', 4e-16)

// halides
ppt('AgCl', { 'Ag+': 1, 'Cl-': 1 }, 'AgCl(s)', 1.8e-10)
ppt('AgBr', { 'Ag+': 1, 'Br-': 1 }, 'AgBr(s)', 5e-13)
ppt('AgI', { 'Ag+': 1, 'I-': 1 }, 'AgI(s)', 8.5e-17)
ppt('PbCl2', { 'Pb2+': 1, 'Cl-': 2 }, 'PbCl2(s)', 1.7e-5)
ppt('PbBr2', { 'Pb2+': 1, 'Br-': 2 }, 'PbBr2(s)', 6.6e-6)
ppt('PbI2', { 'Pb2+': 1, 'I-': 2 }, 'PbI2(s)', 9.8e-9)
// sulfates / sulfites
ppt('BaSO4', { 'Ba2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 }, 'BaSO4(s)', 1.1e-10)
ppt('PbSO4', { 'Pb2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 }, 'PbSO4(s)', 2.5e-8)
ppt('CaSO4', { 'Ca2+': 1, 'SO4 2-': 1 }, 'CaSO4(s)', 4.9e-5)
ppt('Ag2SO4', { 'Ag+': 2, 'SO4 2-': 1 }, 'Ag2SO4(s)', 1.2e-5)
ppt('BaSO3', { 'Ba2+': 1, 'SO3 2-': 1 }, 'BaSO3(s)', 5e-10)
ppt('Ag2SO3', { 'Ag+': 2, 'SO3 2-': 1 }, 'Ag2SO3(s)', 1.5e-14)
// carbonates
ppt('BaCO3', { 'Ba2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'BaCO3(s)', 2.6e-9)
ppt('CaCO3', { 'Ca2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'CaCO3(s)', 3.4e-9)
ppt('MgCO3', { 'Mg2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'MgCO3(s)', 6.8e-6)
ppt('ZnCO3', { 'Zn2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'ZnCO3(s)', 1.5e-10)
ppt('CuCO3', { 'Cu2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'CuCO3(s)', 1.4e-10)
ppt('FeCO3', { 'Fe2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'FeCO3(s)', 3e-11)
ppt('NiCO3', { 'Ni2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'NiCO3(s)', 1.4e-7)
ppt('MnCO3', { 'Mn2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'MnCO3(s)', 2.2e-11)
ppt('PbCO3', { 'Pb2+': 1, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'PbCO3(s)', 7.4e-14)
ppt('Ag2CO3', { 'Ag+': 2, 'CO3 2-': 1 }, 'Ag2CO3(s)', 8.5e-12)
// chromates
ppt('BaCrO4', { 'Ba2+': 1, 'CrO4 2-': 1 }, 'BaCrO4(s)', 1.2e-10)

// Hydrolysis of M3+ by carbonate / hydrogencarbonate: hydroxide + CO2 rather than a carbonate
for (const [m, oh] of [['Al3+', 'Al(OH)3(s)'], ['Cr3+', 'Cr(OH)3(s)'], ['Fe3+', 'Fe(OH)3(s)']] as const) {
  r({ id: `hyd:${m}+CO3`, type: 'complete', priority: 3, reactants: { [m]: 2, 'CO3 2-': 3 }, products: { [oh]: 2, 'CO2(aq)': 3 } })
  r({ id: `hyd:${m}+HCO3`, type: 'complete', priority: 3, reactants: { [m]: 1, 'HCO3-': 3 }, products: { [oh]: 1, 'CO2(aq)': 3 } })
}
// M2+ + 2HCO3- -> MCO3 + CO2 (+H2O)
for (const [m, s] of [['Cu2+', 'CuCO3(s)'], ['Zn2+', 'ZnCO3(s)'], ['Fe2+', 'FeCO3(s)'], ['Pb2+', 'PbCO3(s)'], ['Mn2+', 'MnCO3(s)'], ['Ni2+', 'NiCO3(s)']] as const) {
  r({ id: `hyd:${m}+HCO3`, type: 'complete', priority: 3, reactants: { [m]: 1, 'HCO3-': 2 }, products: { [s]: 1, 'CO2(aq)': 1 } })
}
r({ id: 'hyd:Ag+HCO3', type: 'complete', priority: 3, reactants: { 'Ag+': 2, 'HCO3-': 2 }, products: { 'Ag2CO3(s)': 1, 'CO2(aq)': 1 } })

// ======================================================================= weak base: aqueous ammonia
// Ammonia supplies hydroxide only until the metal is precipitated. Ca2+ and Ba2+ are not
// precipitated by aqueous ammonia (Cambridge notes: "no ppt.").
const nh3ppt: [string, string, number][] = [
  ['Al3+', 'Al(OH)3(s)', 3], ['Cr3+', 'Cr(OH)3(s)', 3], ['Fe3+', 'Fe(OH)3(s)', 3], ['Fe2+', 'Fe(OH)2(s)', 2],
  ['Cu2+', 'Cu(OH)2(s)', 2], ['Zn2+', 'Zn(OH)2(s)', 2], ['Ni2+', 'Ni(OH)2(s)', 2], ['Co2+', 'Co(OH)2(s)', 2],
  ['Mn2+', 'Mn(OH)2(s)', 2], ['Pb2+', 'Pb(OH)2(s)', 2], ['Mg2+', 'Mg(OH)2(s)', 2],
]
for (const [m, s, k] of nh3ppt) {
  r({ id: `nh3ppt:${m}`, type: 'complete', priority: 4, reactants: { [m]: 1, NH3: k }, products: { [s]: 1, 'NH4+': k } })
}
r({ id: 'nh3ppt:Ag+', type: 'complete', priority: 4, reactants: { 'Ag+': 2, NH3: 2 }, products: { 'Ag2O(s)': 1, 'NH4+': 2 } })

// ======================================================================= soluble in excess
// hydroxo complexes with excess strong base
r({ id: 'xs:Al(OH)3+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Al(OH)3(s)': 1, 'OH-': 1 }, products: { 'Al(OH)4-': 1 }, reserve: { 'OH-': 0.02 } })
r({ id: 'xs:Zn(OH)2+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Zn(OH)2(s)': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, products: { 'Zn(OH)4 2-': 1 }, reserve: { 'OH-': 0.02 } })
r({ id: 'xs:Pb(OH)2+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Pb(OH)2(s)': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, products: { 'Pb(OH)4 2-': 1 }, reserve: { 'OH-': 0.05 } })
r({ id: 'xs:Cr(OH)3+OH', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Cr(OH)3(s)': 1, 'OH-': 3 }, products: { 'Cr(OH)6 3-': 1 }, reserve: { 'OH-': 0.05 } })
// ammine complexes with excess ammonia
r({ id: 'xs:Cu(OH)2+NH3', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Cu(OH)2(s)': 1, NH3: 4 }, products: { 'Cu(NH3)4 2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, reserve: { NH3: 0.15 } })
r({ id: 'xs:Zn(OH)2+NH3', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Zn(OH)2(s)': 1, NH3: 4 }, products: { 'Zn(NH3)4 2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, reserve: { NH3: 0.1 } })
r({ id: 'xs:Ni(OH)2+NH3', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Ni(OH)2(s)': 1, NH3: 6 }, products: { 'Ni(NH3)6 2+': 1, 'OH-': 2 }, reserve: { NH3: 0.15 } })
r({ id: 'xs:Ag2O+NH3', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Ag2O(s)': 1, NH3: 4 }, products: { 'Ag(NH3)2+': 2, 'OH-': 2 }, reserve: { NH3: 0.05 } })
// silver halides in ammonia: AgX + 2NH3 ⇌ [Ag(NH3)2]+ + X-, K = Ksp·β2 (β2 = 1.6e7)
r({ id: 'eq:AgCl+NH3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 5, reactants: { 'AgCl(s)': 1, NH3: 2 }, products: { 'Ag(NH3)2+': 1, 'Cl-': 1 }, K: 1.8e-10 * 1.6e7 })
r({ id: 'eq:AgBr+NH3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 5, reactants: { 'AgBr(s)': 1, NH3: 2 }, products: { 'Ag(NH3)2+': 1, 'Br-': 1 }, K: 5e-13 * 1.6e7 })
r({ id: 'eq:AgI+NH3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 5, reactants: { 'AgI(s)': 1, NH3: 2 }, products: { 'Ag(NH3)2+': 1, 'I-': 1 }, K: 8.5e-17 * 1.6e7 })
// silver(I) and copper(I) with thiosulfate
r({ id: 'eq:Ag+S2O3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Ag+': 1, 'S2O3 2-': 2 }, products: { 'Ag(S2O3)2 3-': 1 }, K: 2.9e13 })
r({ id: 'eq:AgCl+S2O3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 5, reactants: { 'AgCl(s)': 1, 'S2O3 2-': 2 }, products: { 'Ag(S2O3)2 3-': 1, 'Cl-': 1 }, K: 1.8e-10 * 2.9e13 })
r({ id: 'eq:AgBr+S2O3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 5, reactants: { 'AgBr(s)': 1, 'S2O3 2-': 2 }, products: { 'Ag(S2O3)2 3-': 1, 'Br-': 1 }, K: 5e-13 * 2.9e13 })
r({ id: 'eq:AgI+S2O3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 5, reactants: { 'AgI(s)': 1, 'S2O3 2-': 2 }, products: { 'Ag(S2O3)2 3-': 1, 'I-': 1 }, K: 8.5e-17 * 2.9e13 })
r({ id: 'eq:CuI+S2O3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 5, reactants: { 'CuI(s)': 1, 'S2O3 2-': 2 }, products: { 'Cu(S2O3)2 3-': 1, 'I-': 1 }, K: 0.02 })

// acid destroys complexes
r({ id: 'acid:Al(OH)4', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Al(OH)4-': 1, 'H+': 1 }, products: { 'Al(OH)3(s)': 1 } })
r({ id: 'acid:Zn(OH)4', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Zn(OH)4 2-': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Zn(OH)2(s)': 1 } })
r({ id: 'acid:Pb(OH)4', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Pb(OH)4 2-': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Pb(OH)2(s)': 1 } })
r({ id: 'acid:Cr(OH)6', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Cr(OH)6 3-': 1, 'H+': 3 }, products: { 'Cr(OH)3(s)': 1 } })
r({ id: 'acid:Cu(NH3)4', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Cu(NH3)4 2+': 1, 'H+': 4 }, products: { 'Cu2+': 1, 'NH4+': 4 } })
r({ id: 'acid:Zn(NH3)4', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Zn(NH3)4 2+': 1, 'H+': 4 }, products: { 'Zn2+': 1, 'NH4+': 4 } })
r({ id: 'acid:Ni(NH3)6', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Ni(NH3)6 2+': 1, 'H+': 6 }, products: { 'Ni2+': 1, 'NH4+': 6 } })
r({ id: 'acid:Ag(NH3)2', type: 'complete', priority: 2, reactants: { 'Ag(NH3)2+': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Ag+': 1, 'NH4+': 2 } })

// acid dissolves hydroxides, oxides and carbonates
const acidDissolve: [string, Stoich, number][] = [
  ['Al(OH)3(s)', { 'Al3+': 1 }, 3], ['Cr(OH)3(s)', { 'Cr3+': 1 }, 3], ['Fe(OH)3(s)', { 'Fe3+': 1 }, 3],
  ['Fe(OH)2(s)', { 'Fe2+': 1 }, 2], ['Cu(OH)2(s)', { 'Cu2+': 1 }, 2], ['Zn(OH)2(s)', { 'Zn2+': 1 }, 2],
  ['Ni(OH)2(s)', { 'Ni2+': 1 }, 2], ['Co(OH)2(s)', { 'Co2+': 1 }, 2], ['Mn(OH)2(s)', { 'Mn2+': 1 }, 2],
  ['Pb(OH)2(s)', { 'Pb2+': 1 }, 2], ['Mg(OH)2(s)', { 'Mg2+': 1 }, 2], ['Ca(OH)2(s)', { 'Ca2+': 1 }, 2],
  ['Ba(OH)2(s)', { 'Ba2+': 1 }, 2], ['Ag2O(s)', { 'Ag+': 2 }, 2], ['CuO(s)', { 'Cu2+': 1 }, 2],
  ['ZnO(s)', { 'Zn2+': 1 }, 2], ['MgO(s)', { 'Mg2+': 1 }, 2], ['CaO(s)', { 'Ca2+': 1 }, 2], ['PbO(s)', { 'Pb2+': 1 }, 2],
  ['Fe2O3(s)', { 'Fe3+': 2 }, 6],
  ['BaSO3(s)', { 'Ba2+': 1, 'SO2(aq)': 1 }, 2], ['Ag2SO3(s)', { 'Ag+': 2, 'SO2(aq)': 1 }, 2],
]
for (const [s, prod, h] of acidDissolve) {
  r({ id: `acid:${s}`, type: 'complete', priority: 4, reactants: { [s]: 1, 'H+': h }, products: prod })
}
// Carbonates react with acid with effervescence. Solid carbonates dissolve over a few seconds.
const carbonates: [string, Stoich][] = [
  ['BaCO3(s)', { 'Ba2+': 1 }], ['CaCO3(s)', { 'Ca2+': 1 }], ['MgCO3(s)', { 'Mg2+': 1 }], ['ZnCO3(s)', { 'Zn2+': 1 }],
  ['CuCO3(s)', { 'Cu2+': 1 }], ['FeCO3(s)', { 'Fe2+': 1 }], ['NiCO3(s)', { 'Ni2+': 1 }], ['MnCO3(s)', { 'Mn2+': 1 }],
  ['PbCO3(s)', { 'Pb2+': 1 }], ['Ag2CO3(s)', { 'Ag+': 2 }],
]
for (const [s, prod] of carbonates) {
  r({ id: `acid:${s}`, type: 'kinetic', reactants: { [s]: 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { ...prod, 'CO2(aq)': 1 }, halfLife: 0.5 })
}

// ======================================================================= redox (fast)
// acidified manganate(VII)
r({ id: 'MnO4+Fe2+', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 1, 'Fe2+': 5, 'H+': 8 }, products: { 'Mn2+': 1, 'Fe3+': 5 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+SO3', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 2, 'SO3 2-': 5, 'H+': 6 }, products: { 'Mn2+': 2, 'SO4 2-': 5 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+SO2', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 2, 'SO2(aq)': 5 }, products: { 'Mn2+': 2, 'SO4 2-': 5, 'H+': 4 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+NO2', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 2, 'NO2-': 5, 'H+': 6 }, products: { 'Mn2+': 2, 'NO3-': 5 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+I-', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 2, 'I-': 10, 'H+': 16 }, products: { 'Mn2+': 2, I2: 5 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+H2O2', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 2, H2O2: 5, 'H+': 6 }, products: { 'Mn2+': 2, 'O2(g)': 5 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+S2O3', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 8, 'S2O3 2-': 5, 'H+': 14 }, products: { 'Mn2+': 8, 'SO4 2-': 10 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+C2O4', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'MnO4-': 2, 'C2O4 2-': 5, 'H+': 16 }, products: { 'Mn2+': 2, 'CO2(aq)': 10 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.T >= 55 ? 4 : x.n('Mn2+') > 0 ? 60 : 600) })
// manganate(VII) in neutral/alkaline solution is reduced to brown MnO2
r({ id: 'MnO4+SO3(alk)', type: 'complete', priority: 7, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 2, 'SO3 2-': 3 }, products: { 'MnO2(s)': 2, 'SO4 2-': 3, 'OH-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+I-(alk)', type: 'complete', priority: 7, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 2, 'I-': 6 }, products: { 'MnO2(s)': 2, I2: 3, 'OH-': 8 } })
r({ id: 'MnO4+Fe(OH)2', type: 'complete', priority: 7, reactants: { 'MnO4-': 1, 'Fe(OH)2(s)': 3 }, products: { 'MnO2(s)': 1, 'Fe(OH)3(s)': 3, 'OH-': 1 } })
// Manganate(VI) disproportionates rapidly in acid. Fresh water extracts of heated KMnO4
// remain visibly green while shaken/settled (9701_w23_31/35 Q2(b)(i)); do not turn them
// purple within a mixing step. Dilute water extracts (9701_w24_34 Q3(a)(ii)) turn purple;
// concentrated extracts stay green initially. This concentration threshold and neutral
// half-life reproduce those observation windows, rather than a fitted kinetic law.
r({ id: 'MnO4 2- disproportionation', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'MnO4 2-': 3 }, products: { 'MnO4-': 2, 'MnO2(s)': 1, 'OH-': 4 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('OH-') > 0.5 ? Infinity : x.c('H+') > 1e-4 ? 0.2 : x.c('MnO4 2-') >= 0.05 ? 600 : 0.5) })

// iron(III) and copper(II) oxidise iodide
r({ id: 'Fe3++I-', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'Fe3+': 2, 'I-': 2 }, products: { 'Fe2+': 2, I2: 1 } })
r({ id: 'Cu2++I-', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'Cu2+': 2, 'I-': 4 }, products: { 'CuI(s)': 2, I2: 1 } })
// dichromate(VI) in acid
r({ id: 'Cr2O7+Fe2+', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'Cr2O7 2-': 1, 'Fe2+': 6, 'H+': 14 }, products: { 'Cr3+': 2, 'Fe3+': 6 } })
r({ id: 'Cr2O7+SO3', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'Cr2O7 2-': 1, 'SO3 2-': 3, 'H+': 8 }, products: { 'Cr3+': 2, 'SO4 2-': 3 } })
r({ id: 'Cr2O7+SO2', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'Cr2O7 2-': 1, 'SO2(aq)': 3, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Cr3+': 2, 'SO4 2-': 3 } })
r({ id: 'Cr2O7+I-', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'Cr2O7 2-': 1, 'I-': 6, 'H+': 14 }, products: { 'Cr3+': 2, I2: 3 } })
r({ id: 'Cr2O7+H2O2', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Cr2O7 2-': 1, H2O2: 3, 'H+': 8 }, products: { 'Cr3+': 2, 'O2(g)': 3 }, halfLife: 8 })
// iodine / thiosulfate / starch
r({ id: 'Cl2+I-', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Cl2(aq)': 1, 'I-': 2 }, products: { I2: 1, 'Cl-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'Cl2+Br-', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'Cl2(aq)': 1, 'Br-': 2 }, products: { 'Br2(aq)': 1, 'Cl-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'Cl2+Fe2+', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'Cl2(aq)': 1, 'Fe2+': 2 }, products: { 'Fe3+': 2, 'Cl-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'I2+S2O3', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { I2: 1, 'S2O3 2-': 2 }, products: { 'I-': 2, 'S4O6 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'starchI2+S2O3', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'starch-I2': 1, 'S2O3 2-': 2 }, products: { starch: 1, 'I-': 2, 'S4O6 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'starch+I2', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 8, reactants: { starch: 1, I2: 1 }, products: { 'starch-I2': 1 }, K: 2e5 })
// chlorate(I) oxidises iodide (and iron(II))
r({ id: 'ClO+Fe2+', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { 'ClO-': 1, 'Fe2+': 2 }, products: { 'Fe3+': 2, 'Cl-': 1, 'OH-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'ClO+S2O3', type: 'complete', priority: 5, reactants: { 'ClO-': 4, 'S2O3 2-': 1 }, products: { 'SO4 2-': 2, 'Cl-': 4, 'H+': 2 } })
// hydrogen peroxide as oxidant
r({ id: 'H2O2+Fe2+', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { H2O2: 1, 'Fe2+': 2 }, products: { 'Fe3+': 2, 'OH-': 2 } })
r({ id: 'H2O2+Fe(OH)2', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { H2O2: 1, 'Fe(OH)2(s)': 2 }, products: { 'Fe(OH)3(s)': 2 } })
r({ id: 'H2O2+Mn(OH)2', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { H2O2: 1, 'Mn(OH)2(s)': 1 }, products: { 'MnO(OH)2(s)': 1 } })
r({ id: 'H2O2+SO3', type: 'complete', priority: 6, reactants: { H2O2: 1, 'SO3 2-': 1 }, products: { 'SO4 2-': 1 } })
r({ id: 'H2O2+I-', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { H2O2: 1, 'I-': 2 }, products: { I2: 1, 'OH-': 2 }, halfLife: (x) => (x.c('H+') > 1e-3 ? 6 : 60) })
r({ id: 'H2O2+Cr(OH)6', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { H2O2: 3, 'Cr(OH)6 3-': 2 }, products: { 'CrO4 2-': 2, 'OH-': 2 }, halfLife: (x) => (x.T > 50 ? 6 : 40) })
// peroxodisulfate oxidises iodide (slowly, faster with Fe catalyst)
r({ id: 'S2O8+I-', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'S2O8 2-': 1, 'I-': 2 }, products: { 'SO4 2-': 2, I2: 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.n('Fe2+') + x.n('Fe3+') > 1e-4 ? 8 : 45) })
// nitrite
r({ id: 'NO2+I-+H+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'NO2-': 2, 'I-': 2, 'H+': 4 }, products: { I2: 1, 'NO(g)': 2 }, halfLife: 3 })
r({ id: 'HNO2 decomposition', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'NO2-': 3, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'NO3-': 1, 'NO(g)': 2 }, halfLife: 6,
  events: ['brown gas seen at the mouth of the tube'] })

// iron(III) + thiosulfate: transient dark violet complex that fades as Fe3+ is reduced
r({ id: 'eq:FeS2O3', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 8, reactants: { 'Fe3+': 1, 'S2O3 2-': 1 }, products: { 'FeS2O3+': 1 }, K: 300 })
r({ id: 'FeS2O3 fade', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'FeS2O3+': 2 }, products: { 'Fe2+': 2, 'S4O6 2-': 1 }, halfLife: 25 })
// iron(III) thiocyanate
r({ id: 'eq:FeSCN', type: 'equilibrium', priority: 8, reactants: { 'Fe3+': 1, 'SCN-': 1 }, products: { 'FeSCN2+': 1 }, K: 140 })

// ======================================================================= metals
const metalRate = (base: number) => (x: RuleCtx) => (x.T > 50 ? base / 3 : base)
r({ id: 'Mg+H+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Mg(s)': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Mg2+': 1, 'H2(g)': 1 }, halfLife: metalRate(8) })
r({ id: 'Zn+H+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Zn(s)': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'Zn2+': 1, 'H2(g)': 1 }, halfLife: metalRate(25) })
r({ id: 'Al+H+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Al(s)': 2, 'H+': 6 }, products: { 'Al3+': 2, 'H2(g)': 3 }, halfLife: metalRate(200) })
r({ id: 'Mg+Cu2+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Mg(s)': 1, 'Cu2+': 1 }, products: { 'Mg2+': 1, 'Cu(s)': 1 }, halfLife: 25 })
r({ id: 'Zn+Cu2+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Zn(s)': 1, 'Cu2+': 1 }, products: { 'Zn2+': 1, 'Cu(s)': 1 }, halfLife: 20 })
r({ id: 'Mg+Fe2+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Mg(s)': 1, 'Fe2+': 1 }, products: { 'Mg2+': 1, 'Fe(s)': 1 }, halfLife: 400 })
r({ id: 'Mg+Fe3+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Mg(s)': 1, 'Fe3+': 2 }, products: { 'Mg2+': 1, 'Fe2+': 2 }, halfLife: 40 })
r({ id: 'Zn+Fe3+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Zn(s)': 1, 'Fe3+': 2 }, products: { 'Zn2+': 1, 'Fe2+': 2 }, halfLife: 40 })
r({ id: 'Mg+Ag+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Mg(s)': 1, 'Ag+': 2 }, products: { 'Mg2+': 1, 'Ag(s)': 2 }, halfLife: 15 })
r({ id: 'Mg+NH4+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Mg(s)': 1, 'NH4+': 2 }, products: { 'Mg2+': 1, NH3: 2, 'H2(g)': 1 }, halfLife: metalRate(150) })
// solutions of hydrolysing cations are acidic enough to make magnesium fizz slowly
r({ id: 'Mg+acidic solution', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Mg(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Mg2+': 1, 'OH-': 2, 'H2(g)': 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.pH() < 4 ? 40 : x.pH() < 6.2 ? 120 : Infinity) })
// aluminium in alkali: hydrogen always; nitrate / nitrite reduced to ammonia on warming
r({ id: 'Al+OH', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Al(s)': 2, 'OH-': 2 }, products: { 'Al(OH)4-': 2, 'H2(g)': 3 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('OH-') < 0.02 ? Infinity : x.T > 45 ? 60 : 200) })
r({ id: 'Al+NO3+OH', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Al(s)': 8, 'NO3-': 3, 'OH-': 5 }, products: { 'Al(OH)4-': 8, NH3: 3 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('OH-') < 0.02 ? Infinity : x.T > 45 ? 10 : 400) })
r({ id: 'Al+NO2+OH', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Al(s)': 2, 'NO2-': 1, 'OH-': 1 }, products: { 'Al(OH)4-': 2, NH3: 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('OH-') < 0.02 ? Infinity : x.T > 45 ? 12 : 300) })

// ======================================================================= slow processes
// air oxidation of iron(II) and manganese(II) hydroxides (starts at the surface)
r({ id: 'air:Fe(OH)2', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Fe(OH)2(s)': 1 }, products: { 'Fe(OH)3(s)': 1 }, halfLife: 900 })
r({ id: 'air:Mn(OH)2', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Mn(OH)2(s)': 1 }, products: { 'MnO(OH)2(s)': 1 }, halfLife: 45 })
// copper(II) hydroxide turns to black CuO when the mixture is warmed
r({ id: 'warm:Cu(OH)2', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Cu(OH)2(s)': 1 }, products: { 'CuO(s)': 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.T >= 55 ? 10 : Infinity) })
// chromium(III) hydroxide very slowly dissolves in excess ammonia (purple solution on standing)
r({ id: 'slow:Cr(OH)3+NH3', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Cr(OH)3(s)': 1, NH3: 6 }, products: { 'Cr(NH3)6 3+': 1, 'OH-': 3 },
  reserve: { NH3: 0.3 }, halfLife: 500 })
// thiosulfate + acid: sulfur slowly appears
// (sulfur first forms invisible nuclei, so the cloudiness appears only after a delay)
r({ id: 'S2O3+H+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'S2O3 2-': 1, 'H+': 2 }, products: { 'S-nuclei': 1, 'SO2(aq)': 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.T > 50 ? 12 : 45) })
r({ id: 'S nuclei grow', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'S-nuclei': 1 }, products: { 'S(s)': 1 }, halfLife: 30 })
// dilute nitric acid slowly oxidises iodide, iron(II) and sulfite
r({ id: 'HNO3+I-', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'I-': 6, 'NO3-': 2, 'H+': 8 }, products: { I2: 3, 'NO(g)': 2 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('H+') > 0.3 ? (x.T > 50 ? 3000 : 30000) : Infinity) })
r({ id: 'HNO3+Fe2+', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Fe2+': 3, 'NO3-': 1, 'H+': 4 }, products: { 'Fe3+': 3, 'NO(g)': 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('H+') > 0.3 ? (x.T > 50 ? 60 : 3000) : Infinity) })
r({ id: 'HNO3+SO2', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'SO2(aq)': 3, 'NO3-': 2 }, products: { 'SO4 2-': 3, 'NO(g)': 2, 'H+': 4 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.c('H+') > 0.3 ? (x.T > 50 ? 60 : 900) : Infinity) })

// catalytic decomposition of hydrogen peroxide
r({ id: 'H2O2 decomposition', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { H2O2: 2 }, products: { 'O2(g)': 1 },
  halfLife: (x) => {
    let k = 0
    if (x.n('MnO2(s)') > 1e-4) k += Math.LN2 / 2
    if (x.n('Fe3+') > 1e-4 || x.n('Fe(OH)3(s)') > 1e-4) k += Math.LN2 / 15
    if (x.n('Cu(NH3)4 2+') > 1e-4 || x.n('CuO(s)') > 1e-4) k += Math.LN2 / 8
    if (x.n('Cu(OH)2(s)') > 1e-4) k += Math.LN2 / 25
    if (x.c('OH-') > 0.1) k += Math.LN2 / 300
    if (x.T > 60) k *= 4
    return k > 0 ? Math.LN2 / k : Infinity
  } })
// peroxide decomposing in ammoniacal copper(II) leaves a black copper(II) oxide residue
r({ id: 'Cu(NH3)4 + H2O2 -> CuO', type: 'kinetic', reactants: { 'Cu(NH3)4 2+': 1 }, products: { 'CuO(s)': 1, 'NH4+': 2, NH3: 2 },
  halfLife: (x) => (x.n('H2O2') > 1e-3 ? 12 : Infinity) })

// ======================================================================= heating dry solids
function thermalRules(speciesId: string, steps: { minTemp: number; halfLife: number; products: Stoich; events?: string[]; coReactants?: Stoich }[]) {
  steps.forEach((s, i) => {
    r({
      id: `heat:${speciesId}:${i}`,
      type: 'kinetic',
      dry: true,
      reactants: { [speciesId]: 1, ...(s.coReactants ?? {}) },
      products: s.products,
      minTemp: s.minTemp,
      halfLife: s.halfLife,
      events: s.events,
    })
  })
}
for (const d of Object.values(COMPOUNDS)) {
  if (d.thermal && !d.asSpecies) thermalRules(`cmp:${d.id}`, d.thermal)
}
for (const [sp, steps] of Object.entries(SOLID_THERMAL)) thermalRules(sp, steps)
for (const m of MIXED_THERMAL) {
  r({
    id: `heat-mixed:${m.reactant}`,
    type: 'kinetic',
    dry: true,
    reactants: { [m.reactant]: 1 },
    products: m.step.products,
    minTemp: m.step.minTemp,
    halfLife: m.step.halfLife,
    events: m.step.events,
    when: (x) => m.needs.some((s) => x.n(s) > 1e-4),
  })
}

export const FAST_RULES = RULES.filter((x) => x.type !== 'kinetic').sort((a, b) => (a.priority ?? 9) - (b.priority ?? 9))
export const SLOW_RULES = RULES.filter((x) => x.type === 'kinetic')

===== END FILE =====

// Species catalogue: every ion, molecule, precipitate and gas the engine tracks.
//
// Aqueous colours use a simple Beer–Lambert model: `tint` is the apparent colour of the
// species at `refConc` (mol dm-3) viewed through a test-tube (~1.3 cm). Mixtures are
// obtained by adding absorbances, so colours combine physically (e.g. yellow Fe3+ with
// pink MnO4- gives orange).

export type Phase = 'aq' | 's' | 'g' | 'l'

export type FlameElement = 'Li' | 'Na' | 'K' | 'Ca' | 'Ba' | 'Cu' | 'Sr'

export interface SpeciesDef {
  id: string
  formula: string
  name: string
  phase: Phase
  charge?: number
  /** aqueous: apparent colour at refConc */
  tint?: string
  refConc?: number
  /** exam-style colour word(s); for aqueous species optionally concentration dependent */
  colourName?: string | ((conc: number) => string)
  /** solids: body colour */
  colour?: string
  /** solids: how visible a given amount is (scattering); default 1 */
  visibility?: number
  /** colour of a solid while hot (thermochromism), e.g. ZnO */
  hotColour?: string
  hotColourName?: string
  flame?: FlameElement
  /** fraction of the total absorbance at which this species names the solution colour (default 0.72) */
  nameDominance?: number
  /** gases: visible colour */
  gasColour?: string
  gasColourName?: string
}

const defs: SpeciesDef[] = []
const add = (d: SpeciesDef) => defs.push(d)

add({ id: 'HCl(g)', formula: 'HCl', name: 'hydrogen chloride', phase: 'g' })
add({ id: 'NO(g)', formula: 'NO', name: 'nitrogen monoxide', phase: 'g' })
add({ id: 'NO2(g)', formula: 'NO₂', name: 'nitrogen dioxide', phase: 'g', gasColour: '#9a4a1c', gasColourName: 'brown' })
add({ id: 'Cl2(g)', formula: 'Cl₂', name: 'chlorine', phase: 'g', gasColour: '#c9d65a', gasColourName: 'pale yellow-green' })
add({ id: 'I2(g)', formula: 'I₂', name: 'iodine vapour', phase: 'g', gasColour: '#6a2a8a', gasColourName: 'purple' })
add({ id: 'H2O(g)', formula: 'H₂O', name: 'water vapour', phase: 'g' })

export const SPECIES: Record<string, SpeciesDef> = {}
for (const d of defs) SPECIES[d.id] = d

export function registerSpecies(d: SpeciesDef) {
  SPECIES[d.id] = d
}

export function species(id: string): SpeciesDef {
  const s = SPECIES[id]
  if (!s) throw new Error(`Unknown species: ${id}`)
  return s
}

export function colourNameOf(id: string, conc = 0.1): string | undefined {
  const s = species(id)
  if (typeof s.colourName === 'function') return s.colourName(conc)
  return s.colourName
}

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/engine/vessel.ts", "bytes": 16263, "sha256": "b70575c3f6c8ccabdd8d5c5c29e0726626e0f622aecd8bdd77d74868dca69ffb"} =====
import { FAST_RULES, SLOW_RULES, type Rule, type RuleCtx } from './rules'
import { SPECIES } from './species'

export type VesselKind = 'test-tube' | 'boiling-tube' | 'hard-glass-tube' | 'filter-paper'

export interface VesselSpec {
  label: string
  /** cross-section area (cm²): converts "x cm depth" to a volume */
  area: number
  /** capacity (cm³) before the tube overflows */
  capacity: number
  /** can be heated strongly */
  heatProof: boolean
}

export const VESSEL_SPECS: Record<VesselKind, VesselSpec> = {
  'test-tube': { label: 'test-tube', area: 1.3, capacity: 14, heatProof: false },
  'boiling-tube': { label: 'boiling tube', area: 3.6, capacity: 48, heatProof: true },
  'hard-glass-tube': { label: 'hard-glass test-tube', area: 1.3, capacity: 14, heatProof: true },
  'filter-paper': { label: 'filter paper', area: 10, capacity: 2, heatProof: false },
}

/** Below this volume (cm³) a vessel is treated as dry. */
export const DRY_VOLUME = 0.05
export const ROOM_T = 20

export type HeatMode = 'warm' | 'boil' | 'gentle' | 'strong'
const HEAT_TARGET: Record<HeatMode, number> = { warm: 65, boil: 100, gentle: 260, strong: 600 }
/** effective flame temperature for a liquid being boiled (reaches 100 °C in about 35 s) */
const BOIL_DRIVE = 125

export interface EngineEvent {
  kind: 'rule' | 'gas' | 'condensation' | 'overflow' | 'boil' | 'dry'
  ruleId?: string
  text?: string
  species?: string
  amount: number
  time: number
}

/** Volatile dissolved gases: Henry constant at 25 °C (mol dm⁻³ atm⁻¹) and van 't Hoff temperature. */
const VOLATILE: Record<string, { gas: string; H25: number; vantHoff: number; rate: number }> = {
  NH3: { gas: 'NH3(g)', H25: 57, vantHoff: 4100, rate: 0.35 },
  'SO2(aq)': { gas: 'SO2(g)', H25: 1.2, vantHoff: 2900, rate: 0.05 },
}

let vesselCounter = 0

export class Vessel {
  id: string
  kind: VesselKind
  label: string
  /** amounts in mmol (aqueous species, solids and dissolved gases) */
  n = new Map<string, number>()
  /** liquid volume, cm³ */
  V = 0
  /** temperature, °C */
  T = ROOM_T
  heating: HeatMode | null = null
  stoppered = false
  /** gases near the mouth of the tube, mmol */
  headspace = new Map<string, number>()
  /** recent bubbling rate (mmol s⁻¹) used for animation */
  bubbleRate = 0
  /** droplets of condensation on the cool part of the tube (arbitrary units, decays) */
  condensation = 0
  /** fraction of precipitate that has settled to the bottom (0 = fully suspended) */
  settled = 0
  /** seconds since the vessel was created */
  age = 0
  /** set when the vessel has been emptied but not rinsed: tiny residue remains */
  log: EngineEvent[] = []

  constructor(kind: VesselKind, label?: string) {
    this.kind = kind
    this.id = `v${++vesselCounter}`
    this.label = label ?? VESSEL_SPECS[kind].label
  }

  get spec() {
    return VESSEL_SPECS[this.kind]
  }

  get wet() {
    return this.V >= DRY_VOLUME
  }

  amount(id: string) {
    return this.n.get(id) ?? 0
  }

  conc(id: string) {
    return this.wet ? this.amount(id) / this.V : 0
  }

  change(id: string, delta: number) {
    const v = (this.n.get(id) ?? 0) + delta
    if (v <= 1e-15) this.n.delete(id)
    else this.n.set(id, v)
  }

  clone(): Vessel {
    const v = new Vessel(this.kind, this.label)
    v.id = this.id
    v.n = new Map(this.n)
    v.V = this.V
    v.T = this.T
    v.heating = this.heating
    v.stoppered = this.stoppered
    v.headspace = new Map(this.headspace)
    v.bubbleRate = this.bubbleRate
    v.condensation = this.condensation
    v.settled = this.settled
    v.age = this.age
    return v
  }

  isEmpty() {
    return this.V < 1e-6 && [...this.n.values()].every((x) => x < 1e-9)
  }

  /** Remove a fraction of the liquid together with dissolved species and (suspended) solids. */
  takeFraction(f: number, includeSolids: boolean): { n: Map<string, number>; V: number } {
    f = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, f))
    const out = new Map<string, number>()
    for (const [id, amt] of this.n) {
      const sp = SPECIES[id]
      const isSolid = sp?.phase === 's'
      if (isSolid && !includeSolids) continue
      // settled precipitate is harder to pour off
      const frac = isSolid ? f * (1 - 0.6 * this.settled) : f
      const moved = amt * frac
      if (moved > 0) {
        out.set(id, moved)
        this.change(id, -moved)
      }
    }
    const V = this.V * f
    this.V -= V
    return { n: out, V }
  }

  /** Add material; overflow is reported by the caller. */
  receive(n: Map<string, number> | Record<string, number>, V: number, temperature = ROOM_T) {
    // A volume-weighted approximation; do not reset a transferred hot solution to room temperature.
    if (V > 0) this.T = (this.T * this.V + temperature * V) / (this.V + V)
    const entries = n instanceof Map ? [...n.entries()] : Object.entries(n)
    for (const [id, amt] of entries) this.change(id, amt)
    this.V += V
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ chemistry
  ctx(): RuleCtx {
    return {
      T: this.T,
      V: this.V,
      wet: this.wet,
      n: (id) => this.amount(id),
      c: (id) => this.conc(id),
      pH: () => estimatePH(this),
    }
  }

  /** Run fast (complete / equilibrium) rules until nothing changes. */
  settle(time: number) {
    if (!this.wet) return
    const ctx = this.ctx()
    // Sweep the rules in priority order until nothing changes. Within a sweep higher-priority
    // rules always act first (e.g. neutralisation before precipitation).
    for (let sweep = 0; sweep < 300; sweep++) {
      let changed = false
      for (const rule of FAST_RULES) {
        if (!ruleApplicable(rule, this, ctx)) continue
        const xi = rule.type === 'complete' ? completeExtent(rule, this) : equilibriumExtent(rule, this)
        if (!significant(rule, this, xi)) continue
        applyRule(rule, this, xi, time)
        changed = true
      }
      if (!changed) break
    }
    this.liberateGases(time)
  }

  /** Advance time by dt seconds: temperature, kinetics, gas loss, settling. */
  advance(dt: number, time: number) {
    let remaining = dt
    while (remaining > 1e-9) {
      const h = Math.min(remaining, remaining > 60 ? 4 : 1)
      remaining -= h
      this.age += h
      this.updateTemperature(h, time)
      const ctx = this.ctx()
      for (const rule of SLOW_RULES) {
        if (!ruleApplicable(rule, this, ctx)) continue
        const t12 = typeof rule.halfLife === 'function' ? rule.halfLife(ctx) : rule.halfLife ?? Infinity
        if (!isFinite(t12)) continue
        const max = completeExtent(rule, this)
        if (max <= 0) continue
        const xi = max * (1 - Math.exp((-Math.LN2 * h) / t12))
        if (xi < 1e-10) continue
        applyRule(rule, this, xi, time + (dt - remaining))
      }
      this.settle(time + (dt - remaining))
      this.volatilise(h, time + (dt - remaining))
      // head-space gas drifts away unless stoppered
      if (!this.stoppered) {
        const k = Math.exp((-Math.LN2 * h) / 15)
        for (const [g, a] of this.headspace) {
          const na = a * k
          if (na < 1e-7) this.headspace.delete(g)
          else this.headspace.set(g, na)
        }
      }
      this.condensation *= this.heating ? 1 : Math.exp((-Math.LN2 * h) / 240)
      this.bubbleRate *= Math.exp((-Math.LN2 * h) / 2)
      if (this.wet) this.settled = Math.min(1, this.settled + (1 - this.settled) * (1 - Math.exp((-Math.LN2 * h) / 90)))
    }
  }

  private updateTemperature(h: number, time: number) {
    let target = this.heating ? HEAT_TARGET[this.heating] : ROOM_T
    // A flame hot enough to boil water drives the liquid above 100 °C; the clamp below then turns
    // the excess heat into evaporation instead of leaving the temperature just short of 100 °C.
    if (this.wet && target >= 100) target = Math.max(target, BOIL_DRIVE)
    const tau = this.heating ? (this.wet ? 25 : 12) : this.wet ? 70 : 45
    let T = this.T + (target - this.T) * (1 - Math.exp(-h / tau))
    if (this.wet && T >= 100) {
      T = 100
      // boiling away water
      const dV = Math.min(this.V, (this.heating === 'boil' ? 0.02 : 0.04) * h)
      this.V -= dV
      if (dV > 0) this.log.push({ kind: 'boil', amount: dV, time })
      if (!this.wet) {
        this.V = 0
        this.log.push({ kind: 'dry', amount: 0, time })
      }
    }
    this.T = T
  }

  /** Gases that are insoluble (or over-saturated) leave immediately as bubbles. */
  liberateGases(time: number) {
    const co2 = this.amount('CO2(aq)')
    if (co2 > 0 && this.wet) {
      const sat = 0.04 * Math.exp(-0.022 * (this.T - 20)) * this.V
      if (co2 > sat) {
        const excess = co2 - sat
        this.change('CO2(aq)', -excess)
        this.addGas('CO2(g)', excess, time)
      }
    } else if (co2 > 0 && !this.wet) {
      this.change('CO2(aq)', -co2)
      this.addGas('CO2(g)', co2, time)
    }
  }

  /** Soluble gases (NH3, SO2) escape gradually, much faster when hot. */
  private volatilise(h: number, time: number) {
    void time
    for (const [id, p] of Object.entries(VOLATILE)) {
      const amt = this.amount(id)
      if (amt <= 0) continue
      if (!this.wet) {
        this.change(id, -amt)
        this.addGas(p.gas, amt, time, false)
        continue
      }
      // escaping vapour is detected through the vapour pressure (see Lab.gasTest), not as bubbles
      const pressure = vapourPressure(this, id)
      const loss = Math.min(amt, p.rate * pressure * h * (this.stoppered ? 0.05 : 1))
      if (loss > 0) this.change(id, -loss)
    }
  }

  addGas(id: string, amount: number, time: number, bubbles = true) {
    if (amount <= 0) return
    let gas = id
    // nitrogen monoxide meets air at the mouth of the tube and turns brown
    if (gas === 'NO(g)') gas = 'NO2(g)'
    if (gas === 'H2O(g)') {
      this.condensation += amount
      this.log.push({ kind: 'condensation', amount, time })
      return
    }
    this.headspace.set(gas, (this.headspace.get(gas) ?? 0) + amount)
    if (bubbles && this.wet) this.bubbleRate += amount
    this.log.push({ kind: 'gas', species: gas, amount, time })
  }

  startHeating(mode: HeatMode | null) {
    this.heating = mode
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------- rule helpers
function ruleApplicable(rule: Rule, v: Vessel, ctx: RuleCtx): boolean {
  if (rule.dry) {
    if (v.wet) return false
    if (rule.minTemp !== undefined && v.T < rule.minTemp) return false
  } else if (!rule.anyPhase && !v.wet) return false
  if (rule.minTemp !== undefined && !rule.dry && v.T < rule.minTemp) return false
  if (rule.type === 'equilibrium') {
    // needs either all reactants or all products present to move
    const hasR = Object.keys(rule.reactants).every((s) => v.amount(s) > 0)
    const hasP = Object.keys(rule.products).every((s) => v.amount(s) > 0)
    if (!hasR && !hasP) return false
  } else {
    for (const s of Object.keys(rule.reactants)) if (v.amount(s) <= 0) return false
  }
  if (rule.when && !rule.when(ctx)) return false
  return true
}

function completeExtent(rule: Rule, v: Vessel): number {
  let xi = Infinity
  for (const [s, nu] of Object.entries(rule.reactants)) {
    const reserve = rule.reserve?.[s] ? rule.reserve[s] * v.V : 0
    xi = Math.min(xi, (v.amount(s) - reserve) / nu)
  }
  return xi === Infinity ? 0 : Math.max(0, xi)
}

const isAqueous = (id: string) => {
  const p = SPECIES[id]?.phase
  return p === 'aq'
}

/** Solve Q(ξ) = K for a single reaction by bisection (Q is monotonic in ξ). */
function equilibriumExtent(rule: Rule, v: Vessel): number {
  const K = rule.K ?? 1
  let hi = Infinity
  for (const [s, nu] of Object.entries(rule.reactants)) hi = Math.min(hi, v.amount(s) / nu)
  let lo = -Infinity
  for (const [s, nu] of Object.entries(rule.products)) lo = Math.max(lo, -v.amount(s) / nu)
  if (!isFinite(hi)) hi = 0
  if (!isFinite(lo)) lo = 0
  if (hi - lo <= 1e-14) return 0
  const V = v.V
  const lnK = Math.log(K)
  const f = (xi: number) => {
    let lnQ = 0
    for (const [s, nu] of Object.entries(rule.products)) {
      if (!isAqueous(s)) continue
      lnQ += nu * Math.log(Math.max(v.amount(s) + nu * xi, 1e-300) / V)
    }
    for (const [s, nu] of Object.entries(rule.reactants)) {
      if (!isAqueous(s)) continue
      lnQ -= nu * Math.log(Math.max(v.amount(s) - nu * xi, 1e-300) / V)
    }
    return lnQ - lnK
  }
  if (f(lo) >= 0) return lo
  if (f(hi) <= 0) return hi
  let a = lo
  let b = hi
  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
    const m = 0.5 * (a + b)
    if (m === a || m === b) break
    if (f(m) > 0) b = m
    else a = m
  }
  return 0.5 * (a + b)
}

function significant(rule: Rule, v: Vessel, xi: number): boolean {
  const a = Math.abs(xi)
  // tiny equilibrium shifts (e.g. Ksp re-dissolution of 1e-7 M) are invisible and only cause churn
  if (a < (rule.type === 'equilibrium' ? 1e-6 : 1e-9)) return false
  let scale = 0
  for (const s of Object.keys(rule.reactants)) scale = Math.max(scale, v.amount(s))
  for (const s of Object.keys(rule.products)) scale = Math.max(scale, v.amount(s))
  return a > (rule.type === 'equilibrium' ? 1e-5 : 1e-7) * scale
}

function applyRule(rule: Rule, v: Vessel, xi: number, time: number) {
  for (const [s, nu] of Object.entries(rule.reactants)) v.change(s, -nu * xi)
  for (const [s, nu] of Object.entries(rule.products)) {
    if (SPECIES[s]?.phase === 'g') v.addGas(s, nu * xi, time)
    else v.change(s, nu * xi)
  }
  v.log.push({ kind: 'rule', ruleId: rule.id, amount: xi, time })
  if (rule.events?.length && xi > 1e-4) {
    for (const e of rule.events) v.log.push({ kind: 'rule', ruleId: rule.id, text: e, amount: xi, time })
  }
  if (xi > 0) {
    // newly formed precipitate is suspended
    for (const s of Object.keys(rule.products)) {
      if (SPECIES[s]?.phase === 's' && v.wet) v.settled *= 0.7
    }
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------- gases & pH
export function henry(id: string, T: number): number {
  const p = VOLATILE[id]
  if (!p) return Infinity
  const TK = T + 273.15
  return p.H25 * Math.exp(p.vantHoff * (1 / TK - 1 / 298.15))
}

/** Partial pressure (atm) of a volatile solute above the solution. */
export function vapourPressure(v: Vessel, id: string): number {
  if (!v.wet) return 0
  return v.conc(id) / henry(id, v.T)
}

const ACIDIC_CATIONS: Record<string, number> = {
  'Fe3+': 2.2, 'Cr3+': 4.0, 'Al3+': 5.0, 'Cu2+': 7.5, 'Pb2+': 7.7, 'Zn2+': 9.0, 'Fe2+': 9.5,
  'Ni2+': 9.9, 'Co2+': 9.7, 'Mn2+': 10.6, 'Mg2+': 11.4, 'Ag+': 12,
}

/** Rough pH estimate (good to about ±1 unit), adequate for indicator paper and conditions. */
export function estimatePH(v: Vessel): number {
  if (!v.wet) return 7
  const c = (id: string) => v.conc(id)
  const H = c('H+')
  const OH = c('OH-')
  if (H > 1e-6) return -Math.log10(H)
  if (OH > 1e-6) return 14 + Math.log10(OH)
  const acid: number[] = []
  const base: number[] = []
  const NH3 = c('NH3')
  const NH4 = c('NH4+')
  if (NH3 > 1e-6 && NH4 > 1e-6) return 9.25 + Math.log10(NH3 / NH4)
  if (NH3 > 1e-6) base.push(14 - 0.5 * (4.75 - Math.log10(NH3)))
  if (NH4 > 1e-6) acid.push(0.5 * (9.25 - Math.log10(NH4)))
  const CO3 = c('CO3 2-')
  const HCO3 = c('HCO3-')
  const CO2 = c('CO2(aq)')
  if (CO3 > 1e-6 && HCO3 > 1e-6) return 10.33 + Math.log10(CO3 / HCO3)
  if (CO3 > 1e-6) base.push(14 - 0.5 * (3.67 - Math.log10(CO3)))
  if (HCO3 > 1e-6) base.push(8.3)
  if (CO2 > 1e-5) acid.push(0.5 * (6.35 - Math.log10(CO2)))
  const SO3 = c('SO3 2-')
  if (SO3 > 1e-6) base.push(14 - 0.5 * (6.8 - Math.log10(SO3)))
  if (c('SO2(aq)') > 1e-5) acid.push(0.5 * (1.8 - Math.log10(c('SO2(aq)'))))
  if (c('ClO-') > 1e-5) base.push(14 - 0.5 * (6.5 - Math.log10(c('ClO-'))))
  if (c('S2O3 2-') > 1e-3) base.push(7.5)
  if (c('NO2-') > 1e-3) base.push(14 - 0.5 * (10.7 - Math.log10(c('NO2-'))))
  for (const [ion, pKa] of Object.entries(ACIDIC_CATIONS)) {
    const x = c(ion)
    if (x > 1e-5) acid.push(0.5 * (pKa - Math.log10(x)))
  }
  if (acid.length && !base.length) return Math.max(1, Math.min(7, Math.min(...acid)))
  if (base.length && !acid.length) return Math.min(13.5, Math.max(7, Math.max(...base)))
  if (acid.length && base.length) return (Math.min(...acid) + Math.max(...base)) / 2
  return 7
}

import type { ActionResult, Lab } from '../engine'

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/papers/archive.ts", "bytes": 1656, "sha256": "956f07d0df56799ea096601974f20d52d46df6faf56ffaa81b73226114fa1714"} =====
import { ARCHIVE_DATA } from './archive-data'
import { RECENT_BENCHES } from './recent-benches'
import type { PaperCase, PaperSource } from './types'

export const PAPER_SOURCES: readonly PaperSource[] = ARCHIVE_DATA
export const sourceById = (id: string) => PAPER_SOURCES.find(p => p.id === id)
export const seriesName = (source: PaperSource) => `${({ m: 'February/March', s: 'May/June', w: 'October/November' } as Record<string, string>)[source.session]} ${source.year}`

export function sourceCase(source: PaperSource): PaperCase {
  const bench = RECENT_BENCHES[source.id]
  return {
    id: source.id, source, coverage: bench ? 'configured' : 'source-only',
    syllabus: source.code === '0620' ? 'Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620' : 'Cambridge International AS & A Level Chemistry 9701',
    level: source.code === '0620' ? 'IGCSE' : 'AS & A Level',
    series: seriesName(source), component: `Paper ${source.paper}`, question: source.question,
    totalMarks: source.totalMarks,
    summary: bench ? 'Original question and mark scheme, with samples prepared for free bench practice.' : 'Original question, written practice and published mark scheme. Interactive bench not yet available.',
    samples: bench?.samples ?? [], reagents: bench?.reagents ?? [], intro: '',
    parts: [{ id: 'response', label: `Q${source.question}`, text: 'Label your answers with the original part numbers. Record observations and conclusions separately.',
      marks: source.totalMarks, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 18 }, markScheme: ['Use the original mark scheme below and award marks for this question only.'] }],
    identities: [], supervisor: () => ({}),
  }
}

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "src/papers/as-q2.ts", "bytes": 6979, "sha256": "e483f6eb5f8da14ae06454d8891ce98fd1bb178bc0f1ce6447c2b4f56f2df989"} =====
// The two available AS Q2s in the four-year archive. Original components share the procedure.
import type { Lab } from '../engine'
import { RECENT_BENCHES } from './recent-benches'
import type { PaperCase } from './types'

const observations = (r: { observations: { text: string }[] }) => r.observations.map(o => o.text).join(' ')

export function nitrogenAndManganese(lab: Lab): Record<string, string> {
  const portion = (sample: string, grams = 0.05, kind: 'test-tube' | 'hard-glass-tube' = 'test-tube') => {
    const tube = lab.newVessel(kind)
    lab.transfer(sample, tube.id, { grams })
    return tube.id
  }
  const nitrogen: string[] = []
  for (const sample of ['FA 6', 'FA 7', 'FA 8']) {
    const tube = portion(sample)
    lab.addReagent(tube, 'water', { cm3: 2 })
    lab.addReagent(tube, 'NaOH-2M', { cm3: 1 })
    lab.heat(tube, 'warm', 30)
    const alkali = observations(lab.gasTest(tube, 'damp-red-litmus'))
    lab.addReagent(tube, 'Al-foil', { pieces: 1 })
    lab.heat(tube, 'warm', 30)
    const aluminium = observations(lab.gasTest(tube, 'damp-red-litmus'))
    const fresh = portion(sample)
    lab.addReagent(fresh, 'water', { cm3: 2 })
    const permanganate = observations(lab.addReagent(fresh, 'KMnO4-H+', { drops: 3 }))
    nitrogen.push(`${sample}: NaOH and heat: ${alkali} Then aluminium and heat: ${aluminium} Fresh sample with acidified KMnO₄: ${permanganate}`)
  }
  const heated = portion('FA 9', 0.1, 'hard-glass-tube')
  const heat = observations(lab.heat(heated, 'strong', 30))
  const oxygen = observations(lab.gasTest(heated, 'glowing-splint'))
  // Test the gas while it is being produced, then continue until the decomposition ends.
  const completed = observations(lab.heat(heated, 'strong', 240))
  lab.wait(180, heated)
  const water = observations(lab.addReagent(heated, 'water', { depth: 2.5 }))
  const oxidant = portion('FA 9', 0.01)
  lab.addReagent(oxidant, 'H2SO4', { depth: 2 })
  lab.addReagent(oxidant, 'water', { depth: 2 })
  const iron = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  lab.addReagent(iron.id, 'FeSO4-acidified-0.1', { depth: 1 })
  const test3 = observations(lab.transfer(oxidant, iron.id, { drops: 3 }))
  const iodide = lab.newVessel('test-tube')
  lab.addReagent(iodide.id, 'KI', { depth: 1 })
  const test4 = observations(lab.transfer(oxidant, iodide.id, { drops: 3 }))
  const manganese = portion('FA 10')
  lab.addReagent(manganese, 'water', { cm3: 2 })
  const initial = observations(lab.addReagent(manganese, 'NaOH-2M', { drops: 5 }))
  const manganeseSolid = () => lab.vessel(manganese).amount('Mn(OH)2(s)') + lab.vessel(manganese).amount('MnO(OH)2(s)')
  const beforeExcess = manganeseSolid()
  const excess = observations(lab.addReagent(manganese, 'NaOH-2M', { cm3: 1 }))
  const insoluble = beforeExcess > 0 && manganeseSolid() >= beforeExcess * 0.99 ? 'The precipitate remains insoluble in excess.' : ''
  const standing = observations(lab.wait(90, manganese))
  return { 'a-i': nitrogen.join('\n'), 'a-ii': 'FA 6: NO₃⁻; FA 7: NO₂⁻; FA 8: NH₄⁺.',
    'b-i': `Test 1: ${heat} ${oxygen} ${completed} Product: oxygen. Test 2: ${water}`,
    'b-ii': `Test 3: ${test3}\nTest 4: ${test4}`, 'b-iii': `${initial} In excess: ${excess} ${insoluble} On standing: ${standing}`, 'b-iv': 'Manganese (Mn).' }
}

function make(id: string): PaperCase {
  return {
    id, level: 'AS & A Level', syllabus: 'Cambridge International AS & A Level Chemistry 9701',
    component: `Paper ${id.slice(-2)} (Advanced Practical Skills)`, series: 'October/November 2023', question: 2, totalMarks: 17,
    summary: 'Three nitrogen-containing salts and two solids containing the same element.',
    ...RECENT_BENCHES[id],
    intro: 'For each test record all observations, including the stage at which a change occurs. Use a boiling tube if a solution is warmed. Rinse and reuse tubes where possible.',
    parts: [
      { id: 'a-i', label: '(a)(i)', marks: 8, text: 'FA 6, FA 7 and FA 8 are salts containing different nitrogen-containing ions listed in the Qualitative analysis notes. List the ions, select reagents to positively identify them, and record your tests and results in a suitable table.',
        answer: { kind: 'table', header: ['salt', 'ions tested, reagents and observations'], rows: ['FA 6', 'FA 7', 'FA 8'] },
        markScheme: ['List NO₃⁻, NO₂⁻ and NH₄⁺; provide a table with salts and reagents.', 'NaOH and heat: only FA 8 gives gas turning damp red litmus blue.', 'Then add Al and heat: FA 6 gives gas turning damp red litmus blue.', 'Fresh sample with acidified KMnO₄ or a named acid: only FA 7 decolourises KMnO₄ or produces brown gas with acid.'] },
      { id: 'a-ii', label: '(a)(ii)', marks: 1, text: 'Use your observations to determine the formulae of the nitrogen-containing ions in FA 6, FA 7 and FA 8.', answer: { kind: 'fields', fields: ['FA 6', 'FA 7', 'FA 8'] }, markScheme: ['FA 6: NO₃⁻; FA 7: NO₂⁻; FA 8: NH₄⁺.'] },
      { id: 'b-i', label: '(b)(i)', marks: 3, text: 'FA 9 and FA 10 contain the same element. Test 1: heat a small spatula measure of FA 9 in a hard-glass test-tube until reaction stops. Record observations and name one product. Keep the tube and leave to cool. Test 2: add a 2–3 cm depth of distilled water to the cooled product, shake and leave to settle. Record observations.', answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 6 }, markScheme: ['Two marking points per mark, maximum 3: black solid/residue; jumps/pops; glowing splint relights; a named product (O₂, MnO₂ or K₂MnO₄); green colour; solution formed.'] },
      { id: 'b-ii', label: '(b)(ii)', marks: 2, text: 'Dissolve a very small spatula measure of FA 9 in a 2 cm depth of dilute sulfuric acid and a 2 cm depth of distilled water. Test 3: add a few drops to a 1 cm depth of aqueous iron(II) sulfate. Test 4: add a few drops to a 1 cm depth of aqueous potassium iodide. Record observations.', answer: { kind: 'table', header: ['test', 'observations'], rows: ['Test 3', 'Test 4'] }, markScheme: ['Test 3: purple to colourless / pale yellow / yellow-brown.', 'Test 4: purple (to colourless) to brown solution.'] },
      { id: 'b-iii', label: '(b)(iii)', marks: 2, text: 'Dissolve a small spatula measure of FA 10 in distilled water. Add aqueous sodium hydroxide to a 1 cm depth of this solution and record observations.', answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 3 }, markScheme: ['Off-white precipitate.', 'Precipitate turns brown(er) on standing and is insoluble in excess NaOH.'] },
      { id: 'b-iv', label: '(b)(iv)', marks: 1, text: 'Identify the element present in FA 9 and FA 10.', answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 1 }, markScheme: ['Manganese / Mn.'] },
    ],
    identities: ['FA 6: sodium nitrate; FA 7: sodium nitrite; FA 8: ammonium chloride.', 'FA 9: potassium manganate(VII); FA 10: manganese(II) sulfate (the source also permits manganese(II) chloride).'],
    supervisor: nitrogenAndManganese,
  }
}

export const AS_Q2_PAPERS = ['9701_w23_31', '9701_w23_35'].map(make)

// Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620, Paper 5 (Practical Test), Question 2.
// Question wording follows the published question papers; unknowns follow the confidential
// instructions (materials lists).

import type { ActionResult, Lab } from '../engine'
import type { PaperCase } from './types'

export const t = (...rs: ActionResult[]) => rs.flatMap((r) => r.observations.map((o) => o.text)).join(' ')

const IG_BENCH_BASE = ['water', 'NaOH', 'HNO3', 'AgNO3', 'Ba(NO3)2']

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const P0620_s24_51: PaperCase = {
  id: '0620_s24_51',
  syllabus: 'Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620',
  level: 'IGCSE',
  series: 'May/June 2024',
  component: 'Paper 51 (Practical Test)',
  question: 2,
  totalMarks: 14,
  summary: 'One solid, E, containing three ions.',
  samples: [
    { id: 'E', label: 'solid E', kind: 'solid', solid: [{ compound: '(NH4)2SO4', grams: 0.667 }, { compound: 'KCr(SO4)2.12H2O', grams: 1.333 }] },
  ],
  reagents: [...IG_BENCH_BASE, 'NaHCO3', 'KMnO4-H+'],
  intro:
    'You are provided with one solid: solid E.\nDo the following tests on solid E, recording all of your observations at each stage.\n\nTests on solid E\nDivide solid E into two approximately equal portions in two boiling tubes.',
  parts: [
    { id: 'a', label: '(a)', text: 'Gently heat the first portion of solid E.\nRecord your observations.', marks: 2, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 3 },
      markScheme: ['Any two from: melts / dissolves / forms a liquid; condensation on tube; becomes green [2]'] },
    { id: 'b-i', label: '(b)(i)',
      preamble: 'To the remaining portion of solid E, add about 5 cm depth of distilled water. Place a stopper in the boiling tube and shake to dissolve solid E and form solution E. Divide solution E into four approximately equal portions in one boiling tube and three test-tubes.',
      text: 'To the first portion of solution E in the boiling tube, add aqueous sodium hydroxide dropwise and then in excess.\nKeep the product formed for use in (b)(ii).\nRecord your observations.', marks: 2, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 3 },
      markScheme: ['M1 green precipitate [1]', 'M2 dissolves / forms a solution [1]'] },
    { id: 'b-ii', label: '(b)(ii)', text: 'Transfer about 2 cm depth of the product from (b)(i) to another boiling tube. Gently warm the mixture and test any gas given off.\nRecord your observations.', marks: 1, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 2 },
      markScheme: ['(red) litmus turns blue [1]'] },
    { id: 'b-iii', label: '(b)(iii)', text: 'Identify the gas given off in (b)(ii).', marks: 1, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 1 },
      markScheme: ['ammonia / NH₃ [1]'] },
    { id: 'c', label: '(c)', text: 'To the second portion of solution E, add about 4 cm depth of aqueous sodium hydrogencarbonate.\nRecord your observations.', marks: 2, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 2 },
      markScheme: ['M1 green precipitate [1]', 'M2 fizzing / effervescence / bubbles [1]'] },
    { id: 'd-i', label: '(d)(i)', text: 'To the third portion of solution E, add a few drops of acidified aqueous potassium manganate(VII).\nRecord your observations.', marks: 1, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 2 },
      markScheme: ['(solution) becomes pink [1]'] },
    { id: 'd-ii', label: '(d)(ii)', text: 'State the conclusion that can be made from the result of the test in (d)(i).', marks: 1, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 2 },
      markScheme: ['not a sulfite / no SO₃²⁻ [1]'] },
    { id: 'e', label: '(e)', text: 'To the fourth portion of solution E, add about 1 cm depth of dilute nitric acid and a few drops of aqueous barium nitrate. Leave the mixture to stand for about two minutes.\nRecord your observations.', marks: 1, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 2 },
      markScheme: ['white precipitate [1]'] },
    { id: 'f', label: '(f)', text: 'Identify the three ions in solid E.', marks: 3, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 3 },
      markScheme: ['M1 chromium(III) / Cr³⁺ [1]', 'M2 ammonium / NH₄⁺ [1]', 'M3 sulfate / SO₄²⁻ [1]'] },
  ],
  identities: ['Solid E: homogeneous mixture of ammonium sulfate and chrome alum, KCr(SO₄)₂·12H₂O, 1 : 2 by mass (2 g).'],
  supervisor(lab: Lab) {
    const E = lab.sampleVessel('E')
    const h = lab.newVessel('boiling-tube', 'boiling tube (heat)')
    lab.transfer(E.id, h.id, { fraction: 0.5 })
    const a = t(lab.heat(h.id, 'gentle', 40))
    lab.addReagent(E.id, 'water', { depth: 5 })
    const [b, t1, t2, t3] = lab.divide(E.id, ['boiling-tube', 'test-tube', 'test-tube', 'test-tube']).vesselIds
    const bi: ActionResult[] = []
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) bi.push(lab.addReagent(b, 'NaOH', { drops: 2 }))
    bi.push(lab.addReagent(b, 'NaOH', { cm3: 6 }))
    const w = lab.newVessel('boiling-tube')
    lab.transfer(b, w.id, { depth: 2 })
    lab.heat(w.id, 'warm', 40)
    const bii = t(lab.gasTest(w.id, 'damp-red-litmus'))
    const c = t(lab.addReagent(t1, 'NaHCO3', { depth: 4 }))
    const d = t(lab.addReagent(t2, 'KMnO4-H+', { drops: 5 }))
    const e = t(lab.addReagent(t3, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 }), lab.addReagent(t3, 'Ba(NO3)2', { drops: 5 }), lab.wait(120, t3))
    return { a, 'b-i': t(...bi), 'b-ii': bii, c, 'd-i': d, e }
  },
}

export const P0620_w24_52: PaperCase = {

    { id: 'd', label: '(d)', text: 'To the third portion of solution M in a test-tube, add about 2 cm depth of aqueous hydrogen peroxide.\nRecord your observations.', marks: 2, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 2 },

    { id: 'f', label: '(f)', text: 'Identify the three ions in solid M.', marks: 3, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 3 },
      markScheme: ['M1 sodium / Na⁺ [1]', 'M2 iron(II) / Fe²⁺ [1]', 'M3 bromide / Br⁻ [1]'] },
    { id: 'g', label: '(g)', preamble: 'Tests on solid N',
      text: 'Transfer approximately half of solid N to a boiling tube. Add about 5 cm depth of dilute sulfuric acid to the boiling tube. Test any gas produced.\nRecord your observations.', marks: 2, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 2 },
      markScheme: ['M1 effervescence / fizzing / bubbles [1]', 'M2 limewater turns milky [1]'] },
    { id: 'h', label: '(h)', text: 'Add the remaining solid N to a boiling tube and add about 2 cm depth of distilled water. Do not place a stopper in the boiling tube.\nShake the boiling tube carefully to dissolve solid N and form solution N.\nTo solution N, add about 2 cm depth of aqueous sodium hydroxide. Gently warm the mixture and test any gas given off.\nRecord your observations.', marks: 1, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 2 },
      markScheme: ['(damp red) litmus (paper) turns blue [1]'] },
    { id: 'i', label: '(i)', text: 'Identify solid N.', marks: 2, answer: { kind: 'lines', lines: 1 },
      markScheme: ['ammonium carbonate / (NH₄)₂CO₃ [2]'] },
  ],
  identities: [
    'Solid M: 50 : 50 mixture by mass of iron(II) sulfate, FeSO₄·7H₂O, and sodium bromide, NaBr (3 g).',
    'Solid N: ammonium carbonate, (NH₄)₂CO₃ (2 g).',
  ],
  supervisor(lab: Lab) {
    const M = lab.sampleVessel('M')
    const a = t(lab.flameTest(M.id))
    const bt = lab.newVessel('boiling-tube', 'boiling tube M')
    lab.transfer(M.id, bt.id, { all: true })
    lab.addReagent(bt.id, 'water', { depth: 5 })
    const [b, t1, t2, t3] = lab.divide(bt.id, ['boiling-tube', 'test-tube', 'test-tube', 'test-tube']).vesselIds
    const br: ActionResult[] = []
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) br.push(lab.addReagent(b, 'NaOH', { drops: 2 }))
    br.push(lab.addReagent(b, 'NaOH', { cm3: 5 }))
    const c = t(lab.addReagent(t1, 'Mg-ribbon', { pieces: 1 }), lab.wait(30, t1))
    const d = t(lab.addReagent(t2, 'H2O2-20vol', { depth: 2 }), lab.wait(20, t2))
    const e = t(lab.addReagent(t3, 'HNO3', { depth: 1 }), lab.addReagent(t3, 'AgNO3', { drops: 5 }), lab.wait(120, t3))
    const N = lab.sampleVessel('N')
    const g1 = lab.newVessel('boiling-tube')
    lab.transfer(N.id, g1.id, { fraction: 0.5 })
    const g = t(lab.addReagent(g1.id, 'H2SO4', { depth: 5 }), lab.gasTest(g1.id, 'limewater'))
    const h1 = lab.newVessel('boiling-tube')
    lab.transfer(N.id, h1.id, { all: true })
    lab.addReagent(h1.id, 'water', { depth: 2 })
    lab.addReagent(h1.id, 'NaOH', { depth: 2 })
    lab.heat(h1.id, 'warm', 30)
    const h = t(lab.gasTest(h1.id, 'damp-red-litmus'))
    return { a, b: t(...br), c, d, e, g, h }
  },
}

import { AS_PAPERS } from './alevel'
import { IGCSE_PAPERS } from './igcse'
import type { PaperCase } from './types'
import { PAPER_SOURCES, sourceCase, sourceById } from './archive'
import { AS_Q2_PAPERS } from './as-q2'

export type { PaperCase, QuestionPart, AnswerSpec } from './types'
export const GUIDED_PAPERS: PaperCase[] = [...IGCSE_PAPERS, ...AS_PAPERS, ...AS_Q2_PAPERS].map(p => ({ ...p, coverage: 'guided', source: sourceById(p.id) }))
export const PAPERS: PaperCase[] = [...GUIDED_PAPERS, ...PAPER_SOURCES.filter(s => !GUIDED_PAPERS.some(p => p.id === s.id)).map(sourceCase)]
export function paperById(id: string) {
  return PAPERS.find((p) => p.id === id)
}

import type { Lab, SampleDef } from '../engine'

export interface SourceAsset {
  readonly pdf: string
  readonly sha256: string
  readonly pages: readonly { readonly path: string; readonly width: number; readonly height: number }[]
}

export interface PaperSource {
  readonly id: string
  readonly code: string
  readonly year: number
  readonly session: string
  readonly paper: string
  readonly question: number
  readonly totalMarks: number
  readonly source_urls: { readonly qp: string; readonly ms: string; readonly materials: readonly string[] }
  readonly qp_pages: readonly number[]
  readonly ms_pages: readonly number[]
  readonly source_note: string
  readonly archiveSha256: string
  readonly assets: { readonly question: SourceAsset; readonly markScheme: SourceAsset; readonly materials1?: SourceAsset }
}

export type AnswerSpec =
  | { kind: 'lines'; lines: number }
  | { kind: 'table'; header: [string, string]; rows: string[] }
  | { kind: 'fields'; fields: string[] }

export interface QuestionPart {
  /** stable id, e.g. "b-ii" */
  id: string
  /** printed label, e.g. "(b)(ii)" */
  label: string
  /** instructions printed before this part (procedure text that belongs to no single part) */
  preamble?: string
  text: string
  marks: number
  answer?: AnswerSpec
  /** published mark scheme points (for self-marking) */
  markScheme: string[]
}

export interface PaperCase {
  source?: PaperSource
  /** Source-only is written practice, not an implemented chemistry simulation. */
  coverage?: 'guided' | 'configured' | 'source-only'
  id: string
  syllabus: 'Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620' | 'Cambridge International AS & A Level Chemistry 9701'
  level: 'IGCSE' | 'AS & A Level'
  series: string
  component: string
  question: number
  totalMarks: number
  /** neutral one-line description (must not give the answer away) */
  summary: string
  samples: SampleDef[]
  /** reagent ids available on the bench */
  reagents: string[]
  intro: string
  parts: QuestionPart[]
  /** confidential instructions: what the unknowns really are (revealed after marking) */
  identities: string[]
  /** parts of the original question that are not simulated */
  omitted?: string
  /** perform the question's instructions; returns the observations for each part id */
  supervisor: (lab: Lab) => Record<string, string>
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import type { JournalEntry } from '../engine'
import { PAPERS, paperById } from '../papers'
import { Exam } from './Exam'
import type { Answers } from './PaperPane'
import { Results } from './Results'
import { remove } from './storage'
import { SvgDefs } from './Tube'

type Screen =
  | { name: 'home' }
  | { name: 'exam'; paperId: string; attempt: number }
  | { name: 'results'; paperId: string; answers: Answers; journal: JournalEntry[] }

export function App() {
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<Screen>({ name: 'home' })
  return (
    <>
      <SvgDefs />
      {screen.name === 'home' && <Home onStart={(id) => setScreen({ name: 'exam', paperId: id, attempt: Date.now() })} />}
      {screen.name === 'exam' && (
        <Exam
          key={screen.attempt}
          paper={paperById(screen.paperId)!}
          onExit={() => setScreen({ name: 'home' })}
          onFinish={(answers, lab) => {
            remove(`marks:${screen.paperId}`)
            setScreen({ name: 'results', paperId: screen.paperId, answers, journal: [...lab.journal] })
          }}
        />
      )}
      {screen.name === 'results' && (
        <Results
          paper={paperById(screen.paperId)!}
          answers={screen.answers}
          journal={screen.journal}
          onHome={() => setScreen({ name: 'home' })}
          onRetry={() => {
            remove(`answers:${screen.paperId}`)
            remove(`marks:${screen.paperId}`)
            remove(`notes:${screen.paperId}`)
            setScreen({ name: 'exam', paperId: screen.paperId, attempt: Date.now() })
          }}
        />
      )}
    </>
  )
}

function Home({ onStart }: { onStart: (id: string) => void }) {
  const [year, setYear] = useState('all')
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState('all')
  const [mode, setMode] = useState('all')
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const filtered = PAPERS.filter(p =>
    (year === 'all' || String(p.source?.year) === year) &&
    (question === 'all' || String(p.question) === question) &&
    (mode === 'all' || (mode === 'guided' ? p.coverage === 'guided' : p.coverage !== 'source-only')) &&
    `${p.id} ${p.series} ${p.component} ${p.level}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase()))
  const groups = [
    { title: 'Cambridge IGCSE Chemistry 0620 · Paper 5 Practical Test', list: filtered.filter((p) => p.level === 'IGCSE') },
    { title: 'Cambridge International AS & A Level Chemistry 9701 · Paper 3', list: filtered.filter((p) => p.level !== 'IGCSE') },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="home">
      <header className="home-hero">
        <h1>Qualitative Analysis Lab</h1>
        <p>
          Carry out the tests of a real past-paper qualitative analysis question on a simulated bench, record your own observations and conclusions,
          then mark yourself against the published mark scheme.
        </p>
      </header>
      <section className="library-intro" aria-label="Paper library">
        <p><strong>2023–2026 · {PAPERS.length} original question sets</strong> · 22 IGCSE and 45 AS papers, with original questions, mark schemes and materials.</p>
        <p className="hint">IGCSE qualitative analysis is Q2. AS question numbers vary: filter by the original question number. This archive does not contain every AS Q2, or October/November 2026 papers. “Source practice” includes written answers and marking; it has no interactive bench.</p>
        <div className="library-filters">
          <label>Year<select aria-label="Year" value={year} onChange={e => setYear(e.target.value)}><option value="all">All years</option>{[2026, 2025, 2024, 2023].map(y => <option key={y}>{y}</option>)}</select></label>
          <label>Question<select aria-label="Question" value={question} onChange={e => setQuestion(e.target.value)}><option value="all">All questions</option>{[1, 2, 3].map(q => <option key={q} value={q}>Question {q}</option>)}</select></label>
          <label>Practice<select aria-label="Practice" value={mode} onChange={e => setMode(e.target.value)}><option value="all">All papers</option><option value="bench">With a bench</option><option value="guided">Guided bench</option></select></label>
          <label>Find paper<input value={query} onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. 9701_s25_31" type="search" /></label>
        </div>
        <p role="status">{filtered.length} papers shown</p>
      </section>
      {groups.map((g) => (
        <section key={g.title} className="paper-group">
          <h2>{g.title}</h2>
          <div className="paper-cards">
            {g.list.map((p) => (
              <button key={p.id} className="paper-card" onClick={() => onStart(p.id)}>
                <div className="pc-top">
                  <span className="pc-series">{p.series}</span>
                  <span className="pc-marks">{p.totalMarks} marks</span>
                </div>
                <div className="pc-title">
                  {p.component.replace(/ \(.*\)/, '')} · Question {p.question}
                </div>
                <div className="pc-summary">{p.summary}</div>
                <span className={`coverage-badge coverage-${p.coverage}`}>{p.coverage === 'guided' ? 'Guided bench' : p.coverage === 'configured' ? 'Free bench · partial simulation' : 'Source practice'}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      ))}
      <footer className="home-foot">
        Questions and mark schemes © Cambridge University Press &amp; Assessment, reproduced for private study. Observations are produced by the
        simulation and may differ in detail from a real laboratory.
      </footer>
    </div>
  )
}
