===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "tools/dropbox_download.py", "bytes": 2432, "sha256": "5f778d8107605f611189b131c7bc08cfd36f4bd08bfab19984fc87f0f680eaf3"} =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Download files from Dropbox temporary links and verify their integrity.

Usage:
    python3 tools/dropbox_download.py <manifest.json> <output_dir>

manifest.json is a list of objects with keys:
    name, download_url, size, content_hash
(the fields returned by the Dropbox MCP `download_link` tool).

Each file is streamed to <output_dir>/<name>.part, then checked against the
expected size and Dropbox content_hash (SHA-256 over the concatenated SHA-256
digests of each 4 MiB block) before being renamed into place.
Note: Dropbox temporary links are single-use, so a failed download needs a
fresh link rather than a retry of the same URL.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
import urllib.request

BLOCK = 4 * 1024 * 1024


def dropbox_content_hash(path):
    overall = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        while True:
            block = f.read(BLOCK)
            if not block:
                break
            overall.update(hashlib.sha256(block).digest())
    return overall.hexdigest()


def download(entry, out_dir):
    name = os.path.basename(entry["name"])
    dest = os.path.join(out_dir, name)
    part = dest + ".part"
    with urllib.request.urlopen(entry["download_url"], timeout=60) as resp, open(part, "wb") as out:
        done = 0
        while True:
            chunk = resp.read(1024 * 1024)
            if not chunk:
                break
            out.write(chunk)
            done += len(chunk)
    size = os.path.getsize(part)
    if entry.get("size") is not None and size != int(entry["size"]):
        raise RuntimeError(f"{name}: size {size} != expected {entry['size']}")
    if entry.get("content_hash"):
        got = dropbox_content_hash(part)
        if got != entry["content_hash"]:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{name}: content_hash {got} != expected {entry['content_hash']}")
    os.replace(part, dest)
    print(f"OK  {name}  {size} bytes  (hash verified)")


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) != 3:
        sys.exit(__doc__)
    with open(sys.argv[1]) as f:
        entries = json.load(f)
    os.makedirs(sys.argv[2], exist_ok=True)
    failed = 0
    for entry in entries:
        try:
            download(entry, sys.argv[2])
        except Exception as exc:
            failed += 1
            print(f"FAIL {entry.get('name')}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== END FILE =====

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "tools/prepare_past_papers.py", "bytes": 5520, "sha256": "b7a70dba2eb91322eeab6b80db1eced5af90545b55a238fd48114f25edc14c7b"} =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build an offline, source-faithful 2023–2026 paper library from the verified LFS archive.

Does not infer unknown chemicals or claim a question has an interactive simulation.
Original page images preserve tables, formulae, and answer spaces without OCR rewriting.
"""
import concurrent.futures
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import re
import sys
import zipfile

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ARCHIVE = ROOT / 'dropbox_downloads/Chemistry_Qualitative_Practicals_2001-2026.zip'
EXPECTED = '4b2e3266cc666b68ee1aa092fb07ea7edd68b72c5d3b0a45f92e35251dab678c'
OUTPUT = ROOT / 'public/past-papers'


def digest(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def prepare(row):
    import fitz
    from PIL import Image
    # Each worker opens its own ZIP handle; no shared seek cursor.
    with zipfile.ZipFile(ARCHIVE) as archive:
        directory = OUTPUT / row['id']
        directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        assets = {}
        total = None
        for kind, source in [('question', row['qp_extract']), ('markScheme', row['ms_extract'])] + [
            (f'materials{i + 1}', path) for i, path in enumerate(row['ci_extracts'])
        ]:
            data = archive.read(source)
            if not data.startswith(b'%PDF-'):
                raise ValueError(f'{source}: not a PDF')
            pdf = fitz.open(stream=data, filetype='pdf')
            if not len(pdf):
                raise ValueError(f'{source}: no pages')
            filename = kind + '.pdf'
            (directory / filename).write_bytes(data)
            pages = []
            for i, page in enumerate(pdf):
                name = f'{kind}-{i + 1}.webp'
                image_path = directory / name
                if not image_path.exists():
                    pix = page.get_pixmap(matrix=fitz.Matrix(1440 / page.rect.width, 1440 / page.rect.width), alpha=False)
                    image = Image.frombytes('RGB', (pix.width, pix.height), pix.samples)
                    image.save(image_path, 'WEBP', quality=90, method=4)
                pages.append({'path': f'past-papers/{row["id"]}/{name}', 'width': 1440,
                              'height': round(1440 * page.rect.height / page.rect.width)})
            assets[kind] = {'pdf': f'past-papers/{row["id"]}/{filename}', 'sha256': digest(data), 'pages': pages}
            if kind == 'question':
                text = '\n'.join(page.get_text() for page in pdf)
                totals = re.findall(r'\[\s*Total\s*:\s*(\d+)\s*\]', text, re.I)
                if len(set(totals)) != 1:
                    raise ValueError(f'{row["id"]}: cannot verify total marks: {totals}')
                total = int(totals[0])
            pdf.close()
        return {**{k: row[k] for k in ['id', 'code', 'year', 'session', 'paper', 'question', 'source_urls', 'qp_pages', 'ms_pages', 'source_note']},
                'totalMarks': total, 'assets': assets, 'archiveSha256': EXPECTED}


def main():
    if '--check' in sys.argv:
        data_file = ROOT / 'src/papers/archive-data.ts'
        data = json.loads(data_file.read_text().split(' = ', 1)[1].removesuffix(' as const\n'))
        checked = 0
        for row in data:
            for asset in row['assets'].values():
                path = ROOT / 'public' / asset['pdf']
                if not path.exists() or digest(path.read_bytes()) != asset['sha256']:
                    raise SystemExit(f'Missing or changed original PDF: {path}. Run npm run prepare:papers.')
                for page in asset['pages']:
                    image = ROOT / 'public' / page['path']
                    if not image.exists() or image.stat().st_size < 100:
                        raise SystemExit(f'Missing source page: {image}. Run npm run prepare:papers.')
                checked += 1
        print(f'Verified {len(data)} source sets, {checked} original PDFs and all page images.')
        return
    with ARCHIVE.open('rb') as archive:
        if hashlib.file_digest(archive, 'sha256').hexdigest() != EXPECTED:
            raise SystemExit('Archive checksum mismatch or LFS pointer not downloaded. Run git lfs pull first.')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(ARCHIVE) as archive:
        rows = [row for row in json.loads(archive.read('Paper_Index.json'))
                if 2023 <= row['year'] <= 2026 and row['collection'] in ['IGCSE_0620_P5', 'AS_AL_9701_P3']]
        # Do not quietly accept a renamed collection or an incomplete import.
        all_recent = [row for row in json.loads(archive.read('Paper_Index.json')) if 2023 <= row['year'] <= 2026]
        if len(rows) != len(all_recent) or len(rows) != 67:
            raise SystemExit(f'Unexpected source inventory: {len(rows)} of {len(all_recent)} (expected 67)')
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as executor:
        results = list(executor.map(prepare, rows))
    results.sort(key=lambda r: (r['code'], -r['year'], {'w': 0, 's': 1, 'm': 2}[r['session']], r['paper']))
    output = '// Generated by tools/prepare_past_papers.py from the checksum-verified archive.\n'
    output += 'export const ARCHIVE_DATA = ' + json.dumps(results, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + ' as const\n'
    (ROOT / 'src/papers/archive-data.ts').write_text(output)
    print(f'Prepared {len(results)} original question sets with mark schemes and materials; '
          f'{sum(len(r["assets"]["question"]["pages"]) for r in results)} question pages. No synthetic papers added.')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "START-HERE-核心包说明.md", "bytes": 2741, "sha256": "7fddc1e77d5dd7f8a744c285e807b500690f767b82cde2f8d1082355f34b06d7"} =====
# 核心源码包（小于 10 MB）

快照提交：7237221264aec7bf613aed17fe275a238718de5d
仓库：https://github.com/ASH19900601/ChemistrySiumlator
分支：claude/trusting-franklin-x92q1i

## 保留内容

- src/：化学引擎、React 界面、9 组引导流程、自由实验台配置、67 组原题的来源索引。
- e2e/、src/**/__tests__/：浏览器和单元测试。
- docs/：开发、使用、覆盖边界、问题修复和下一轮优化分析。
- package.json、package-lock.json、构建配置、Git 属性与项目工作说明。
- tools/：从完整资料包生成原题页面的脚本和固定 Python 依赖版本。

## 为缩小体积未包含

- 约 125 MiB 的原始真题研究 ZIP，以及 dropbox_downloads/ 中的研究报告副本。
- public/past-papers/ 的原卷 PDF、页面图像和 dist/ 生产构建。
- Git 历史、node_modules、Python 虚拟环境、缓存及云环境配置。

因此，这是核心源码交付，不是带齐全部原卷资源的离线成品。67 组来源索引保留，但不能把索引当作已经附带对应原卷。

## 安装与核心验证

建议使用已验证的 Node.js 24.19.0 和 npm 11.9.0，在本目录执行：

    npm ci
    npm test
    npm run typecheck
    npm run dev

化学引擎和引导流程的文字题目、内置评分说明可以用于核心开发；原卷 PDF/图片不会显示，依赖原卷的书面练习无法完整使用。

## 恢复完整题库与生产构建

完整的浏览器检查和 npm run build 会检查或使用原卷文件；本包刻意未附这些大文件，不要把资源缺失误判为引擎单元测试失败，也不要关闭完整性检查。

方法一：从此前的完整项目压缩包复制 dropbox_downloads/ 和 public/past-papers/ 到本目录，再运行 npm run check:papers。

方法二：在联网并已认证 GitHub 的机器取得完整仓库，使用 Git LFS 拉取资料后生成：

    git clone --branch claude/trusting-franklin-x92q1i https://github.com/ASH19900601/ChemistrySiumlator.git full-repository
    cd full-repository
    git lfs install --local
    git lfs pull --include=dropbox_downloads/Chemistry_Qualitative_Practicals_2001-2026.zip
    python3 -m venv ../.chemistry-paper-tools
    . ../.chemistry-paper-tools/bin/activate
    python -m pip install -r tools/requirements.txt
    npm ci
    npm run prepare:papers
    npm run check:papers
    npm run build

恢复完成后也可将生成资料复制回核心源码包。运行浏览器检查需要安装 Chromium；可设置 CHROMIUM_PATH 或执行 npx playwright install chromium。

原题覆盖与未完成项目以 docs/paper-coverage-2023-2026.md 为准。包内 MANIFEST.json 记录内容文件的字节数和 SHA-256。
